Abstract: We aim to improve frozen flow-matching image generators by adding inference computation inside the denoiser, without changing model weights or the outer sampler. Existing generators usually spend extra test-time computation by increasing the number of sampling steps, which repeatedly evaluates the entire denoiser and couples quality gains to sampler cost. A key challenge is how to use extra computation inside a frozen transformer denoiser: the method must decide which tokens, layers, and sampling times receive repeated updates while preserving the original generation pipeline. We introduce a training-free looping framework that repeatedly applies selected transformer layers inside each denoising call. Dense and Sparse Token Loop vary the token scope; Sampling-Progress Gating and the loop layer range specify when and where looping is active; loop count and strength control the repeated updates; and Loop Guidance combines ordinary and looped vector-field predictions. Across two Scale-RAE model scales, loop variants improve primary and auxiliary quality metrics with competitive quality--efficiency trade-offs. Loop Guidance further improves both primary metrics across all three tested models; on Scale-RAE DiT2.4B, it raises GenEval from 0.4471 to 0.5691 and DPG-Bench from 0.7656 to 0.8053. Code will be released.

</details>


### [69] [Subtraction-Based Tumor Segmentation and Lesion-Centered pCR Prediction for the MAMA-MIA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29162)
*Kai Geissler,Raphael Schäfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FME团队在MAMA-MIA挑战赛中，使用五折残差编码器nnU-Net集成进行乳腺肿瘤分割，并集成25个预训练3D视频分类器进行病理完全缓解（pCR）预测，在两个任务中均排名第二。分割方法取得了0.882的绩效-公平性综合得分，pCR方法综合得分为0.664。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在外部多国家队列上，基于治疗前动态对比增强乳腺MRI进行原发性肿瘤分割和pCR预测的性能，特别是检验方法在跨中心域偏移下的鲁棒性和公平性。

Method: 分割任务：使用五折残差编码器的nnU-Net集成，仅输入首期增强后减去除对比前图像，结合镜像测试时增强和最大连通分量滤波。pCR预测任务：集成25个预训练3D视频分类器，输入来自增强前和两个增强后体素的病灶中心裁剪。

Result: 分割任务综合得分为0.882，Dice为0.713，归一化Hausdorff距离为0.099；pCR预测综合得分0.664，平衡准确率0.541，等化几率差异0.212；两任务均排名第二。

Conclusion: 减法输入与集成策略能够有效支持跨站点的鲁棒肿瘤分割，但仅基于治疗前DCE-MRI进行pCR预测的性能仍然有限。

Abstract: We describe the submission of team FME to the MAMA-MIA Challenge, which evaluated primary tumor segmentation and prediction of pathological complete response (pCR) from pretreatment dynamic contrast-enhanced breast MRI on an external multi-country cohort. For segmentation, we trained a five-fold residual-encoder nnU-Net ensemble using only the first post-contrast minus pre-contrast image, combined with mirroring test-time augmentation and largest-connected-component filtering. For pCR prediction, we ensembled 25 pretrained 3D video classifiers trained on lesion-centred crops from the pre-contrast and first two post-contrast volumes. FME ranked second in both tasks. The segmentation method achieved a combined performance-fairness score of 0.882, with Dice 0.713 and normalized Hausdorff distance 0.099. The pCR method achieved a combined score of 0.664, balanced accuracy of 0.541, and equalized-odds disparity of 0.212. The results indicate that subtraction-based input and ensembling support robust tumor segmentation under cross-site domain shift, whereas pCR prediction from baseline DCE-MRI alone remains limited.
  For the submission repository, see https://github.com/FraunhoferMEVIS/MAMA-MIA-Challenge-FME

</details>


### [70] [Mapping-Based Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29164)
*Freddie Åström,Michael Felsberg,George Baravdish*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的基于张量的泛函，用于目标图像增强和去噪。该公式通过显式正则化从第一性原理出发融合应用相关与上下文信息，证明极小值存在性，并在张量对称约束、凸性与几何解释方面给出结果，在非线性应用（如伽马校正和目标值域滤波）中表现优异，去噪性能与专用PDE方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中很少有变分模型能够同时描述应用相关信息与去噪问题的上下文知识。作者旨在弥合这一空白，提出一个能够将两种信息整合到统一框架中的变分泛函。

Method: 提出基于张量的变分泛函，通过显式正则化项将应用相关和上下文信息以第一性原理方式融入公式。作者证明了极小值存在性，并分析了张量对称约束、凸性以及泛函的几何解释。

Result: 该框架在含有非线性函数的应用（如伽马校正和针对性值域滤波）中表现优异；在一般去噪任务中，其结果与基于PDE的最先进专用方法相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于张量的变分框架能够有效统一应用相关分析（非线性处理）与通用去噪任务，兼具理论保证（存在性、凸性）与实用性能。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a novel tensor-based functional for targeted image enhancement and denoising. Via explicit regularization, our formulation incorporates application dependent and contextual information using first principles. Few works in literature treat variational models that describe both application dependent information and contextual knowledge of the denoising problem. We prove the existence of a minimizer and present results on tensor symmetry constraints, convexity, and geometric interpretation of the proposed functional. We show that our framework excels in applications where nonlinear functions are present such as in gamma correction and targeted value range filtering. We also study general denoising performance where we show comparable results to dedicated PDE-based state of the art methods.

</details>


### [71] [A Tensor Variational Formulation of Gradient Energy Total Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29172)
*Freddie Åström,George Baravdish,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量的全变分新变分方法——梯度能量全变分（GETV），将梯度能量张量引入GETV，证明了对应Euler-Lagrange方程是张量型全变分偏微分方程，并证明GETV是凸泛函。实验结果优于扩展各向异性扩散（EAD）和全变分（TV）等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的结构张量在推导张量型全变分公式对应的Euler-Lagrange方程时存在困难，无法进行形式化的数学推导。作者希望通过引入梯度能量张量来解决这一问题，从而获得一个可形式化推导的凸全变分泛函。

Method: 将梯度能量张量引入张量型全变分公式（GETV），推导对应的Euler-Lagrange方程，并采用凸性证明方法来验证GETV泛函的凸性。通过数值实验在灰度图像和彩色图像上评估方法性能。

Result: GETV被证明是凸泛函，且其Euler-Lagrange方程可以形式化推导。实验结果表明GETV在灰度图像和彩色图像去噪方面优于EAD和TV等现有最先进的变分去噪方法。

Conclusion: GETV作为一种基于梯度能量张量的凸全变分公式，相比使用结构张量的方法具有可形式化推导的优势，且在去噪效果上优于现有的主流变分方法，是一种有竞争力的图像去噪方案。

Abstract: We present a novel variational approach to a tensor-based total variation formulation which is called gradient energy total variation, GETV. We introduce the gradient energy tensor [6] into the GETV and show that the corresponding Euler-Lagrange (E-L) equation is a tensor-based partial differential equation of total variation type. Furthermore, we give a proof which shows that GETV is a convex functional. This approach, in contrast to the commonly used structure tensor, enables a formal derivation of the corresponding E-L equation. Experimental results suggest that GETV compares favourably to other state of the art variational denoising methods such as extended anisotropic diffusion (EAD)[1] and total variation (TV) [18] for gray-scale and colour images.

</details>


### [72] [Dynamic-Robust Photometric-Semantic Reconstruction for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29177)
*Boyu Cai,Li Yang,Yan Xu,Wei Liu,Nian Liu,Sikui Zhang,Yan Wang,Chunfeng Yuan,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPAR是一种联合语义-几何编码架构，通过显式隔离瞬态动态噪声，将新颖视图合成（NVS）与开放词汇分割（OVS）统一在端到端框架中，在动态场景下实现多视图一致的时空稳定表征。在D-RE10K基准上达到SOTA，3视图PSNR 22.15dB、4视图23.33dB，运动掩码预测mIoU达88.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NVS与OVS集成模型（feed-forward 3D基础模型）依赖静态场景假设，在非受约束的动态环境中会导致空间特征严重错位，亟需填补这一关键空白。

Method: 提出SPAR联合语义-几何编码架构，在潜在空间聚合之前显式隔离瞬态动态噪声；引入动态区域感知的端到端训练范式，将运动估计与多视角视觉及语义学习结构性耦合，使网络内在地解决运动冲突并从动态输入中蒸馏多视图一致、时间稳定的场景表征。

Result: 在D-RE10K基准上达到SOTA：3输入视图PSNR 22.15dB，4输入视图PSNR 23.33dB；自监督训练下运动掩码预测mIoU达88.5%；发现光度重建与语义理解之间存在强任务协同效应，语义合成学习能持续提升新颖视图渲染的光度保真度。

Conclusion: SPAR通过显式建模动态噪声并耦合运动估计与多视角语义学习，成功将NVS与OVS扩展到动态场景，展示了语义学习对光度重建的正向促进作用，证明了跨任务协同学习的价值。

Abstract: The integration of novel view synthesis (NVS) and open-vocabulary segmentation (OVS) has recently yielded powerful feed-forward 3D foundation models. However, their inherent reliance on static-scene assumptions leads to severe misalignment of spatial features in unconstrained dynamic environments. To bridge this critical gap, we propose SPAR, a novel joint semantic-geometric encoding architecture that explicitly isolates transient dynamic noise prior to latent space aggregation. Furthermore, we introduce a dynamic-region-aware end-to-end training paradigm that structurally couples motion estimation with multi-view visual and semantic learning. This unified approach enables the network to inherently resolve motion conflicts and distill multi-view consistent, temporally stable scene representations from dynamic inputs. Extensive experiments on the challenging D-RE10K benchmark demonstrate that SPAR achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our end-to-end approach achieves exceptional novel view synthesis quality, yielding a PSNR of 22.15 dB and 23.33 dB given only 3 and 4 input views respectively. Despite being trained in a self-supervised manner, our model achieves an mIoU of 88.5% for motion mask prediction. Furthermore, our analysis reveals a strong inter-task synergy between photometric scene reconstruction and semantic understanding, where semantic synthesis learning consistently enhances photometric fidelity in novel view rendering. Code will be available at https://github.com/dmucby/SPAR.

</details>


### [73] [Foundational feature fusion for conditional flow matching in 6D pose estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29183)
*Amir Hamza,Davide Boscaini,Fabio Poiesi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunFlow6D 提出了一种基于条件流匹配的物体6D位姿估计方法，利用几何与外观基础模型的特征取代任务专用的编码器训练，并通过交叉注意力融合机制动态整合两类特征，在BOP基准的四个数据集上超越现有最优方法，同时降低监督需求与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于条件流匹配的6D位姿估计方法需要训练任务专用的编码器进行物体-场景重叠监督，并依赖简单的特征融合策略来解决位姿歧义，这引入了额外的训练负担且限制了性能上限。作者旨在消除对专用编码器训练的依赖，并设计更有效的特征融合机制以提升位姿估计的精度与效率。

Method: FunFlow6D 采用流匹配公式进行渐进式去噪与配准；利用几何和外观基础模型的预训练特征作为条件输入，避免任务专用编码器训练；引入基于交叉注意力的融合机制，动态结合几何与外观特征，为流匹配模块提供更丰富的条件信息。

Result: 在BOP基准的四个数据集上，FunFlow6D 超越了此前的最先进方法，同时减少了监督需求和内存开销。大量消融实验验证了各提出组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过利用基础模型特征与交叉注意力融合机制，FunFlow6D 在6D位姿估计任务上实现了更高的精度与更优的训练效率，验证了无需任务专用编码器训练的流匹配公式在该任务上的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Conditional flow matching has enabled a step forward in object 6D pose estimation, achieving state-of-the-art performance by progressively denoising and registering object representations to observed scenes. Existing methods require training task-specific encoders supervised on object-scene overlap and rely on trivial feature fusion strategies to resolve pose ambiguities. We present FunFlow6D, a novel flow matching-based formulation that leverages features from geometric and appearance foundation models for pose estimation, eliminating the need for task-specific encoder training. We also introduce a cross attention-based fusion mechanism that dynamically combines geometric and appearance features to provide richer conditioning for the flow matching module. Experiments on four datasets from the BOP benchmark show that FunFlow6D outperforms the previous state of the art while reducing supervision requirements and memory overhead. Extensive ablations validate the contribution of each proposed component. Project website: https://tev-fbk.github.io/FunFlow6D/.

</details>


### [74] [Multi-Scale Temporal Domain Alignment for Federated Video Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29186)
*Lee En-Yi Hannah,Haozhi Cao,Yuecong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出METAL框架，通过多尺度时间域对齐解决联邦视频域适应（FVDA）中的时间信息对齐挑战，在Epic-Kitchens-55和Daily-DA上超越现有方法最高达28.47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦视频域适应（FVDA）支持在分布式和非IID视频数据集上的协作学习且保护隐私，但由于时间信息对齐困难而研究不足，亟需新的方法来利用时间信息改进跨域视频动作识别。

Method: METAL框架包含四个关键组件：(1)在源客户端训练每个时间尺度的Transformer编码器；(2)在每个时间尺度上独立进行知识投票生成鲁棒的伪标签；(3)引入新颖的L2方差惩罚项以在尺度级知识蒸馏中强制跨尺度一致性，防止单一主导尺度；(4)通过置信度加权的尺度级预测聚合进行知识蒸馏来训练晚期融合头，综合利用互补的时间信息。

Result: 在Epic-Kitchens-55和Daily-DA数据集上取得了最先进的性能，相对现有FDA方法提升高达28.47%。消融实验证明多尺度蒸馏和尺度协调对于有效的时间知识迁移至关重要。

Conclusion: METAL通过多尺度时间域对齐有效克服了FVDA中时间信息对齐的挑战，证明了多尺度蒸馏与尺度协调机制是跨域时间知识迁移的关键。

Abstract: Federated Video Domain Adaptation (FVDA) enables collaborative learning across distributed and non-IID video datasets while preserving privacy, but is under-explored due to challenges in aligning temporal information. We propose Multi-scalE Temporal domAin aLignment (METAL), a novel framework that leverages temporal information at multiple resolutions to improve cross-domain video action recognition with only model parameter transfers. METAL trains per-scale transformer encoders on source-clients, then performs independent knowledge voting at each temporal scale to generate robust pseudo-labels on the target-server. A novel $L_2$ variance penalty enforces cross-scale consistency during scale-based knowledge distillation, preventing a singular dominant scale. The late fusion aggregates features across different scales, where the fusion head is trained via knowledge distillation using confidence-weighted aggregation of scale-wise predictions, enabling the model to effectively exploit complementary temporal information for final predictions. Experiments on Epic-Kitchens-55 and Daily-DA demonstrate state-of-the-art performances, with gains up to 28.47% over current FDA methods. Ablation studies prove that multi-scale distillation and scale coordination are critical for effective temporal knowledge transfer.

</details>


### [75] [OPUS-V2: Bridging the Gap between Sparse Points and Dense Voxels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29187)
*Jiabao Wang,Qiang Meng,Liujiang Yan,Ke Wang,Qibin Hou,Ming-Ming Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OPUS-V2是基于点稀疏占位预测框架OPUS的改进方法，通过引入轻量级点-体素变换（PVT）模块，将稀疏预测自适应映射到稠密体素空间，消除了手工设计的启发式操作，并在特征与占位生成过程中解耦，支持任意占位分辨率。在Occ3D数据集上达到44.0 rayIoU的SOTA性能，在OpenOccupancy数据集上达到16.4 mIoU，并以20.6 FPS实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于点的占位预测范式通过在3D空间中稀疏建模，在精度与效率之间取得了吸引人的平衡。然而其预测本质上与自动驾驶系统所需的稠密体素占位不一致，导致训练和推理阶段必须依赖手工设计的启发式操作，限制了最终性能。

Method: OPUS-V2基于OPUS点稀疏方法构建，在解码器后引入轻量级点-体素变换（PVT）模块，将稀疏预测自适应映射到稠密体素空间，消除次优操作；同时将特征生成与占位生成过程解耦，使框架能够适应任意占位分辨率。

Result: OPUS-V2在Occ3D数据集上达到SOTA的44.0 rayIoU；在更具挑战性的OpenOccupancy数据集上达到具有竞争力的16.4 mIoU，同时以20.6 FPS实时运行。

Conclusion: OPUS-V2通过点-体素变换模块和特征/占位解耦设计，有效解决了基于点占位预测范式与稠密体素占位之间的固有失配问题，在精度和效率上均取得了领先表现。

Abstract: The point-based occupancy prediction paradigm has achieved an attractive trade-off between accuracy and efficiency by modeling 3D space sparsely. However, its predictions inherently mismatch the dense voxel-based occupancy required by self-driving systems, necessitating hand-crafted heuristics during training and inference that limit final performance. To overcome these limitations, we propose OPUS-V2, a novel framework built upon the pioneering OPUS (occupancy prediction using a sparse set) point-based approach. OPUS-V2 incorporates a lightweight point-voxel transformation (PVT) module behind the decoder to adaptively map sparse predictions into the dense voxel space, eliminating the need for suboptimal operations and improving model accuracy. Furthermore, our architecture decouples feature and occupancy generation processes, allowing OPUS-V2 to adapt to arbitrary occupancy resolutions. OPUS-V2 achieves a state-of-the-art rayIoU of 44.0 on the Occ3D dataset. On the more challenging OpenOccupancy dataset, it attains a competitive 16.4 mIoU while running in real time at 20.6 FPS.

</details>


### [76] [RLG-TPV: Radar- and LiDAR-Guided Tri-Perspective View Fusion for Camera-Radar 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29194)
*Ahmet Mete Dokgoz,A. Enes Doruk,Hasan F. Ates*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RLG-TPV，一种融合相机与雷达的多模态TPV 3D检测框架，利用雷达几何引导和训练时LiDAR监督来构建表示，在nuScenes上实现0.4981 mAP和0.5959 NDS，推理时仅需相机和雷达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TPV表示提升方法主要基于相机，导致沿投影相机光线的采样图像证据深度不明确。缺乏雷达等几何传感器在表示构建阶段的引导，限制了TPV方案在3D目标检测中的精度。

Method: 1) 射线引导的可变形注意力提升：利用LiDAR监督的相机深度概率和雷达视锥占据来加权采样图像特征；2) 雷达在提升前精细化深度分布；3) 训练时用LiDAR导出的类别占据目标监督侧平面和前平面（推理时移除对应头，部署仅需相机和雷达）；4) 多普勒引导的时间融合：利用雷达径向速度建立运动场对齐过去特征，并用门控限制无支撑运动区域的扭曲；5) RCS感知的雷达散点模块，根据雷达横截面条件将雷达证据在空间邻域传播。

Result: 在nuScenes验证集上达到0.4981 mAP和0.5959 NDS，相对已发表的CRN基线将方向误差和速度误差分别降低31.9%和30.7%。消融研究表明射线级几何引导是最终性能的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 通过雷达与训练时LiDAR的互补几何引导，RLG-TPV在不牺牲推理效率（仅需相机和雷达）的前提下显著提升了多模态TPV 3D检测的精度，射线级几何引导被证实为性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Tri-Perspective View (TPV) representations describe 3D scene structure through top, side, and front feature planes, but existing TPV lifting is primarily camera-based, leaving the depth of sampled image evidence ambiguous along projected camera rays. We propose RLG-TPV, a multimodal TPV framework for camera-radar 3D object detection in which radar and training-time LiDAR provide complementary geometric guidance during representation construction. A ray-guided deformable-attention lift weights sampled image features using LiDAR-supervised camera depth probabilities and radar frustum occupancy, while radar additionally refines the depth distribution before lifting. Because conventional radar provides limited elevation information, LiDAR-derived class-occupancy targets supervise the side and front planes during training; the corresponding heads are removed at inference, so deployment requires only cameras and radar. For temporal aggregation, Doppler-guided temporal fusion aligns past features using a motion field anchored by measured radar radial velocity, with gating that limits warping in regions without supported motion. An RCS-aware radar scatter further allows radar evidence to spread over spatial neighborhoods conditioned on radar cross section. On the nuScenes validation set, RLG-TPV achieves 0.4981 mAP and 0.5959 NDS, reducing orientation and velocity error by 31.9\% and 30.7\% relative to the published CRN baseline. Ablation studies show that ray-level geometric guidance is a major contributor to the final performance.

</details>


### [77] [Ground-to-Satellite Localization in Unconstrained Image Collections for 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29211)
*Angel Daruna,Ben Southall,Niluthpol Chowdhury Mithun,Kshitij Minhas,Nicholas Meegan,Qiao Wang,Bogdan Matei,Supun Samarasekera,Rakesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的层次化跨视角定位框架，利用从地面图像集合派生的Structure-from-Motion（SfM）模型的几何约束，实现相对于卫星影像的地面图像精确定位，并支持度量尺度估计、重复结构检测以及不连通SfM重建的合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨视角定位方法要求全景影像或已知初始位置等严格条件，难以应用于野外非受限图像集合的三维场景重建。作者希望通过利用SfM模型中的几何约束来突破这些限制，实现更通用的、度量精确的地理定位三维重建。

Method: 提出一个鲁棒的层次化跨视角定位框架：首先通过跨视角匹配方法生成从粗到细的位姿假设；然后利用核密度估计（Kernel Density Estimation）对来自一个或多个SfM模型的噪声预测进行聚合，恢复一致性对齐同时滤除外点，最终获得鲁棒的位姿估计。

Result: 实验表明该方法在具有挑战性的图像集合上实现了可靠的定位性能。实验结果还显示，基于卫星参考的对齐能够实现精确的度量尺度估计、重复结构（doppelgänger）检测以及不连通SfM重建的合并，从而生成比仅使用SfM更完整的地理定位站点模型。

Conclusion: 通过将SfM几何约束与卫星影像跨视角匹配相结合，本文方法显著提升了在野外地表场景下的定位鲁棒性和重建完整性，证明了从非受限地面图像集合进行度量精确的3D场景重建的可行性。

Abstract: Ground image localization with respect to satellite imagery is a key enabler for metrically-accurate, geo-localized 3D scene reconstruction from unconstrained image collections. Existing cross-view localization methods have strict requirements such as panoramic imagery or known initial locations, limiting their applicability for in-the-wild reconstruction settings. We propose a robust hierarchical cross-view localization framework that leverages geometric constraints from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) models derived from unconstrained ground image collections. Our method generates coarse-to-fine pose hypotheses through a cross-view matching approach and aggregates noisy predictions across SfM model(s) using Kernel Density Estimation to recover consensus alignments while filtering outliers. Experiments demonstrate reliable localization performance from challenging image collections. Empirically we found satellite-referenced alignment enables accurate metric scale estimation, doppelgänger detection, and merging of disjoint SfM reconstructions, resulting in more complete, geo-localized site models than are possible with SfM alone.

</details>


### [78] [SGPDFuse: Semantically-Guided Physics-Disentanglement General Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29220)
*Haozhen Wei,Chengjun Jiang,Yutong Guo,Xinrui Ju,Xingyuan Li,Xiang Chen,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SGPDFuse，一种基于物理解耦的语义引导多模态图像融合框架，利用预训练视觉基础模型构建语义-物理参数桥（SPPB），通过内在-变化原则解耦不变场景属性与瞬态环境因素，并引入语义对齐机制（余弦相似度锚定重要语义特征、Gram矩阵正则化保证物理纹理保真），在红外-可见光、多聚焦和多曝光三个基准上以单一架构实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多模态图像融合方法依赖盲目特征聚合，虽然擅长信号累积，但无法区分关键内容与物理退化（如环境干扰）。这种盲融合方式忽略了场景本质内容与环境因素之间的本质区别，导致融合结果中重要目标与伪影难以有效分离。

Method: 1) 提出SGPDFuse框架，将输入映射为物理解耦的结构化表示；2) 基于预训练视觉基础模型构建语义-物理参数桥（SPPB）；3) 利用内在-变化（Intrinsic-Variation）原则解耦不变场景属性与瞬态环境因素；4) 引入语义对齐机制：通过余弦相似度将融合表示锚定到基础模型特征空间中的显著语义特征以保留关键目标，同时通过Gram矩阵正则化强制物理纹理保真以消除伪影。

Result: 在红外-可见光、多聚焦和多曝光三个基准数据集上，SGPDFuse仅凭单一架构便取得了最先进（SOTA）的性能表现。

Conclusion: SGPDFuse通过物理感知的解耦策略和语义引导的对齐机制，有效弥合了盲目特征聚合与本质内容保留之间的鸿沟，证明单一统一架构可以跨多种多模态融合任务取得最优结果。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion (MMIF) aims to integrate complementary sensor data into a single representation that preserves intrinsic scene reality while eliminating environmental interferences. Most existing approaches rely on blind feature aggregation, which excels at signal accumulation but fails to distinguish essential content from physical degradations. We propose SGPDFuse, which bridges this gap by mapping inputs into a physics-disentangled structural representation via a Semantic-Physical Parametric Bridge (SPPB) built on pretrained vision foundation models, utilizing the Intrinsic-Variation principle to decouple invariant scene attributes from transient environmental factors. To guide this decomposition, we introduce a Semantic Alignment mechanism: we explicitly anchor the fused representation to salient semantic features in the same foundation model feature space via cosine similarity to preserve critical targets, while enforcing physical texture fidelity through Gram-matrix regularization to strictly eliminate unnatural artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGPDFuse achieves state-of-the-art performance across infrared-visible, multi-focus, and multi-exposure benchmarks using a single architecture.

</details>


### [79] [Using Channel Representations in Regularization Terms: A Case Study on Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29227)
*Christian Heinemann,Freddie Åström,George Baravdish,Kai Krajsek,Michael Felsberg,Hanno Scharr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于通道表示的图像非线性扩散滤波方法，通过软直方图表示像素邻域并构造新的能量泛函，导出含有通道表示加权项的非线性鲁棒扩散方案，在混合高斯和脉冲噪声（如数据缺失）的图像重建中表现良好，去噪性能与现有扩散方案及最先进方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散滤波方法在处理混合噪声（如高斯噪声与脉冲噪声并存）时性能受限，需要一种能够更鲁棒地感知图像局部结构并适应复杂噪声类型的扩散框架。

Method: 利用通道编码（channel encoding）构建图像像素邻域的软直方图表示，在此基础上构造新的能量泛函；通过求解Euler-Lagrange方程推导出非线性的鲁棒扩散更新方案，其中通道表示产生的额外加权项用于引导扩散过程。

Result: 在图像重建问题中，该方法对高斯噪声与脉冲噪声（如缺失数据）的混合情形表现良好；在常见标量图像的去噪实验中，性能与现有扩散方案及先进去噪方法相当。

Conclusion: 基于通道表示的软直方图能量泛函可以有效驱动非线性鲁棒扩散，为混合噪声下的图像重建与去噪提供了一种具有竞争力的新方案。

Abstract: In this work we propose a novel non-linear diffusion filtering approach for images based on their channel representation. To derive the diffusion update scheme we formulate a novel energy functional using a soft-histogram representation of image pixel neighborhoods obtained from the channel encoding. The resulting Euler-Lagrange equation yields a non-linear robust diffusion scheme with additional weighting terms stemming from the channel representation which steer the diffusion process. We apply this novel energy formulation to image reconstruction problems, showing good performance in the presence of mixtures of Gaussian and impulse-like noise, e.g. missing data. In denoising experiments of common scalar-valued images our approach performs competitive compared to other diffusion schemes as well as state-of-the-art denoising methods for the considered noise types.

</details>


### [80] [Compact Snapshot Spectral Imaging with Calibration-Free Aperture Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29230)
*Tao Lv,Quan Yuan,Shiqiao Li,Chenglong Huang,Linsen Chen,Chongde Zi,Shuming Wang,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑、低成本、免标定的快照光谱成像（SSI）方法——孔径衍射成像光谱仪（ADIS），仅由衍射透镜、二元掩模和Bayer滤光传感器组成，体积与标准RGB相机相当。ADIS利用理论计算的PSF实现免标定光谱重建，并引入正交衍射感知展开框架（ODAUF）与体素位移Transformer（VST）构成ODAUVST网络，实现全分辨率光谱恢复，在商业相机尺寸内验证了免标定SSI的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快照光谱成像（SSI）能够提供高维时空-光谱观测以揭示内在物理特性，但其复杂的系统结构和重复性标定需求限制了边缘应用。现有SSI系统硬件复杂、成本高、标定繁琐，亟需一种紧凑、经济且免标定的解决方案。

Method: 提出ADIS光学系统（仅含衍射透镜+二元掩模+Bayer滤光传感器，零额外物理体积）；利用理论计算PSF实现免标定重建，鲁棒于透镜差异；设计稀疏约束反问题求解框架ODAUF，并引入VST（体素位移Transformer）增强正交衍射感知，形成ODAUVST网络。

Result: 通过理论阐述、系统全面的对比实验以及真实SSI结果验证，ADIS在商业相机体积内实现了免标定的全分辨率快照光谱成像，恢复性能优异且参数量更少。

Conclusion: ADIS证明了在标准RGB相机物理体积内实现免标定全分辨率快照光谱成像的可行性，弥合了仿真与真实之间的差距，为光谱成像的边缘部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Snapshot Spectral Imaging (SSI) provides high-dimensional temporal-spatial-spectral observation to uncover intrinsic physical characteristics. However, its complex system and repetitive calibration requirements hinder edge applications. Here, we propose a compact, cost-effective, calibration-free SSI method, Aperture Diffraction Imaging Spectrometer (ADIS), which consists only of a diffractive lens with a binary mask and a Bayer-filtered sensor, requiring no additional physical footprint compared to standard RGB cameras. ADIS disperses and multiplexes wavelengths, mapping energy to distinct sensor locations, enabling full-resolution recovery from superpixel-level encodings. ADIS directly leverages theoretically computed PSFs to enable calibration-free spectral reconstruction, while tolerating lens-dependent variations across different optical configurations and bridging the gap between simulation and reality. To achieve SSI by solving a sparsely-constrained inverse problem, we introduce the Orthogonal Diffraction-Aware Unfolding Framework (ODAUF) with Voxel Shift Transformer (VST) for improved orthogonal diffraction perception. Integrating VST into ODAUF forms the efficient Orthogonal Diffraction-Aware Unfolding Voxel Shift Transformer (ODAUVST), delivering excellent recovery and reduced parameters. By elaborating on theory, systematic and comprehensive comparing, and demonstrating real SSI results, we validate the superiority of ADIS, achieving calibration-free full-resolution SSI within a commercial camera footprint.

</details>


### [81] [Background-Free Objectness Learning for Class-Agnostic Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29232)
*Dania Batool,Liliana Lo Presti,Marco La Cascia,Filippo Vella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: B-FOR提出了一种无需背景监督的密集类无关检测框架，通过预测多尺度目标中心和位移感知尺度场来学习目标性（objectness），在不完整标注下实现跨类别和跨数据集的通用目标定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检测器在闭环监督下将未标注区域隐式视为背景，导致目标性偏差——视觉上有效但未标注的目标被当作负样本，使目标性学习与标注类别绑定而非通用目标结构。这在类无关检测和开放世界检测中尤为严重。

Method: B-FOR将检测建模为密集多尺度目标中心和尺度场的预测，目标假设作为局部空间结构从预测场中涌现。监督仅限定在可靠标注区域，通过空间结构化的软目标避免前景-背景判别。同时引入位移感知尺度场，将目标范围建模为所学目标性场的空间变化属性，支持从涌现局部极大值中解码目标。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC、MS-COCO和Open Images上展示了出色的未见类别泛化和跨数据集目标分布适应能力。相较先前的类无关基线，B-FOR将召回率提升了超过10个AR点。消融研究表明，局部目标性监督和位移感知尺度场对不完整标注下的类无关定位均至关重要。

Conclusion: B-FOR通过重新定义目标性学习方式，在不完整标注下有效解耦了目标性与类别信息的绑定，为类无关和开放世界检测提供了一种新的范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Object detectors are typically trained under closed-set supervision, where unlabeled regions are implicitly treated as background. Under incomplete annotations, this assumption introduces objectness bias: visually valid but unlabeled objects are used as negatives, tying objectness to the annotated taxonomy rather than generic object structure. This limitation is particularly problematic for class-agnostic and open-world detection. This paper proposes Background-Free Objectness Learning (B-FOR), a dense class-agnostic detection framework that learns objectness without explicit background supervision on unlabeled regions. B-FOR formulates detection as the prediction of dense multi-scale object-center and scale fields, from which object hypotheses emerge as local spatial structures. Supervision is confined to reliable annotated regions through spatially structured soft targets, avoiding foreground-background discrimination. To support decoding from emergent local maxima, the paper further introduces displacement-aware scale fields that model object extent as a spatially varying property of the learned objectness field. Experiments on PASCAL VOC, MS-COCO, and Open Images demonstrate strong generalization to unseen categories and cross-dataset object distributions. B-FOR improves recall by more than +10 AR points over prior class-agnostic baselines. Ablation studies show that both localized objectness supervision and displacement-aware scale fields are critical for class-agnostic localization under incomplete annotations. Code available at: https://github.com/Daniaawan/B-FOR.

</details>


### [82] [Generalization over Memorization: Generalization-Aware Diffusion Adaptation for Single-Image Multi-View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29233)
*Jie Li,Xingchen Zou,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GoM框架，在ACM Multimedia 2026单图引导多角度图像合成大挑战中夺冠（293支队伍中排名第一）。通过识别共享训练/验证场景导致的模型选择失败，引入场景分离验证、曝光匹配选择和定向扩散适配，结合4B整流流DiT的rank-32 LoRA微调，证明在少数据生成建模中，验证设计和训练轨迹控制与架构规模同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有40个训练场景的极小数据条件下，需要从单张RGB图像生成26个目标视角，且禁止显式几何、外部渲染等辅助手段。作者发现了一个关键的模型选择失败：训练集和验证集共享场景会导致记忆化被误认为可迁移的视角控制能力，这促使他们设计更严谨的验证与选择策略。

Method: GoM框架包含三大部分：(1) 场景分离验证——确保验证场景与训练场景不重叠，避免记忆化干扰评估；(2) 曝光匹配选择——使选择过程中的曝光条件与最终任务一致；(3) 定向扩散适配——在4B整流流扩散Transformer上采用rank-32 LoRA、优化器重启、晚期检查点平均和VAE解码器调优。推理时每个视角一次前向传播。

Result: 系统在公开Phase-A排行榜上于293支注册队伍中排名第一，56支队伍至少获得一次评分提交。通过300余次离线实验和24次在线提交验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 验证设计（尤其是训练/验证场景分离）和训练轨迹控制在小数据生成建模中可能比架构规模更重要；模型选择协议的可信度直接决定生成的泛化性能。

Abstract: We present the winning solution to the ACM Multimedia 2026 Grand Challenge on Single-Image Guided Multi-Angle Image Synthesis. It ranks first among 293 registered teams; 56 teams obtained at least one scored submission on the public Phase-A leaderboard. With only 40 training scenes, the challenge requires 26 target views from one RGB model and one forward pass per view; it prohibits explicit geometry, external rendering, chained generation, candidate selection, and post-processing. We identify a critical model-selection failure: shared training and validation scenes make memorization appear as transferable view control. We therefore introduce GoM. Short for Generalization over Memorization, the framework combines scene-disjoint validation, exposure-matched selection, and targeted diffusion adaptation. Its synthesis model adapts a 4B rectified-flow DiT using rank-32 LoRA, optimizer restarts, late-checkpoint averaging, and VAE decoder tuning. More than 300 offline experiments and 24 online submissions show that validation design and training-trajectory control can matter as much as architecture scale in small-data generative modeling.

</details>


### [83] [Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal Anti-UAV Detection via Evidential Fusion and Conflict-Discounted Belief Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29235)
*Sharanda Suttorp,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansour Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文扩展EDTC至多模态RGB-Thermal反无人机感知，提出折扣信念融合（DBF）将模态间冲突转化为不确定性质量。虽然多模态融合在Anti-UAV基准上优于单流（测试精度0.670），但DBF与无折扣平均在经验上无法区分，因为基准中近乎普遍的存在性和空洞漏检编码抑制了模态间冲突，导致折扣机制失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反无人机多模态系统以确定性方式融合RGB和热红外流，无法建模预测不确定性，在传感器流退化（遮挡、快速运动、模态故障）时可靠性不足。虽然EDTC已利用证据深度学习进行仅热红外感知，但跨模态证据融合仍是空白。

Method: 将EDTC扩展至多模态RGB-Thermal感知，使用折扣信念融合（DBF），将模态间冲突转换为不确定性质量后再聚合流意见。边界框通过选择不确定性较低的模态来解析。

Result: 在Anti-UAV基准上，多模态融合一致优于任一单流（测试精度0.670 vs IR 0.604、RGB 0.598），实时速度至少38 FPS。融合不确定性校准良好（ECE 0.057），但作为定位失败检测器弱于空间方差（AUROC 0.626 vs 0.739）。DBF与无折扣平均经验上不可区分，因基准模态间冲突接近零。

Conclusion: 零结果是结构性的：基准中近乎普遍的存在性和空洞漏检编码共同抑制了模态间冲突。这一诊断界定了冲突感知融合在何处能带来可衡量的收益，为未来在更高冲突场景下的研究指明方向。

Abstract: Anti-UAV perception systems must remain reliable when sensor streams degrade under occlusion, fast motion, or modality-specific failure. Existing multimodal anti-UAV systems fuse RGB and thermal streams deterministically, without modeling predictive uncertainty, and cannot express doubt when streams disagree. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) produces calibrated per-class uncertainty in a single forward pass. EDTC already exploits this for thermal-only perception, yet cross-modal evidential fusion remains unaddressed.
  This paper extends EDTC to multimodal RGB-Thermal perception via Discounted Belief Fusion (DBF), which converts inter-modal conflict into uncertainty mass before aggregating stream opinions. Bounding boxes are resolved by selecting the lower-uncertainty modality. On the Anti-UAV benchmark, multimodal fusion consistently outperforms either single stream (test Acc 0.670 vs. 0.604 IR, 0.598 RGB) at real-time speed (at least 38 FPS). However, DBF is empirically indistinguishable from undiscounted averaging: near-zero inter-modal conflict on this presence-dominated benchmark leaves the discounting step inert. The fused uncertainty is well-calibrated (ECE 0.057) yet expectedly a weaker localization failure detector than spatial variance (AUROC 0.626 vs. 0.739). The null result is structural: the benchmark's near-universal presence and vacuous miss-encoding jointly suppress inter-modal conflict, a diagnosis that delimits where conflict-aware fusion provides measurable benefit.

</details>


### [84] [AGRICAM: A Track-Mounted Crop Pollination Monitoring Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29237)
*Malika Nisal Ratnayake,Adel N. Toosi,James Cook,Romina Rader,Alan Dorin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGRICAM，一个面向受保护种植系统中大规模授粉监测的自主移动机器人系统，能在低成本轨道上自主移动、采集昆虫影像与环境数据，并在商业蓝莓农场完成了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昆虫授粉对全球粮食生产至关重要，但商业农场尺度下的授粉者监测仍是难题。现有的计算机视觉和深度学习技术虽能详细分析授粉者行为，但监测方案需要在细节覆盖、空间范围和人力/技术资源之间进行权衡，缺乏能大规模、自动化运行的解决方案。

Method: AGRICAM是一种专用机器人系统，主要组成部分包括：(1) 沿低成本易安装轨道自主移动的平台；(2) 两个RGB相机、微气候传感器、GPS、RFID模块、运动传感器；(3) 4G蜂窝网络用于数据传输；(4) 网页界面支持远程配置与调度。系统自动采集昆虫位置与环境的视频图像数据，上传云端后用计算机视觉模型定量分析授粉者拜访频率及时空活动变化。

Result: 在商业蓝莓农场的80米长工业塑料隧道中部署并运行了30小时，成功绘制了昆虫授粉模式图。空间分析确认了隧道内授粉者均匀分布，符合农场管理预期；数据还揭示了昆虫活动随一天中的时间和微气候的变化规律。

Conclusion: AGRICAM已被证明是一种可扩展、自动化的作物授粉监测系统，能够支持数据驱动的决策以优化授粉管理，从而提升作物产量和粮食安全。

Abstract: Insect pollination is critical for global food production, yet monitoring pollinators at commercial farm scale remains a challenge. Recent advances in computer vision and deep learning have enabled detailed analysis of pollinator behaviour, but monitoring must trade-off detail against spatial coverage and human or technological resources. This paper presents the Automated Guided Robot for Insect and Crop Activity Monitoring (AGRICAM), a purpose-built robotic system designed to meet the requirements of large-scale pollination monitoring in protected cropping systems. AGRICAM operates autonomously on low-cost, easily installed track for movement along crop rows, without disrupting farm operations or insect behaviour. The platform integrates two RGB cameras, microclimate sensors, GPS and RFID modules, motion sensors, and 4G cellular network connectivity for data transmission. A web interface enables remote device configuration and scheduling. The system autonomously captures video and image data of insects' locations and local environmental conditions. These are transferred to the cloud and analysed using computer vision models to quantify pollinator visitation and spatio-temporal activity variation. We deployed the system on a commercial blueberry farm to demonstrate and test its capability. It successfully mapped insect pollination patterns across 80 m long industrial polytunnels over 30 hours. This data enabled spatial analyses of insect activity we used to confirm a uniform pollinator distribution within polytunnels, as desired by the farm management team. The data also highlighted variation of insect activity associated with time of day and microclimate. AGRICAM therefore has been shown to be a scalable, automated crop pollination monitor that can support data-driven decisions to enhance pollination management, thereby improving crop productivity and food security.

</details>


### [85] [DARD: Zero-Shot Degradation-Aware Retinex-Guided Diffusion for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29243)
*Wenjie Cai,Yuezhe Yang,Jianyang Xia,Xingbo Dong,Zhe Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DARD，一个零样本的退化感知Retinex引导扩散框架，用于低光图像增强。通过测试时退化感知Retinex分解提取图像特定物理先验，以时间步自适应频率融合策略注入反向扩散，并引入物理一致性与CLIP语义引导的逆向精炼过程，在多个真实低光基准上超越现有零样本方法，并显著提升下游语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的低光图像增强方法要么依赖成对监督，要么在零样本设置中缺乏可靠的场景约束，导致结构不一致和颜色漂移。传统Retinex模型虽能提供可解释的物理先验作为场景约束，但在真实场景混合退化下表现不佳，因此需要将Retinex物理先验与扩散模型的生成能力结合。

Method: DARD包含三个核心环节：(1) 通过测试时退化感知Retinex分解从退化输入中提取图像特定的物理先验，提供零样本恢复的结构引导；(2) 通过时间步自适应频率融合策略将这些先验注入反向扩散过程，平衡结构锚定与细节生成；(3) 引入基于物理一致性和CLIP语义引导的逆向精炼过程，抑制采样过程中的结构伪影和语义漂移。

Result: 大量实验表明DARD在失真和感知指标上均表现优异，在多个真实低光基准上持续超越现有零样本基线。在下游语义分割任务中，DARD增强的图像相比AGLLDiff在mIoU上实现了28.10%的相对提升。

Conclusion: DARD通过将Retinex物理先验注入扩散生成过程，成功解决了零样本低光增强中缺乏可靠场景约束的问题，兼顾了结构一致性、颜色保真和细节生成，并在下游任务中展现出显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Existing diffusion-based enhancement methods provide strong generative capability for low-light image enhancement (LLIE), yet they either rely on paired supervision or lack reliable scene constraints in zero-shot settings, often leading to structural inconsistency and color drift. Motivated by conventional Retinex models, which offer physically interpretable priors that can serve as reliable scene constraints yet struggle with mixed degradations in real-world scenarios, we propose DARD, a zero-shot Degradation-Aware Retinex-guided Diffusion framework for LLIE. DARD first extracts image-specific physical priors from the degraded input through a test-time degradation-aware Retinex decomposition, thereby providing reliable structural guidance for zero-shot restoration. It then injects these priors into reverse diffusion through a timestep-adaptive frequency fusion strategy to balance structural anchoring and detail generation. Finally, a guided reverse refinement process with physical consistency and Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP)-based semantic guidance is introduced to suppress structural artifacts and semantic drift during sampling. Extensive experiments show that DARD achieves strong distortion and perceptual performance and consistently outperforms existing zero-shot baselines across multiple real-world low-light benchmarks. To further validate the practical utility of our method for downstream applications, we evaluated its impact on semantic segmentation. Experiments demonstrate that images enhanced by DARD achieve a 28.10% relative improvement in mIoU over AGLLDiff.

</details>


### [86] [QCell: Recombining and Aligning Cell Queries for Overlapping Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29253)
*Yaroslav Prytula,Anton Popov,Dmytro Fishman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QCell是一种基于查询的新型模型，通过实例重组模块和对比查询对齐目标，在潜在空间中解重叠细胞实例，并在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微图像中重叠细胞的实例分割仍具挑战性，半透明结构产生弱边界和重叠区域的混合视觉证据。现有方法依赖局部感兴趣区域或形状先验，缺乏跨重叠对象的全局推理能力。

Method: 提出QCell模型，包含两个关键组件：(i)实例重组模块，在潜在空间中对查询表示进行分解与重组，使模型能够推理重叠下的完整对象结构；(ii)对比查询对齐目标，结合独特实例特征学习与重叠细胞查询的分离。同时引入新的Organoid数据集基准。

Result: QCell在多个基准上超越现有最先进方法，在ISBI2014上AP提升+2.2，AJI提升+2.7。

Conclusion: 基于查询的全局推理方法能有效解决显微镜图像中的重叠细胞分割问题，QCell通过潜在空间的实例重组与对比学习实现了显著的性能提升，并发布了新的数据集与开源代码。

Abstract: Instance segmentation of overlapping cells in microscopy remains challenging due to semi-transparent structures that produce weak boundaries and mixed visual evidence in overlap regions. Existing methods address this through local regions of interest or shape priors but lack global reasoning across overlapping objects. We present QCell, a novel query-based model that de-overlaps cell instances in microscopy scenes. Our approach combines (i) an instance recombination module that decomposes and recombines query representations in latent space, enabling the model to reason about complete object structure under overlap, and (ii) a contrastive query alignment objective that combines distinctive instance feature learning and separation of overlapping cell queries. We additionally introduce a new Organoid dataset benchmark for overlapping cell segmentation. We show that QCell outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple benchmarks, achieving +2.2 AP and +2.7 AJI on ISBI2014. Code is available at https://github.com/SlavkoPrytula/QCell

</details>


### [87] [Learning to Ground Before Reading: Unified PCB Engineering Drawing Parsing with Compact Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29268)
*Jinghao Liu,Xingrun Liu,Gengchen Sun,Han Xiao,Xingyu Chen,Yuhui Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于紧凑VLM的PCB工程图纸全页解析方法，无需检测器和裁剪识别器，通过坐标token监督和定位优先课程学习，显著提升定位F1，提供无检测器的基准确立了新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB工程图纸混合稀疏图形、密集表格和依赖页面位置的文本，现有解析器大多依赖区域定位后裁剪识别，一旦定位失败则无法在下游恢复，因此需要一个能够直接读取全页而不依赖检测器的解析方案。

Method: 训练紧凑VLM直接读取整页PDF，输出区域类别、归一化框和文本/HTML内容的序列。将边界框转换为坐标token进行监督。采用定位优先课程学习（Localization-First Curriculum），先学习类别-框格式再加入内容目标，并使用内容感知重采样。

Result: 在ED数据集验证集上，Localization-First相比联合训练在严格定位F1上提升0.0955（95%置信区间[0.0350, 0.1572]）。G-Unified模型获得最低NED、最高cell F1，且是唯一取得非零完全匹配分数的模型。

Conclusion: 定位优先课程学习有效解决了类别/框token稀疏与内容序列过长的优化困难，证明了无需检测器即可实现全页PCB工程图纸解析的可行性，为后续研究提供了检测器自由的基线。

Abstract: PCB engineering drawings mix sparse graphics, dense tables, and text whose meaning depends on page position. Localizing the regions and sending crops to specialized recognizers are determined as the methods for most parsers, so missed regions cannot be recovered downstream. We train a compact VLM to read the full page and get a sequence of region classes, normalized boxes, and text or HTML content. Bounding boxes are converted to coordinate tokens for supervision. Inference uses no detector or crop parser. The joint target is difficult to optimize because class and box tokens are sparse relative to the much longer content sequences. Our localization-first curriculum learns the class-box format before adding content targets with content-aware resampling. On the fixed validation split of the Engineering Drawing Dataset (ED dataset), Localization-First improves strict localization F1 by 0.0955 over joint training (paired image-bootstrap 95% interval: [0.0350, 0.1572]). G-Unified has the lowest NED, highest cell F1, and only nonzero exact-match score. It provides a detector-free baseline for full-page PCB drawing parsing.

</details>


### [88] [LightFuse: Relightable Interactive Gaussian Scene Reconstruction via Multi-Scan Fusion and 2D Gaussian Ray Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29269)
*Haonan Zhou,Gaoxiang Linghu,Youlin Jia,Hongyu Cui,Kewei Wei,Kaiyue Zhou,Bruce X. B. Yu,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightFuse是一个基于2D高斯的交互式场景重建框架，通过显式的材质-光照分解和基于物理的光线追踪，支持在物体重排后渲染出新布局下的逼真光照效果，并在合成场景上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么将光照烘焙进外观中，要么仅针对固定场景恢复材质和光照，无法处理物体重新排列后的阴影不一致和间接光照错误问题。

Method: LightFuse首先融合不同状态下的观测，重建共享背景和可移动物体；然后进行面向光线追踪的几何细化，产生更完整一致的表面；最后在细化几何上通过可微单次弹射光线追踪的分阶段训练，将共享的金属-粗糙度材质与状态特定的环境光照分离。场景支持交互后通过光线追踪重新计算外观。

Result: 在合成场景实验中，LightFuse实现了最先进的重光照质量，平均比最强基线PSNR高9.74dB，SSIM高0.121。

Conclusion: LightFuse成功将交互式场景重建扩展为支持显式材质-光照分解和物理重光照的可编辑3D模型，使得物体重排、材质编辑和重光照均得到支持，且交互后通过光线追踪重新计算渲染结果。

Abstract: Relightable interactive scene reconstruction aims to build an editable 3D model from scans of different object arrangements and render new layouts under novel illumination. Existing methods either bake lighting into appearance or recover material and illumination only for fixed scenes, leaving edited layouts with inconsistent shadows and indirect lighting. We present LightFuse, a 2D Gaussian framework that extends interactive scene reconstruction with explicit material-illumination decomposition and physically based relighting. LightFuse first fuses observations across states to reconstruct a shared background and movable objects. It then conducts ray-tracing-oriented geometry refinement to produce more complete and consistent surfaces. On the refined geometry, staged training with differentiable one-bounce ray tracing separates shared metallic--roughness material from state-specific environment lighting. The resulting scene supports object rearrangement, material editing, and relighting, while ray tracing recomputes appearance after each interaction. Experiments across synthetic scenes demonstrate state-of-the-art relighting quality, outperforming the strongest baseline by +9.74\,dB PSNR and +0.121 SSIM on average. Project page: https://zhn202.github.io/LightFuse/

</details>


### [89] [RAGDiffusion++: From Macro-Retrieval to Micro-Fidelity Alignment for Garment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29280)
*Yuhan Li,Xianfeng Tan,Fangao Zeng,Wenxiang Shang,Pipei Huang,Hao Zhou,Zhiyu Jin,Wenjun Zhang,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AR-GRPO框架，通过强化学习重塑流模型的采样分布，并结合对抗正则化（动态判别器）和高保真奖励模型（Garment-RM），解决服装生成中高频纹理细节丢失（High-Frequency Trajectory Collapse）与奖励模型漏洞利用（Artifact Hacking）两大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然之前的工作RAGDiffusion通过检索增强的宏观约束解决了结构性幻觉问题，但工业级微纹理真实感仍是未解决的瓶颈。作者将其形式化为"高频轨迹坍缩"：SFT收敛到训练分布的条件均值（被低频纹理主导），导致高频图案（织物编织、复杂logo）几乎不可采样；而直接应用RL后训练又会触发"伪影攻击"，模型利用通用奖励模型的语义偏差生成欺骗性的棋盘噪声。核心动机是证明RL能在准确奖励引导下从根本上重塑流模型的采样分布，同时对抗正则化可防止对奖励盲区的利用。

Method: 实现该原则需要三个前提：(i)内在容量：构建27,725对的STGarment-Plus高复杂度服装数据集，并升级为Dual-Image-Stream FLUX架构；(ii)感知奖励：设计属性感知奖励模型Garment-RM，在500K图像上通过细粒度对比学习训练，达到84.67%的人类偏好准确率；(iii)防攻击机制：提出Adversarial-Regularized GRPO（AR-GRPO）策略，在RL采样轨迹中集成动态判别器以惩罚伪影，同时丰富真实高频细节。

Result: Garment-RM在500K图像上通过细粒度对比学习训练，实现84.67%的人类偏好准确率；提出的三个核心组件（STGarment-Plus数据集、Dual-Image-Stream FLUX架构、AR-GRPO策略）共同实现了从宏观拓扑正确到微观物理保真的工业级服装资产生成。

Conclusion: 作者证明RL可以根本性地重塑流模型的采样分布，在准确的奖励引导下提升高保真轨迹的概率，而对抗正则化能防止对奖励盲区的利用。这一框架结合数据集、架构和训练策略三要素，系统性地解决了高频纹理坍缩和伪影攻击问题，推动了服装生成达到工业级水平。

Abstract: Standard clothing asset generation---restoring forward-facing flat-lay garment images from diverse real-world contexts---holds immense commercial value yet demands both macroscopic topological accuracy and microscopic physical fidelity. Although our previous work RAGDiffusion effectively eradicated large-scale structural hallucinations via retrieval-augmented macro-constraints, achieving industrial-grade micro-texture realism remains an unsolved bottleneck. We formally identify this limitation as High-Frequency Trajectory Collapse: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) converges to the conditional mean of the training distribution, which is dominated by smooth, low-frequency textures, causing high-frequency patterns (e.g., fabric weaves, intricate logos) to become nearly un-sampleable. Naively applying Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training further triggers Artifact Hacking, where models exploit semantic biases in generic reward models by generating deceptive checkerboard noise. Our key insight is that RL can fundamentally reshape the sampling distribution of flow models---elevating the probability of high-fidelity trajectories under accurate reward guidance---while adversarial regularization prevents exploitation of reward blind spots. Realizing this principle requires three prerequisites: (i)inherent capacity, established through a 27,725-pair high-complexity garment dataset (STGarment-Plus) and a Dual-Image-Stream FLUX architecture upgrade; (ii)perceptive reward, provided by a novel attribute-aware reward model (Garment-RM) trained on 500K images via fine-grained contrastive learning, achieving 84.67% human preference accuracy; and (iii)hacking prevention, enforced by our Adversarial-Regularized GRPO (AR-GRPO) strategy that integrates a dynamic discriminator into the RL sampling trajectory to penalize artifacts while enriching authentic high-frequency details.

</details>


### [90] [Elastic Token Compression for Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29281)
*Eduard Zamfir,Christian Reisswig,Zongwei Wu,Yongqin Xian,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Region Token Interface (RTI)，通过检测预训练像素级文本到图像扩散模型中特征变化最剧烈的区域，沿希尔伯特序将平坦区域的面片池化为区域token，从而在保持图像质量的同时显著减少计算量（2.0×加速保持质量，2.6×接近质量），并通过训练时随机采样区域数量使单个检查点适应任意计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像的细节集中在图像的一小部分区域，但扩散模型在每一层、每一个timestep都对所有面片分配完整token，造成大量计算浪费。现有方法（相似度合并、潜在瓶颈、跳过）分别存在组分散、丢失位置信息、删除而非摘要等问题，无法有效利用图像平坦区域的重叠连续性。研究发现中间块token在平坦区域是冗余的，且这种冗余形成连通的、内容形状的区域，需要同几何形状的token才能有效利用。

Method: 利用希尔伯特序对面片排序，使连续位置始终为图像邻居，任意连续运行（contiguous run）即为连通区域。方法在模型中特征变化最大的位置进行切割（cut），将每个运行池化为一个区域token。在微调阶段随机采样区域数量，使单一检查点可适配任意推理预算。该方法将二维分组问题转化为一维切割问题。

Result: 在匹配预算下优于此前所有reduction方法；在2.0×速度下与密集token质量相当，在2.6×速度下仍保持接近的质量。代码和模型已开源。

Conclusion: 区域token（按内容形状组织的token）是高效利用像素空间扩散模型冗余的关键，希尔伯特序+单维切割为这类几何约束的分组提供了优雅且有效的方案，且随机预算训练使一个模型即可服务多种部署场景。

Abstract: Natural images concentrate their detail in a small fraction of the frame, yet diffusion models spend a full token on every patch, in every layer and at every timestep. The waste is largest in pixel-space models, with no autoencoder to absorb low-level redundancy first. Probing a pretrained pixel text-to-image transformer, we find its middle-block tokens redundant wherever the image is flat. The redundancy occupies connected, content-shaped regions, and exploiting it requires tokens with the same geometry. Cutting a Hilbert ordering of the patches provides them. Consecutive positions are always image neighbours, so any contiguous run is a connected region whose size and shape follow the content, and grouping in two dimensions becomes a cut in one. Existing reductions each lose part of this. Similarity merging scatters its groups, latent bottlenecks discard position, and skipping deletes what it should summarize. We cut where the model's features change most and pool each run into one region token. Our Region Token Interface (\method{}) adapts a diffusion model to these tokens, with the region count drawn at random during fine-tuning so one checkpoint serves every budget. \method{} leads prior reduction methods at matched budgets, matches dense quality at $2.0\times$ the speed, and stays close at $2.6\times$. The code and models are open-sourced at https://eduardzamfir.github.io/rti

</details>


### [91] [3D-MRL: Nested Multimodal 3D Representations via Matryoshka Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29285)
*Márcus Lobo,Vitor Matias,Jeová Farias,Moacir Ponti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D-MRL框架，基于嵌套表示学习让单个3D点云模型在不同嵌入维度下生成多粒度表示，无需重训练即可适应不同计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态3D预训练方法生成固定维度嵌入，不同计算预算需要训练不同尺寸的模型，缺乏灵活性和效率。

Method: 将Matryoshka表示学习应用于3D编码器，通过与冻结的CLIP图像和文本嵌入对齐点云，并在多个嵌入维度上施加对比监督，学习嵌套的3D表示。

Result: 在Objaverse-LVIS上Top-1准确率从46.8%提升至50.9%；在零样本、少样本3D识别及检索任务上均达到竞争性性能；不同嵌入维度可提供不同语义/几何粒度。

Conclusion: 3D-MRL证明了单一模型通过嵌套表示即可支持多维度嵌入输出，兼顾性能与计算效率，且不同维度嵌入具有不同的语义特性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models align point clouds with image and text embeddings, enabling zero-shot recognition, retrieval, and open-vocabulary understanding of 3D shapes. Existing multimodal 3D pre-training methods produce fixed-dimensional embeddings, requiring separate models for different computational budgets. We propose 3D Matryoshka Representation Learning (3D-MRL), a multimodal 3D pre-training framework based on Matryoshka Representation Learning. 3D-MRL learns nested 3D representations by aligning point clouds with frozen CLIP image and text embeddings while applying contrastive supervision across multiple embedding dimensions. The Matryoshka objective is applied only to the 3D encoder, allowing a single model to produce representations at different dimensionalities without retraining. Experiments on the Objaverse-LVIS, ModelNet40, and ScanNet datasets show that 3D-MRL achieves competitive performance on zero-shot and few-shot 3D recognition tasks. In addition, the learned representations support retrieval across different embedding dimensions within a single model. On Objaverse-LVIS, 3D-MRL improves Top-1 accuracy from 46.8% to 50.9%. Retrieval experiments further show that different embedding dimensions yield varying levels of semantic and geometric specificity.

</details>


### [92] [PERSIST: Persistent-State Discrimination for Shot Boundary Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29287)
*Tingyu Lin,Christian Stippel,Armin Dadras,Jakob Zenzmaier,Florian Kleber,Wolfgang Aigner,Robert Sablatnig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PERSIST将镜头边界检测（SBD）重构为"边界语义判别"问题，通过持续更新的潜在时间状态来区分真实镜头切换与瞬态干扰（如手抖、光照闪烁、运动模糊等），在2,727视频诊断集上消除33-80%的伪阳性，并在保持召回率的同时大幅降低TransNetV2的假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SBD将问题视为局部视觉不连续点的定位，但许多非镜头切换因素（手持抖动、光照闪烁、运动模糊、遮挡、档案材料损坏）同样产生尖锐的局部变化，导致大量假阳性。需要一种能区分真正的语义边界与瞬态噪声的方法。

Method: PERSIST框架包含三个核心组件：1）FiLM条件化的正弦表示网络维持连续潜在状态，用于检测视频潜在时间状态的持续更新；2）结构化判别器结合三种语义线索（局部变化、瞬态脉冲、回归趋势）；3）双速率时间骨干网络，将所有线索整合为单一可解释的逐帧信号。持久性准则被训练进分类器，门控三元组保持逐帧可读，潜在状态可测量地具备边界判别性。

Result: 在2,727视频的逐子类型诊断中，PERSIST相比同等训练的线索检测器消除了33-80%的闪光、文字覆盖和档案伪阳性；在匹配的真实转换召回率下，相比TransNetV2将其伪事件假阳性减少约一半（诊断集）和约四分之一（ClipShots素材）；在在线、广播、短视频和历史档案迁移评估中与最强的公开检测器达到同等水平，但训练更严格——仅使用ClipShots真实转换，而对比方法使用了85%合成转换的额外语料。

Conclusion: PERSIST证明将SBD重构为持久性语义判别而非单纯的局部不连续检测，能够在更严格的训练条件下达到甚至超越现有最强检测器的性能，同时显著减少假阳性，且每个决策都具有可检查性。

Abstract: Shot boundary detection (SBD) is widely treated as the localisation of local visual discontinuities, yet many false positives such as hand-held shake, illumination flicker, motion blur, occlusion, and damaged archival material produce equally sharp local change without introducing a new shot. We reformulate SBD as boundary semantic discrimination: a frame is favoured as a boundary only when its local change evidence is accompanied by a persistent update of the video's latent temporal state, rather than a transient excursion that returns to the surrounding trend. This persistence test is operationalised with a continuous latent state from a FiLM-conditioned sinusoidal representation network and a structured discriminator that combines three semantic cues, local change, transient impulse, and return-to-trend, into a single interpretable per-frame signal over a dual-rate temporal backbone. The resulting framework, PERSIST, turns every decision into an inspectable one: the persistence criterion is trained into the classifier, its per-frame effect stays readable from the gate triple, and its learned latent state is measurably boundary-discriminative. On a 2,727-video per-subtype diagnostic it removes 33-80% of flash, text-overlay, and archival false positives relative to an identically trained cue detector, and at matched true-transition recall it roughly halves TransNetV2's pseudo-event false positives on that diagnostic and cuts its false positives on ClipShots footage by about a quarter, while preserving recall. It does so while reaching parity with the strongest public detector across online, broadcast, short-form, and historical-archive transfer evaluations, under markedly stricter training: it learns from ClipShots real transitions only, whereas the anchor draws on additional corpora whose transitions are 85% synthetic. Code is available at https://github.com/linty5/PERSIST.

</details>


### [93] [AOI-Net: Structural Face AOI-Guided Eye-Gaze Track Representation Learning for Autism Spectrum Disorder Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29289)
*Zhanpei Huang,Binbin Sun,Jialiang Chen,Yiou Wang,Taochen Chen,Yuzhu Ji,Yiqun Zhang,Yiu-Ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AOI-Net框架，将短时时间动态与面部兴趣区（AOI）级结构组织联合建模，用于自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）的注视行为筛查，在大规模临床数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于眼动追踪的ASD筛查方法主要利用离散注视轨迹和注视事件，产生了以短时时间动态为主导的表征，无法充分利用长时间依赖关系；同时注视行为在语义上有意义的AOI上自然组织，但其注意力分配与转移关系很少被显式建模。

Method: 提出结构面部AOI引导的眼注视轨迹网络（AOI-Net），通过网络门控机制自适应地集成互补的时间表征与AOI级结构表征；并引入类分布感知学习以缓解ASD与典型发育个体之间的类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在包含8个刺激子集、超过1300名参与者的大规模临床眼动数据库上，AOI-Net一致性地优于现有最先进方法，且提供了可解释的注视行为建模。

Conclusion: AOI-Net为可扩展的AI驱动ASD筛查提供了实用基础，其结构化的AOI指导建模增强了对目光行为理解的解释性，适用于真实医疗场景。

Abstract: Eye-movement tracking has emerged as a promising non-invasive approach to Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) screening, with systematic differences in attentional allocation and revisit behaviors observed during socially interactive tasks. Existing computational methods typically characterize eye-movements using discrete gaze trajectories and fixation events, yielding representations dominated by short-range temporal dynamics and limiting models that primarily emphasize long-range dependencies. Meanwhile, gaze behavior is naturally organized across semantically meaningful Areas of Interest (AOIs), whose attention allocation and transitions provide important structural cues, yet their relationships are rarely modeled explicitly. To address these limitations, we propose a structural face AOI-guided Eye-Gaze Track Network (AOI-Net) that jointly models short-term temporal dynamics and AOI-level structural organization. A network gating mechanism adaptively integrates the complementary temporal and structural representations according to their contributions to gaze-behavior characterization. To mitigate the pronounced class imbalance commonly encountered between individuals with ASD and Typically Developing (TD) participants in clinical datasets, class-distribution-aware learning is further employed to facilitate discriminative embedding learning under skewed class distributions. Experiments on a unique and large-scale clinical eye-tracking database comprising eight stimulus subsets and more than 1,300 participants show that AOI-Net consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The proposed framework also enables interpretable gaze-behavior modeling and provides a practical basis for scalable AI-driven ASD screening in real-world healthcare. The code is available at https://github.com/Zhanpei-ai/CIM-AOI-Net/tree/main/Code

</details>


### [94] [Hyper3-CLIP: Hierarchy-Conditioned Hyperbolic Vision-Language Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29313)
*Matin Mahmood,Antonio Rueda-Toicen,Mohamed ElBassat,Seifeldin Elkerdany,Weixing Wang,Gerard de Melo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hyper3-CLIP 是一种层次条件化的双曲视觉-语言模型，将全局/局部/全局-局部对比学习与查询条件化视觉池化相结合，利用从文本构建的轻量级查询层次（完整标题、句子片段、局部部件描述、提取短语）来训练模型，实现图像-文本、整体-部件和父-子蕴含损失，在多项检索和多标签分类任务上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的两个研究方向（双曲VLM与文本条件化VLM）相互独立：双曲型VLM虽然能编码部分-整体和父子等关系结构，但使用静态图像和区域特征；查询条件化方法实现了细粒度对齐，却缺乏层次化的几何结构。两者未能结合，导致在编码关系结构的同时实现细粒度对齐的需求未被满足。

Method: 提出Hyper3-CLIP，包含三个核心组成部分：(1) 全局、局部及全局-局部对比学习；(2) 查询条件化的视觉池化机制，查询来自从文本构建的轻量级层次结构（完整标题、句子片段、局部部件描述和提取短语）；(3) 支持图像-文本、整体-部件、父-子三种蕴含损失的训练范式。每个查询对视觉patch进行条件化池化，且该池化仅在训练阶段启用，推理时无额外开销。

Result: 在COCO和Flickr数据集上提升了R@5和R@10检索性能，在VOC和COCO多标签分类上取得改进，同时在层次指标上保持竞争力。此外对零样本提示敏感性进行了审计，并研究了训练中局部GRIT部件预算的影响。

Conclusion: Hyper3-CLIP成功弥合了双曲VLM与查询条件化方法之间的鸿沟，通过层次查询条件化池化在不牺牲层次度量能力的前提下，显著提升了细粒度检索和分类性能，且推理阶段无需额外计算成本。

Abstract: CLIP-like vision-language models (VLMs) trained with contrastive objectives learn strong global image-text representations, but their Euclidean embeddings and global pooling fail to encode relational structure such as part-whole and parent-child relations. Hyperbolic VLMs address this gap with entailment-based objectives, and text-conditioned variants improve fine-grained alignment through sentence- and phrase-level queries. However, these two lines of work remain separate: hyperbolic VLMs use static image and region features, while query-conditioned methods lack hierarchical geometric structure. We present Hyper3-CLIP, a hierarchy-conditioned hyperbolic VLM that combines global, local, and global-local contrastive learning with query-conditioned visual pooling. To train the model, we construct lightweight query hierarchies from text, comprising full captions, sentence fragments, localized part descriptions, and extracted phrases. Each query conditions the pooling of visual patches, and the resulting representations support image-text, whole-part, and parent-child entailment losses. Query-conditioned pooling is active only during training. Hyper3-CLIP improves R@5 and R@10 retrieval on COCO and Flickr, as well as multi-label classification on VOC and COCO, while remaining competitive on hierarchy metrics. We also audit zero-shot prompt sensitivity under fixed prompt regimes and study the effect of the localized GRIT part budget used during training. Code is available at https://github.com/Hyper3Labs/hyper3-clip.

</details>


### [95] [Test-Time Scaling for Video Diffusion Models via Diagnosis-Guided Candidate Recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29322)
*Hangzhou He,Lunhao Duan,Shanshan Zhao,Kaiwen Li,Qing-Guo Chen,Weihua Luo,Yanye Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEARS是一个免训练的测试时扩展（TTS）框架，通过诊断引导的候选回收机制，将视频扩散模型中低分候选样本转化为可编辑先验，在生成-评估-编辑循环中实现自适应修复和回收，在匹配的NFE预算下显著超越现有视频TTS方法，使1.3B模型获得与14B模型相当的总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型虽生成质量高，但依赖闭源系统或昂贵的大规模基础设施。轻量级模型可以通过测试时扩展（TTS）在推理阶段增加计算来提升质量，但现有TTS方法局限于噪声搜索范式——生成后丢弃低分候选，既浪费计算，又丢弃了样本中已编码的部分运动、布局或外观结构。

Method: GEARS提出诊断引导的候选回收机制，包含两个协作组件：(1) Stage-Aware Scheduler——决定修复什么、何时修复、哪些候选应保留/回收/丢弃；(2) Candidate Recycler——从关键帧和多维奖励反馈中诊断可恢复的失败模式，推导候选特定的修复提示，并通过流形感知的潜在SDEdit修复候选。修复后的候选被回收进搜索池，形成超越标准噪声扰动的细化路径，同时保留有用结构。

Result: 在匹配NFE预算下，GEARS在VBench上一致优于现有视频TTS方法，将1.3B模型提升到与14B模型相当的总分。消融实验验证了自适应调度、诊断条件编辑和流形感知重去噪的必要性。

Conclusion: 通过将生成-丢弃的浪费范式转化为生成-评估-编辑的循环，GEARS实现了对轻量级视频生成器的训练无关增强，在有限推理预算下显著提升生成质量，缩小了轻量级与大规模模型之间的质量差距。

Abstract: Recent video diffusion models have achieved remarkable generation quality, but high-fidelity results still largely depend on closed-source systems or costly large-scale infrastructure. Test-time scaling (TTS) offers a training-free way to improve lightweight generators by spending additional inference compute, yet existing methods mostly remain within a noise-search paradigm: they sample, select, or perturb denoising trajectories and discard low-scoring candidates after expensive generation. This generate-and-discard process wastes not only computation but also the partial motion, layout, or appearance structure already encoded in recoverable samples. We present \textbf{GEARS} (\textbf{G}uided \textbf{E}diting for \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{R}ecycling \textbf{S}earch), a training-free framework that introduces {diagnosis-guided candidate recycling} into video TTS by turning such candidates into editable priors through a generation-evaluation-editing loop. GEARS consists of two collaborative components. The \textbf{Stage-Aware Scheduler} determines what to repair, when to repair it, and which candidates should be preserved, recycled, or discarded. The \textbf{Candidate Recycler} diagnoses recoverable failures from keyframes and multi-dimensional reward feedback, derives candidate-specific repair prompts, and repairs the corresponding candidates through manifold-aware latent SDEdit. The repaired candidates are recycled into the search pool, creating refinement paths beyond standard noise perturbation while preserving useful structure. Under matched NFE budgets, GEARS consistently outperforms existing video TTS methods on VBench, bringing a 1.3B model to a total score comparable to a 14B counterpart, and ablations verify the necessity of adaptive scheduling, diagnosis-conditioned editing, and manifold-aware re-denoising. Code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [96] [Neural video codecs quality assessment dataset and benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29331)
*Nikolay Safonov,Nikita Gornostaev,Alexandra Dubonos,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个大规模主观视频质量数据集，包含神经视频编解码器（NVC）和传统视频编解码器压缩的视频，通过众包成对比较收集主观评分，为评估和开发适用于NVC的视频质量指标提供基准资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频流量占全球网络流量的重要份额，神经视频编解码器（NVC）作为新兴的深度学习视频压缩方法带来了新的时间压缩范式，传统质量评估方法难以应对这些新挑战，因此需要针对NVC的主观质量数据集来支撑质量指标的发展和评估。

Method: 构建了一个大规模主观测评数据集，视频同时由神经与传统编解码器压缩；通过众包成对比较（pairwise comparisons）的方式收集主观评分，并公开数据集供社区使用。

Result: 成功构建并公开了一个大规模主观压缩视频质量数据集，涵盖神经和传统编解码器压缩的视频，为神经视频编解码器的质量指标开发与基准测试提供了宝贵资源。

Conclusion: 该数据集为评估神经视频编解码器引入的新时间压缩范式提供了重要基础，有助于推动适用于NVC的视频质量指标的开发、基准测试和进一步改进。

Abstract: Video traffic constitutes a significant share of global web traffic. To reduce its volume, video codecs have been developed and continuously improved. While the industry has achieved substantial progress in traditional video coding, neural video codecs (NVCs) have recently emerged as a new approach that applies deep learning to video compression. This creates new challenges for compression quality assessment, which is essential for the further development and improvement of such codecs. In particular, it is important to evaluate the novel temporal compression paradigms introduced by NVCs. In this work, we present a large-scale subjective dataset of videos compressed with both neural and traditional video codecs. The subjective scores were collected through crowd-sourced pairwise comparisons. The proposed dataset provides a valuable resource for the development and benchmarking of video quality metrics tailored to neural video codecs. The dataset is available at the following link: https://videoprocessing.github.io/nvc-dataset-benchmark

</details>


### [97] [GenFirst: Generation Before Reconstruction for Stable End-to-End Latent Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29335)
*Guangting Zheng,Yiyuan Zhang,Tao Yang,Yunpeng Chen,Rui Zhu,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了无需潜在空间坍缩的直接端到端训练，提出GenFirst（先生成后重建）策略，通过分析发现熵项对防止潜在空间坍缩至关重要，且重建与生成存在不对称学习动力学。在ImageNet-256上SiT达到gFID 0.97（CFG）/1.45（无CFG），MMDiT在GenEval上达到0.90，并扩展到共享视觉潜在空间和统一图文生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在生成模型通常采用两阶段流水线：先训练变分自编码器进行重建，再在冻结的潜在空间上训练生成模型。但重建优化的潜在空间不一定有利于生成。端到端联合训练虽具吸引力，但面临潜在空间坍缩和生成-重建冲突两大挑战。

Method: 通过分析不同目标函数如何塑造潜在空间，识别出两个关键洞察：(1) KL散度目标中的熵项对防止坍缩至关重要——重建和先验拟合倾向于缩小后验，而熵保留非退化潜在不确定性；(2) 重建与生成存在不对称学习动力学——重建快速且有强监督，生成较慢且难以优化。基于此提出GenFirst策略：先生成目标在弱重建压力下塑造潜在空间，随后逐步增强重建以恢复视觉细节。目标函数支持连续自回归先验（精确似然）和SiT先验（隐式似然）。

Result: SiT在ImageNet-256上达到gFID 0.97（带CFG）和1.45（无CFG），MMDiT在文本到图像生成上GenEval得分0.90。框架还可扩展到共享视觉潜在生成与表示学习，以及连续统一图文生成。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了稳定端到端潜在学习在多种生成先验和模态上的通用性，为潜在生成模型的端到端训练提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: Latent generative models typically follow a two-stage pipeline, training a variational autoencoder for reconstruction and then a generative model on the frozen latent space. Since reconstruction-optimized latents are not necessarily generation-friendly, jointly training both models is an appealing alternative. However, direct end-to-end training remains challenging, as it is prone to latent collapse and faces a generation-reconstruction conflict. We revisit this problem by analyzing how different objectives shape the latent space and identify two key insights. First, the entropy term in the Kullback-Leibler divergence objective is essential for preventing collapse: reconstruction and prior fitting tend to shrink the posterior, while entropy preserves non-degenerate latent uncertainty. Second, reconstruction and generation exhibit asymmetric learning dynamics: reconstruction is fast and strongly supervised, whereas generation is slower and harder to optimize. Based on these insights, we achieve the first direct end-to-end training without latent collapse and propose GenFirst, a simple generation-before-reconstruction strategy. The generative objective first shapes the latent space under weak reconstruction pressure, after which reconstruction is progressively strengthened to recover visual details. We validate GenFirst with continuous autoregressive priors with exact likelihoods and SiT priors with implicit likelihoods. With our end-to-end objective and GenFirst, SiT achieves a gFID of 0.97 with CFG and 1.45 without CFG on ImageNet-256, while MMDiT reaches a GenEval score of 0.90 on text-to-image generation. Beyond image generation, we extend the framework to shared visual latents for generation and representation learning, and to continuous unified text-image generation. These results demonstrate the generality of stable end-to-end latent learning across generative priors and modalities.

</details>


### [98] [FISICA: A Deployed Service for Plantar-Pressure and Posture Assessment with Ontology-Grounded Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29336)
*Juhwan Song,Heejung Kim,Juntae Noh,Jonghak Ryu,Huiju Park,Junseong Lee,Dohyeon Ahn,Byungwoo Jo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FISICA是一个已在生产环境中运行的体态评估与推荐服务，通过一次站立会话的两张照片即可返回足底载荷测量、姿态坐标、3D虚拟化身、视觉报告以及鞋类和锻炼候选推荐。其核心方法贡献在于虚拟化身的构建：不使用经过调谐的增益将测量角度映射到骨骼上，而是用与受试者相同的测量函数来测量虚拟化身，并通过迭代求解使两者一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体态评估方法在将角度测量映射到虚拟化身骨骼时依赖人工调谐的增益参数，缺乏通用性和准确性。同时，生产级体态评估系统需要综合硬件测量、规则驱动的推荐逻辑与可解释性，而目前缺乏一套集成的端到端生产解决方案。

Method: 该方法由三部分构成：(1) 硬件层面，采用定制体脂秤，配备1cm网格上634个力敏感元件和四个称重传感器，采集足底载荷数据；(2) 推荐层面，采用规则驱动的评估器控制所有推荐，语言模型仅负责解释已存储的结果；(3) 核心算法层面，在采样不变的脊柱度量上，使用与受试者相同的测量函数来测量3D虚拟化身，迭代求解直至两者一致，取代传统的调谐增益映射方法。

Result: 该脊柱度量将正常与脊柱后凸（kyphotic）记录区分开达7.2度，而单关节公式仅为0.9度。生产环境中，通用API中位响应时间为0.023秒，足底压力分析为0.45秒，推荐为2.16-2.26秒且规则部分每次试验均在1秒内完成。服务的姿态关键点图在公共数据上达到0.960 PCK@0.2，目录包含699款鞋和10,500条类型化事实。已批准的研究为尚未完成的验证提供了放射学参考标准。

Conclusion: FISICA证明了以测量一致的化身构建策略可显著提升体态诊断的判别力，同时以规则驱动+语言模型解释的架构保障了生产环境中的可靠性、可控性与可解释性。系统在延迟和精度上均满足生产要求，但放射学验证仍待完成。

Abstract: FISICA is a body-assessment and recommendation service running in production. One standing session with two photographs returns foot-loading measures, posture coordinates, a driven 3D avatar, a visual report, and ranked shoe and exercise candidates. Measurement comes from a purpose-built scale carrying 634 force-sensitive elements on a 1 cm grid and four load cells, and a rule-based evaluator controls every recommendation while a language model only explains the stored result. The method contribution is the avatar. Instead of mapping a measured angle onto a rig through a tuned gain, we measure the avatar with the same function used on the subject and solve until the two agree, on a sampling-invariant spinal metric that separated a normal from a kyphotic record by 7.2 degrees against 0.9 degrees for a single-joint formulation. In production, general APIs respond at a 0.023 s median, plantar-pressure analysis at 0.45 s, and recommendation at 2.16 s to 2.26 s with the rule-based portion under one second in every trial. The served keypoint graph reaches 0.960 PCK@0.2 on public data, and the catalog holds 699 shoes with 10,500 typed facts. An approved study supplies the radiographic reference for the validation still ahead.

</details>


### [99] [GSPotential: Camera Potential Field for Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29346)
*Zeyuan An,Yanghang Xiao,Zhiying Leng,Yijun Feng,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSPotential利用相机势场（Camera Potential Field）量化视图空间中监督的不均匀分布，通过概率性球面采样策略在低势区域放置虚拟相机以提供几何引导，并通过方向覆盖线索实现弱覆盖区域的保守高斯更新，从而解决3D高斯泼溅在稀疏视图场景下的过拟合和几何伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在稀疏视图场景下因光度监督不足而严重过拟合并产生几何伪影。现有方法虽引入外部先验（深度、点云、扩散模型）进行正则化，但往往忽视了监督在视图空间中的非均匀分布，导致先验使用和基元控制缺乏针对性。

Method: 提出GSPotential框架：（1）利用相机势场（Camera Potential Field）量化视图空间中的监督失衡，识别光度约束最匮乏的"监督谷"区域；（2）设计概率性球面采样策略，在低势区域放置信息量丰富的虚拟相机，从这些视角渲染点云以提供靶向几何引导；（3）利用势场提供的方向覆盖线索，在弱覆盖空间扇区中对高斯更新进行保守约束。

Result: 大量实验表明，GSPotential在保持竞争力的训练效率的同时，实现了高保真的重建质量。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模视图空间监督分布的不均匀性并利用势场引导虚拟相机采样与高斯更新控制，GSPotential有效缓解了稀疏视图下3DGS的过拟合与几何伪影问题，兼顾了重建保真度与训练效率。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved remarkable success in photorealistic rendering, yet it suffers from severe overfitting and geometric artifacts in sparse-view scenarios due to the inherent deficiency of photometric supervision. Recent advances have attempted to regularize optimization by incorporating external priors, such as depth, point clouds, or diffusion models. However, these methods typically overlook the non-uniform distribution of supervision across the viewing space, resulting in limited specificity in prior use and primitive control. In this paper, we propose GSPotential, a framework that quantifies view-space supervision imbalance using a Camera Potential Field. Our key insight is to identify supervision valleys where photometric constraints are most deficient, and use the potential field to guide reconstruction from two complementary aspects. First, we devise a probabilistic spherical sampling strategy that places informative virtual cameras in low-potential regions. Point-cloud renderings from these views then provide targeted geometric guidance. Second, the same field provides a directional coverage cue for conservative Gaussian updates in weakly covered spatial sectors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GSPotential achieves high reconstruction fidelity while maintaining competitive training efficiency.

</details>


### [100] [Sketch2Inspire: Structure-Sensitive Evaluation for Product Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29364)
*Ge Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sketch2Inspire，一个基于Amazon Berkeley Objects的子集构建的产品设计检索基准资源，融合文本、边缘素描和文本-素描混合查询，区分类别级检索与结构敏感的类内检索，并包含人工分级参考协议。评估显示基于CLIP编码器的轻量级系统在多种相关性定义下，加权晚期融合 consistently 表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期产品设计检索不仅需要类别识别，还需要同时匹配短语义意图和粗略结构线索。现有产品图像资源和通用图文检索基准很少将类别检索与类内结构匹配分离，缺乏能够诊断多模态信息贡献的评估资源。

Method: 从Amazon Berkeley Objects中筛选构建Sketch2Inspire，包含对齐的文本查询、基于边缘的素描代理查询及融合查询。设计了三个层级的相关性评估：宽泛相关性、自动结构敏感相关性和人工分级相关性。评估一个基于预训练CLIP编码器的轻量级参考系统，比较纯文本检索、纯素描检索、加权晚期融合和文本优先重排序四种策略（不更新模型权重）。

Result: 宽泛相关性下晚期融合取得最高nDCG=0.9962；自动结构敏感相关性下晚期融合再次最高（nDCG=0.7015），优于纯文本检索（0.5912）；人工分级结果中晚期融合nDCG@10=0.9133，纯文本检索第二（0.9030）。

Conclusion: 多模态输入的检索增益取决于相关性的定义方式。Sketch2Inspire提供了一个诊断性基准资源，用于评估各模态的贡献，并支持开发结合独立人工标注的结构感知产品检索协议。

Abstract: Early-stage product design retrieval often requires more than category recognition: designers may need reference examples that match both a short semantic intent and a rough structural cue. Existing product-image resources and generic image--text retrieval benchmarks rarely separate category retrieval from within-category structural fit. We present Sketch2Inspire, built from a curated subset of Amazon Berkeley Objects with aligned text queries, edge-based sketch-proxy queries, and fused text--sketch queries. The resource separates broad category-level retrieval from structure-sensitive within-category retrieval and includes a human-graded reference protocol for calibration. We evaluate a lightweight reference system based on pretrained CLIP-family encoders, comparing text-only retrieval, sketch-only retrieval, weighted late fusion, and text-first reranking without updating model weights. Under broad relevance, late fusion obtains the highest score (nDCG = 0.9962). Under automatic structure-sensitive relevance, late fusion again obtains the highest score (nDCG = 0.7015), exceeding text-only retrieval (nDCG = 0.5912). In the human-graded results, late fusion obtains the highest nDCG@10 (0.9133), while text-only retrieval ranks second (0.9030). These results show that the retrieval gain from multimodal input depends on how relevance is defined. Sketch2Inspire therefore provides a diagnostic resource for evaluating modality contribution and supports the development of structure-aware product-retrieval protocols with independent human annotation.

</details>


### [101] [Think, Look, and Revise: Inconsistency-Aware Visual Self-Correction in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29374)
*Yu Cheng,Arushi Goel,Hakan Bilen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReVISE框架，通过验证和动态错误恢复机制增强工具增强的多模态推理，使MLLMs能够验证工具输出、重新表述查询并在工具不可靠时回退到内在推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具增强多模态推理方法很少验证工具输出，导致无法检测和恢复工具失败，从而限制了复杂视觉任务的推理能力。

Method: ReVISE包含两个核心组件：(1) 精心构建的训练数据集，监督模型的反省行为，使其能验证工具证据、在视觉不匹配时重新表述查询、在外部工具不可靠时回退到内在接地；(2) 基于强化学习的目标奖励机制，鼓励内部反省并惩罚空间错位。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，ReVISE相较于现有方法取得了一致的性能提升，验证了错误检测与纠正在工具增强多模态推理中的重要性。

Conclusion: 工具输出的验证与动态错误恢复是工具增强多模态推理中关键且被忽视的环节，ReVISE证明了通过监督式反省训练和强化学习奖励能够有效增强模型对工具失败的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Tool-augmented multimodal reasoning integrates external tools (e.g., object detection, depth estimation) into multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to address perceptual bottlenecks in complex visual tasks. However, existing approaches rarely verify tool outputs, limiting their ability to detect and recover from tool failures. We propose ReVISE, a framework that equips MLLMs with verification and dynamic error recovery for tool-augmented reasoning. ReVISE introduces (1) a curated training dataset that supervises reflective behaviors, enabling models to validate tool-derived evidence, reformulate queries when visual mismatches arise, and fall back to intrinsic grounding when external tools are unreliable; and (2) a reinforcement learning based targeted rewards that encourage internal reflection and penalize spatial misalignment. Experiments on several benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods, highlighting the importance of error detection and correction in tool-augmented multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [102] [FiLM-GPNet: Geometry-Aware Pseudo-Supervised Phase Restoration with Zero-Shot Generalization for Large Temporal InSAR Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29384)
*Getnet Demil,Muhammad Farhan Humayun,Tomi Westerlund,Jukka Heikkonen,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FiLM-GPNet，一种基于几何条件（geometry-conditioned）的缠绕相位恢复网络，利用Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）和7维几何描述符显式适应不同SAR采集几何，通过Goldstein滤波器的伪监督和干涉物理的三元组闭合一致性正则化训练，在Capella Spotlight数据上将时间残差降低66-68%，闭合误差降低10-13%，并展现出良好的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密商业SAR时间序列使得时序InSAR分析成为可能，但固定经典滤波器（如Goldstein滤波）在异构采集几何下无法适应，导致相位质量退化与时序一致性受损。作者旨在解决该问题，提出一种能显式适应采集差异的几何条件化网络。难点：如何使模型适应不同轨道的几何差异，同时保持干涉测量物理一致性。

Method: FiLM-GPNet采用以下核心技术：1) 使用Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）层结合7维per-pair几何描述符，显式编码采集几何差异并调节网络特征；2) 通过Goldstein滤波后的干涉图进行伪监督训练；3) 利用三元组闭合一致性（triplet-closure consistency）作为干涉物理先验进行正则化；4) 同时估计逐像素aleatoric不确定性。

Result: 在IEEE GRSS 2026数据融合竞赛的三个Capella Spotlight堆栈上：Hawaii与Western Australia相比Goldstein基线时间残差分别降低68%和66%；闭合误差分别降低10%和13%；Western Australia上解缠成功率提升7.7个百分点，DEM NMAD改善31%；对洛杉矶第三堆栈无重训练零样本泛化表现良好。

Conclusion: 几何条件化的相位恢复方法（FiLM-GPNet）相比固定经典滤波在异构采集几何堆栈上具有显著优势，且具备跨地理和几何域的零样本泛化能力，可作为固定经典滤波的有效替代方案，支持在不同堆栈间的泛化部署。

Abstract: The growing availability of dense commercial Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) time series enables temporal Interferometric SAR (InSAR) analysis, but fixed classical filters fail under heterogeneous acquisition geometries, degrading phase quality and temporal consistency. We propose FiLM-GPNet, a geometry-conditioned network for wrapped-phase restoration that explicitly adapts to acquisition differences using Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) and a 7D per-pair geometry descriptor. The model is trained with pseudo-supervision from Goldstein-filtered interferograms and regularized by interferometric physics via triplet-closure consistency, while also estimating per-pixel aleatoric uncertainty. Experiments on three Capella Spotlight stacks from the IEEE GRSS 2026 Data Fusion Contest show that FiLM-GPNet reduces temporal residual by 68% (Hawaii) and 66% (Western Australia) relative to the Goldstein baseline, alongside closure error reductions of 10% and 13%, respectively. In Western Australia, it further improves unwrapping success rate by 7.7 percentage points and Digital Elevation Model (DEM) Normalized Median Absolute Deviation (NMAD) by 31%. The model also shows strong zero-shot generalization to a geographically and geometrically distinct third stack (Los Angeles) without retraining, supporting geometry-conditioned restoration as an effective alternative to fixed classical filtering across heterogeneous stacks.

</details>


### [103] [GATE: Reliability-Gated Gaussian Evidence Fusion for Training-Free Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29395)
*Pedram MohajerAnsari,Amir Salarpour,Run Wang,Mert D. Pesé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GATE是一个无需训练的、两阶段直推式测试时自适应框架，通过构建文本高斯和图像高斯两类互补的分布证据，并利用类别级可靠性门控和广义专家乘积融合，在完全冻结图像编码器、文本编码器和提示参数的情况下，显著提升CLIP和SigLIP等视觉语言模型在分布偏移场景下的零样本识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（如CLIP和SigLIP）在预训练分布之外的测试数据上预测性能会退化。现有的测试时自适应方法要么仅依赖提示侧（prompt-side）自适应，要么仅依赖图像侧目标证据，未能充分利用二者的互补信息。本文旨在提出一种无需训练、无需源数据和目标标签的方法，通过同时利用文本和图像两侧的分布证据来提升冻结视觉语言模型在目标域上的鲁棒性。

Method: GATE采用两阶段直推式测试时自适应框架：1）在共享的视觉-语言特征空间中构建两类互补的高斯证据源——文本高斯（由多个语言描述估计得到）和图像高斯（由可靠的未标注目标样本估计得到）；2）设计类别级可靠性门控机制来控制图像伪证据的影响程度；3）采用分数级广义专家乘积（Product-of-Experts）融合，对原始零样本logits产生归一化的残差校正，实现校正项的补充。

Result: 在细粒度识别数据集、ImageNet系列分布偏移、多种CLIP骨干网络以及SigLIP-B/16上，GATE在每个基准/骨干网络组中均取得最佳平均准确率。相比零样本基线平均提升5.41个点，比最强的非GATE基线平均提升1.94个点。

Conclusion: GATE证明了在完全冻结视觉语言模型参数的条件下，通过可靠性门控的分布级证据进行测试时自适应是有效且实用的方案，为无需训练的VLM自适应提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP and SigLIP provide strong zero-shot recognition, but their predictions can degrade when deployed on target data that differ from the pretraining distribution.
