To address these challenges, we propose VCAR, a training-free coarse-to-fine segmentation strategy based on View Completeness and Axis-aware Boundary Refinement. In the coarse stage, a visibility-based weighted multi-view voting scheme rapidly localizes the target. In the fine stage, an object-centric sphere derived from the coarse result generates supplementary viewpoints via Spherical Spiral Sampling (SSS), allowing multi-view voting on the augmented views to precisely refine object boundaries and suppress irrelevant 3D Gaussians. Moreover, we introduce Axis-aware Boundary Refinement (ABR) to mitigate artifacts from anisotropic primitives. By decomposing the projected 2D covariance into per-axis contributions, ABR identifies the dominant axis responsible for boundary leakage and applies targeted anisotropic compression exclusively along that axis. Extensive experiments on NVOS and LERF demonstrate that VCAR achieves state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy and efficiency without training. Our code is available at https://github.com/DDKK0526/VCAR.

</details>


### [238] [SurgSkill-Bench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Surgical Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30872)
*Chaohui Dang,Zheheng Jiang,James Glasbey,David Luke,Theodoros Arvanitis,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgSkill-Bench，一个包含214个手术训练模拟视频、六维OSATS评分和专家评语注释的视频-评分-文本基准数据集，并定义了两种评估设置（纯视频OSATS预测和专家评语辅助预测）。在内部视频级验证中，内容自适应关键帧采样提升了纯视频性能，评语提供了额外的评分相关信号，最佳平均AUROC达到0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术技能评估对于外科培训和结构化反馈至关重要，但当前流程依赖大量人工专家审查。现有自动化方法主要集中在视觉输入上，缺乏同时支持手术表现、结构化技能评分和评估者反馈的联合研究。

Method: 构建SurgSkill-Bench数据集（214个视频+六维OSATS评分+专家自由文本评语），定义两种评估设置：纯视频OSATS预测和专家评语辅助预测。采用冻结视觉主干网络、内容自适应关键帧采样和视频-文本共注意力融合模块进行受控基线实验。

Result: 在内部视频级验证下，内容自适应采样在纯视频设置中提升了性能；评估者评语在辅助设置中提供了额外的评分相关信号。最佳平均AUROC达到0.88（基于数据集特定中位数二分类）。

Conclusion: SurgSkill-Bench为手术技能自动化评估提供了多模态基准，但需注意数据集规模、元数据完整性和评语辅助预测解释等方面的评估限制。代码将在后续公开发布。

Abstract: Objective assessment of surgical technical skill is important for surgical training and structured feedback, but current workflows remain dependent on labor-intensive expert review. Existing automated approaches primarily focus on visual inputs and provide limited support for jointly studying operative performance, structured skill scores, and evaluator feedback. We introduce SurgSkill-Bench, an initial video-score-text benchmark-style dataset containing 214 surgical training simulation videos, six-dimensional OSATS scores, and expert free-text comments. We define two evaluation settings: video-only OSATS prediction for automated assessment and post hoc expert-comment-assisted prediction, where evaluator comments are available as auxiliary information. We provide controlled baseline experiments using representative frozen visual backbones, content-adaptive key-frame sampling, and a simple video-text co-attention fusion module. Under internal video-level validation, content-adaptive sampling improves video-only performance in this dataset, while evaluator comments provide additional score-related signal in the assisted setting. The best mean AUROC reaches 0.88 under dataset-specific median dichotomization. We further discuss evaluation constraints related to dataset scale, metadata completeness, and the interpretation of comment-assisted prediction. Code will be released publicly at a later date.

</details>


### [239] [A Controlled Evaluation of Model Rankings and Input Reliance in Surface Water Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30895)
*Kittipat Phunjanna,Kristóf Karacs,Chayut Ngamkhanong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出地表水分割中常用的聚合指标（如全局IoU）仅能排序模型配置，但无法解释性能差异的原因、排序的稳定性或模型对特定输入的依赖；通过多种分析手段（重复配置比较、配对测试块分析、固定检查点输入压力测试、地理加权）揭示排序的不稳定性，并提出评估证据应与声明相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于揭示聚合指标（如全局IoU）在模型配置排序中的局限性——它不能回答"为何某一配置更好"、"相近排序是否稳定"以及"预测对单个输入的依赖程度"这三个关键问题。

Method: 主要在Sen1Floods11数据集上开展四类分析：1) 重复配置比较（多随机种子）；2) 配对测试块分析；3) 固定检查点的输入压力测试；4) 地理重加权；并在GEOID-Flood数据集上进行有监督输入配置的次级评估。

Result: 跨模态学生模型在Sen1Floods11上取得最高三种子平均IoU，但相近排序随种子和地理加权而变化；辅助输入排序在Swin-UNet和U-Net之间存在差异；GEOID-Flood评估显示有监督辅助输入效应具有一致性，但精确架构排序仍依赖配置；固定检查点测试证实对地形和WorldCover的依赖，但未显示干净输入的性能优势。

Conclusion: 聚合指标对排序完整配置仍有用，但排序稳定性、组件归因、输入依赖和部署范围需要不同类型证据来支撑；性能评估的证据类型应与所提出的声明相匹配。

Abstract: Performance evaluation for surface-water segmentation commonly uses an aggregate metric such as global intersection-over-union (IoU) to rank model configurations. However, a configuration ranking does not by itself establish why one system performs better, whether a close ordering is stable, or how strongly predictions rely on individual inputs. We examine these distinctions primarily on Sen1Floods11 through repeated configuration comparisons, paired test-chip analysis, fixed-checkpoint input stress tests, and geographic reweighting, with a targeted secondary evaluation of supervised input configurations on GEOID-Flood. The cross-modal student achieves the highest three-seed mean IoU on Sen1Floods11, but close orderings vary across seeds and geographic weighting, while ancillary-input rankings differ between Swin-UNet and U-Net. The GEOID-Flood evaluation shows substantial agreement in supervised ancillary-input effects, although the exact architecture ordering remains configuration dependent. Fixed-checkpoint tests further establish reliance on terrain and WorldCover without establishing a clean-input performance benefit, while target semantics and the later WorldCover prior restrict the evaluation to retrospective all-water segmentation. These results show that aggregate metrics remain useful for ranking complete configurations, but ranking stability, component attribution, input reliance, and deployment scope require distinct evidence. Performance evaluation should therefore match the evidence reported to the claim being made.

</details>


### [240] [Rad-R: A Raw-ADC Radar Dataset and Capture-Invariant SSM for Hardware-Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30896)
*Mainak Mallick,Junghwan Yim,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Rad-R，一个配备4芯片77GHz TI MMWCAS-RF-EVM级联（192个虚拟通道）的原始ADC雷达数据集，为每种记录配对受控硬件故障及校准严重程度、独立物理严重性测量以及帧同步的IMU、温度、GPS和摄像头流。提出原始IQ Mamba SSM模型（RadrNet）家族，在基准测试中表现优异，其中RadrNet-DS-CI排名第一（0.663 macro-F1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载毫米波雷达可能发生振动、天线错位、雷达罩堵塞和接收通道退化等硬件故障，这些故障会在感知开始前破坏信号。由于每种故障条件必须在物理硬件上诱导和测量，故障数据极其稀缺，现有原始雷达数据缺乏系统性的控制故障标注。

Method: 构建Rad-R数据集，使用4芯片77GHz TI MMWCAS-RF-EVM级联系统（192个虚拟通道）采集原始ADC数据，每个记录配对一个可控严重程度的硬件故障、独立物理严重性测量以及多模态同步流。提出RadrNet（原始IQ Mamba状态空间模型）及其变体RadrNet-DS（绝对相位）和RadrNet-DS-CI（用每帧标准化幅度和相对chirp间相位替代绝对相位），在七种代表性视觉骨干网络和不同协议下进行基准测试。

Result: 剪片内性能接近饱和（macro-F1>0.98），但跨严重程度泛化仍然困难（绝对相位RadrNet-DS降至0.49）。RadrNet-DS-CI在受控基准上排名第一（0.663 vs 最强RD-CNN的0.628，三次随机种子），RadrNet系列在任意时间和少量样本预算上也领先。雷达微多普勒与独立测量的IMU振动能量协变（跨条件混合Spearman ρ=0.41）。

Conclusion: Rad-R作为首个配合受控硬件故障和物理严重性测量的原始ADC雷达数据集，揭示了剪片内故障分类接近饱和但跨严重程度泛化仍具挑战的现状。RadrNet-DS-CI通过标准化幅度和相对相位设计改善了泛化能力，同时跨模态分析验证了雷达微多普勒与IMU振动能量之间的关联。

Abstract: Automotive mmWave radar can develop vibration, antenna misalignment, radome blockage, and receive-channel degradation that corrupt the signal before perception begins. Data for these faults are scarce because each condition must be induced and measured on physical hardware. We introduce Rad-R, a raw-ADC dataset captured with a 4-chip 77GHz TI MMWCAS-RF-EVM cascade (192 virtual channels). Unlike existing raw-radar datasets, Rad-R pairs each recording with a controlled hardware fault at a calibrated severity, an independent physical severity measurement, and frame-synchronised IMU, temperature, GPS, and camera streams. Rad-R is a single-session dataset, so our generalisation claims are confined to a controlled cross-severity protocol in which train and test use physically distinct captures. A reproducible benchmark evaluates seven representative vision backbones and the proposed raw-IQ Mamba SSM (RadrNet) under within-clip, chirp-wise anytime, few-shot cross-capture, and controlled cross-severity protocols. Within-clip performance is near-saturated ($>0.98$ macro-F1), whereas cross-severity generalisation remains difficult: the absolute-phase RadrNet-DS falls to $0.49$ macro-F1. RadrNet-DS-CI replaces absolute phase with per-frame-standardised magnitude and relative chirp-to-chirp phase and ranks first on the controlled benchmark ($0.663$ vs. $0.628$ for the strongest RD-CNN; three seeds); the RadrNet family also leads on the anytime and few-shot budgets. A descriptive cross-modal analysis further finds that radar micro-Doppler covaries with independently measured IMU vibration energy (pooled Spearman $ρ=0.41$ across conditions). The complete dataset and code will be released publicly under permissive licences.

</details>


### [241] [Uncertainty-Aware Trajectory Forecasting from Imperfect Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30899)
*Stephane Da Silva Martins,Victor Petrovic,Emanuel Aldea,Sylvie Le Hégarat-Mascle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不确定性感知的轨迹预测框架，将追踪器输出的可靠性信号（包括定位不确定性和数据关联模糊性）通过高斯分布编码传递至预测器，使预测输出为高斯轨迹而非确定性坐标，并通过OU扰动与知识蒸馏训练，提升在真实追踪噪声下的预测精度与概率可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测模型大多在干净的人工标注轨迹上训练和评估，而实际部署中依赖多目标追踪器产生的观测数据，存在定位抖动、检测不稳定/缺失以及数据关联歧义等问题。这些噪声通常被忽略或通过去噪处理去除，但作者认为追踪产生的可靠性线索本身应作为有价值的信息传递给预测器，而非被丢弃。

Method: 提出一种即插即用的不确定性感知公式化方法：(1) 将每个观测状态编码为高斯分布表示，其协方差通过全方差律整合检测级定位不确定性与关联级模糊性；(2) 对现有预测骨干网络进行最小化架构改动，输入为高斯观测，输出为高斯预测而非确定性坐标；(3) 训练时结合时间相关的奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克（OU）扰动模拟结构化观测噪声，并采用基于响应的知识蒸馏，从在干净轨迹上训练的教师模型迁移知识，以增强模型对结构化观测噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Oxford Town Centre和VIRAT数据集上使用真实追踪器输出，以及ETH/UCY的伪检测协议上进行了实验验证。结果表明所提出的方法显著提升了位移精度，并改善了概率预测的可靠性-锐度权衡。

Conclusion: 将追踪器产生的可靠性或不确定性信号视为信息源而非噪声，能够有效提升轨迹预测在现实部署条件下的表现。该方法在保持对现有模型最小改动的同时，兼顾了预测精度与概率校准质量。

Abstract: Most trajectory forecasting models are trained on clean annotated histories, and are often evaluated under the same idealized assumption, although practical deployments rely on trajectories produced by imperfect multi-object trackers. The real-world observations exhibit localization jitter, missed or unstable detections, and data-association ambiguity, which are usually either ignored or removed through denoising. This paper instead treats tracking-derived reliability cues as an informative signal to be propagated to the predictor. We propose a plug-in uncertainty-aware formulation in which each observed state is encoded as an uncertain state representation, modeled by a Gaussian distribution whose covariance combines detection-level localization uncertainty and association-level ambiguity through the law of total variance. Existing backbones are adapted with minimal architectural changes: input trajectories are represented as Gaussian observations, and predicted trajectories are produced as Gaussian forecasts rather than deterministic coordinates. To train predictors that remain robust under structured observation noise, we combine temporally correlated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck perturbations with response-based knowledge distillation from a teacher trained on clean trajectories. Experiments on Oxford Town Centre and VIRAT using real tracker outputs, together with a complementary ETH/UCY pseudo-detection protocol, show that the proposed formulation improves displacement accuracy and the reliability-sharpness trade-off of probabilistic forecasts.

</details>


### [242] [Audio-Driven Adversarial Defense for 3D Talking Face Generation with totally Visual Fidelity Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30951)
*Rui-Qing Sun,Chen-Hao Cui,Hui-Yang Zhao,Tian Lan,Zhijing Wu,Xian-Ling Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于心理声学掩蔽的音频域防御方法，通过将保护性扰动隐藏在感知掩蔽的频率区域中，在不显著损害感知质量的前提下，有效抑制音频驱动的3D说话人脸生成，实现隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动的3D说话人脸生成技术可以从单目视频重建可复用的3D人像并通过任意语音驱动，导致身份冒充的现实威胁日益严重。现有防御方法主要在视觉域注入微妙扰动来破坏身份获取，但人脸具有强结构先验和社会敏感性，视觉扰动容易损害画质，且会被常见的现实变换（如缩放）轻松削弱。因此需要一种新的防御思路。

Method: 将防御模态从视觉域转移到音频域，利用心理声学掩蔽原理，将保护性扰动隐藏在人耳感知不到（被掩蔽）的语音频率区域中，从而在降低感知失真的同时，干扰3D说话人脸生成中的面部动画渲染过程，使生成的面部动画不可靠。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能有效退化3D说话人脸生成效果，同时保持令人满意的感知质量，证明了心理声学引导的音频扰动在隐私保护人像方向上的实用性和前景。

Conclusion: 心理声学引导的音频域扰动为隐私保护的人像防护提供了一种实用且富有前景的新方向，弥补了视觉域防御在感知质量和鲁棒性方面的不足。

Abstract: The rapid development of generative portrait models has raised growing concerns about privacy leakage and identity misuse. In particular, audio-driven 3D talking face generation can reconstruct a reusable 3D portrait of a target person from a monocular video and animate it with arbitrary speech, making realistic identity impersonation alarmingly practical. Existing proactive defenses mainly operate in the visual domain by injecting subtle perturbations into acial regions to disrupt identity acquisition. However, such perturbations often compromise visual quality due to the strong structural priors and social sensitivity of human faces, and are easily weakened by common real-world transformations such as resizing. To overcome these limitations, we propose an imperceptible audio defense for audio-driven 3D talking face generation by shifting protection from the visual modality to the audio modality. Specifically,we exploit psychoacoustic masking to hide protective perturbations within perceptually masked frequency regions of the speech signal, thereby reducing perceptual distortion while suppressing reliable facial animation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method effectively degrades 3D talking face generation while preserving favorable perceptual quality. These findings highlight psychoacoustically guided audio perturbations as a practical and promising direction for privacy-preserving portrait protection.

</details>


### [243] [LOCI: A Locator-Critic with Refinement Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30959)
*Walid Bousselham,Mathilde Caron,Arsha Nagrani,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Locator-Critic (LOCI)，一个无需训练的框架，将视觉搜索与证据验证解耦，通过定位器和评论器两个智能体的迭代交互，显著提升VLM在复杂视觉理解任务上的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在复杂视觉理解任务上仍表现不佳，作者认为核心问题并非高层推理能力不足，而是VLM无法准确定位图像中的关键细节，导致产生看似合理但基于错误感知基础的推理。

Method: LOCI采用训练无关的双智能体框架：Locator智能体负责提出候选视觉证据，Critic智能体负责评估证据的相关性和充分性。两个智能体进行迭代精化循环，逐步改进证据直到足以回答给定问题，实现视觉搜索与证据验证的解耦和自我纠正。

Result: LOCI在多个复杂视觉基准上取得最先进结果，对开源模型（如Qwen3-VL）和闭源模型（如Gemini 2.5 Pro）均有显著提升：Qwen3-VL在V*上+12.1、HR-Bench上+5.8、VisualProbe-Hard上+11.2；Gemini 2.5 Pro在V*上+8.9、HR-Bench上+4.3、VisualProbe-Hard上+4.8。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉定位与证据验证解耦并引入迭代自我纠正机制，LOCI有效解决了VLM在复杂视觉理解中的关键细节定位问题，进而大幅提升推理准确性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) still struggle on tasks requiring complex visual understanding. We argue that the core issue is not high-level reasoning, but instead failing to locate critical details in the image. Due to this shortcoming, VLMs generate often plausible but incorrect reasoning based on flawed perceptual grounding. To address this, we propose Locator-Critic (LOCI), a training-free framework that decouples visual search from evidence verification. LOCI employs a Locator agent to propose candidate visual evidence and a separate Critic agent to evaluate its relevance and sufficiency. These agents engage in an iterative refinement loop, progressively improving the evidence until it is adequate to answer the given question. This decoupled, self-correcting process yields substantial performance gains, achieving state-of-the-art results on multiple complex visual benchmarks. LOCI improves accuracy for both open-weight models like Qwen3-VL (+12.1 on V*, +5.8 on HR-Bench and +11.2 on VisualProbe-Hard) and proprietary models like Gemini 2.5 Pro (+8.9 on V*, +4.3 on HR-Bench, +4.8 on VisualProbe-Hard).

</details>


### [244] [Vision Models Predict Urban Scene Appraisal with Limited Neural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30964)
*Kaizhen Tan,Yuantao Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用公开EEG数据测试预训练视觉嵌入对城市街道场景的表征是否与人类大脑的感知组织方式一致。结果发现，虽然这些嵌入能较好地预测人类评分（最高r=0.87），但神经网络表征与大脑表征的对应度普遍很低（最佳DINOv2 ViT-B仅达噪声下限的29.6%），且两者指标不相关。预测评分并不能证明模型以大脑的方式组织场景表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉嵌入常被用于建模人类对城市景观的评价，但此前仅通过预测人类评分来验证。高预测准确率并不能证明这些嵌入的潜在组织结构与人类感知一致，因此需要从表征几何层面直接与脑数据对照验证。

Method: 使用63名成人观看并评分56个柏林街道场景的公开EEG数据，估计场景随时间变化的表现几何（representational geometry），并将17种特征空间的表征几何与脑数据进行对应度比较。特征空间涵盖语言监督、自监督、类别监督和密集预测训练范式、两个数量级的模型规模以及可解释控制（如Gabor能量描述子）。同时分别检验模型预测人类评分的能力与神经对应度之间的关系。

Result: 所有模型与脑数据的对应度普遍偏低：最佳模型DINOv2 ViT-B仅达到噪声下限的29.6%，全部模型范围在11.0%-29.6%之间；Gabor能量描述子与最佳模型无显著差异且优于所有语言监督模型。模型内层级仍存在深度-时间对应（深层匹配晚期神经响应）。预测评分能力最高可达r=0.87，但该指标与神经对应度不相关；向神经几何方向重加权特征会降低所有模型的评分预测能力。

Conclusion: 预测街道场景评分对证明模型以大脑方式组织场景表征是弱证据。该基准仅使用公开数据且无需训练，评估新表征仅需55张图像的嵌入，可作为模型中间层表征-大脑一致性检验的标准工具。

Abstract: Pretrained vision embeddings are increasingly used as general-purpose representations for modelling how people appraise urban scenes, and are validated almost entirely by how well they predict human ratings. High predictive accuracy does not establish that these embeddings organise scenes as human perception does. We test the two properties separately against brain data. Using openly released EEG from 63 adults who viewed and rated 56 Berlin street scenes, we estimate the representational geometry of the scenes over time, the proportion of that geometry that is explainable at all, and its correspondence with seventeen feature spaces spanning language-supervised, self-supervised, category-supervised and dense-prediction training, two orders of magnitude of scale, and interpretable controls. Correspondence is low throughout: the best representation, DINOv2 ViT-B, reaches 29.6% of the lower bound of the noise ceiling, the panel spans 11.0% to 29.6%, and a Gabor energy descriptor is indistinguishable from the best model while outperforming every language-supervised model tested. Within a model, deeper layers still match later neural responses, so the hierarchical correspondence found for object recognition survives even at this low overall level. The same embeddings predict held-out appraisal ratings well, up to r = 0.87, and the two measures do not track each other across models; reweighting features towards the neural geometry lowers appraisal prediction for every model tested, against a control of matched dimensionality. Predicting how a street is appraised is therefore weak evidence that a model represents the street as the brain does. The benchmark uses only public data and requires no training, so evaluating a new representation needs only its embeddings for 55 images.

</details>


### [245] [MR-JEPA: A General Purpose Video Foundation Model for Cardiac MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30975)
*Athira J. Jacob,Puneet Sharma,Dorin Comaniciu,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MR-JEPA是一个面向心脏磁共振成像(CMR)的自监督视频基础模型，将LeJEPA扩展到3D时空输入，通过tubelet标记化、时空掩码增强和2D CMR基础模型初始化，在多序列(电影、LGE、mapping)数据上进行预训练，并在六个下游任务中超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMR深度学习方法大多处理单个2D切片，忽略了CMR数据中丰富的时序(电影)和空间(LGE/mapping)上下文信息；且此前CMR视频模型仅限于电影数据，无法利用多序列数据的互补信息。

Method: MR-JEPA扩展LeJEPA到3D时空输入，采用tubelet标记化技术、时空掩码增强策略，并利用2D CMR基础模型进行初始化。模型在10,505名患者、两个中心的多序列(cine, LGE, mapping)无标注数据上进行自监督预训练，然后使用统一的多视角门控注意力架构在六个下游任务上评估冻结编码器。

Result: 在全部五个回归任务上超越对比方法，LV EF MAE达4.79%(r=0.764)，GLS MAE为1.87(r=0.805)，在应变任务上相比基线的MAE降低21-27%；疾病检测方面macro AUC达到0.868，与特定领域基线竞争性相当。相比使用更多数据和文本监督的领域特定CMR模型以及自然视频基础模型均有优势。

Conclusion: MR-JEPA证明了统一视频编码器能够有效利用多样的CMR序列进行多视角临床心脏定量分析和诊断，展示了全自监督预训练目标在医学影像领域基础模型中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Cardiac magnetic resonance imaging (CMR) produces rich sequential data such as temporal cine videos and spatial LGE/mapping stacks, yet most deep learning approaches process individual 2D slices, discarding this context. We present MR-JEPA, a self-supervised video foundation model for CMR that extends LeJEPA to 3D spatiotemporal inputs through tubelet tokenization, spatiotemporal masking augmentation, and initialization from a 2D CMR foundation model. Unlike prior CMR video models limited to cine data, MR-JEPA is pretrained on multi-sequence data (cine, LGE, mapping) from 10,505 patients across two centers without annotations. We evaluate the frozen encoder on six downstream tasks using a unified multi-view gated attention architecture: LV ejection fraction, RV ejection fraction, three myocardial strains (GLS, GCS, GRS), and four-class disease detection. MR-JEPA outperforms other compared methods on all five regression tasks, including both a domain-specific CMR model pretrained on more data with text supervision and a natural-video foundation model, achieving an LV EF MAE of 4.79% (r =0.764) and a GLS MAE of 1.87 (r=0.805), with 21-27% MAE reductions over baselines on strain tasks. For disease detection, MR-JEPA achieved a macro AUG of 0.868, remaining competitive with the domain-specific baseline despite using a fully self-supervised pretraining objective. These results demonstrate the potential of a unified video encoder for robust, multi-view utilization of diverse CMR sequences in clinical cardiac quantification and diagnosis.

</details>


### [246] [Multi-View Reflective Surface Inspection via Semantic-Saliency Cross-Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30997)
*Van-Giang Nguyen,Thanh-Tuan Tran,Xuan-Hieu Phan,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多视角检测框架，用于反射性智能手机盖板玻璃的缺陷检测。每个RGB视角由共享的视角专家处理：VLM生成类别感知的语义框，法线参考重建分支生成类别无关的显著性图，两者空间一致性作为重排序语义提议的证据。在282张生产线图像上，AP50从52.6%提升至62.6%；在94个产品上，跨视角证据召回率从75.5%（最佳单视角）提升至88.3%（三视角融合）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射性智能手机盖板玻璃难以从单一固定视角检测缺陷，因为缺陷可见性随观察几何和镜面反射变化。这带来两个挑战：缺陷在某些视角下可见性弱，且可用的视觉证据存在空间歧义。现有方法无法同时解决弱可见性和空间不确定性问题。

Method: 提出多视角检测框架：(1) 每个RGB观测由共享的逐视角专家处理；(2) 视觉语言模型(VLM)生成类别感知的语义框；(3) 法线参考重建分支生成类别无关的显著性图；(4) 利用语义框与显著性图的空间一致性作为支持证据，对语义提议进行重排序（不修改坐标，也不将显著性视为真值）；(5) 在产品层面无跨视角配准地组合证据记录。

Result: 在282张生产线图像上，语义-显著性关联通过重排序固定的语义提议将AP50从52.6%提升至62.6%；在94个产品上，跨视角证据召回率R_prod@0.5从最佳单视角的75.5%提升至使用全部三个视角的88.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明语义-显著性交叉验证与多视角光学观测在反射表面检测中具有互补作用，两者结合可显著提升检测性能，证明了该框架在反射性表面检测领域的实用价值。

Abstract: Reflective smartphone cover glass is challenging to inspect from a single fixed viewpoint because defect visibility varies with viewing geometry and specular reflections. This gives rise to two practical challenges: defects may be weakly observable from certain viewpoints, while the available visual evidence may remain spatially ambiguous. To address these issues, we propose a multi-view inspection framework in which each RGB observation is processed by a shared per-view expert. A vision-language model (VLM) produces class-aware semantic boxes, while a normal-reference reconstruction branch provides class-agnostic saliency. Their spatial agreement is used as supporting evidence to rank semantic proposals without modifying their coordinates or treating saliency as ground truth. The resulting evidence records are combined at product level without cross-view registration. On 282 production-line images, semantic-saliency association improves $AP_{50}$ from 52.6% to 62.6% by re-ranking fixed semantic proposals. Across 94 products, cross-view evidence recall $R_{\rm prod}@0.5$ increases from 75.5% for the best single view to 88.3% using all three views. These results support the complementary roles of semantic-saliency cross-verification and additional optical observations in reflective-surface inspection.

</details>


### [247] [From Intent to Evidence: Policy-Steered Multi-Strategy Retrieval for Long-Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31005)
*Can Zhang,Baofeng Zhang,Xiaotian Han,Junyuan Shang,Yuchen Ding,Shuohuan Wang,Dianhai Yu,Ruirui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VESTA，一个无训练的长视频智能体，通过路由条件控制的"获取-验证-整合"循环，根据证据特性动态选择聚焦、回忆或对比检索策略，并配备时间证据账本管理系统。在Video-MME-v2上平均准确率提升2.7个百分点，在LongVideoBench长子集上提升6.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频智能体在所有问题上采用统一的证据获取行为，忽视了证据是集中分布、需要广泛覆盖还是需要区分竞争假设这一关键差异，导致在实质性推理开始之前就可能失败。而针对每个问题定制细粒度解决方案又限制了智能体的自主探索能力，因此需要一种更智能的路径选择机制。

Method: VESTA采用路由条件控制的获取-验证-整合三阶段循环。探索前，意图路由器推断证据获取策略（聚焦检索、回忆检索或对比检索，基于共享的视觉-语音场景索引）以及证据核算策略（配置探索期间维护的证据视图）。策略引导的检索产生临时参考，多模态证据操作将其转化为观测，而Reasoner可以自由验证这些观测、利用中间发现重新查询或检查检索集之外的区域。时间证据账本将观测整合为包含时间位置、来源、覆盖度、冲突、验证结果和假设支持度的自适应压缩视图，暴露缺失和未解决的证据以指导后续获取，最终优先使用已验证的观测。

Result: 在Video-MME-v2上，VESTA相比VideoARM将平均准确率提升2.7个百分点，并在所有六个报告指标上均获得提升。在LongVideoBench、EgoSchema和LVBench上使用共享查询时模型，在LongVideoBench长子集上提升6.9个百分点，在LVBench上提升1.5个百分点，并在EgoSchema上与VideoARM持平。

Conclusion: VESTA通过按需路由不同证据获取策略而非统一行为，有效解决了长视频智能体在证据获取阶段的瓶颈问题，同时在保持自主探索能力的前提下提升了多基准性能，验证了"策略路由+自适应证据管理"这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Existing long-video agents acquire evidence through one uniform behavior, ignoring whether the required evidence is concentrated, requires broad occurrence coverage, or must discriminate competing hypotheses---which can cause failure before substantive reasoning begins. Prescribing a fine-grained solution procedure for every question is not a satisfactory remedy, as it restricts autonomous exploration. We propose VESTA, a training-free long-video agent organized as a route-conditioned acquire--verify--consolidate loop. Before exploration, an intent router infers an evidence-acquisition policy---focused, recall, or contrastive retrieval over a shared visual--speech scene index---together with an evidence-accounting policy that configures the evidence view maintained during exploration. Policy-steered retrieval yields provisional references that multimodal evidence operations convert into observations, while the Reasoner remains free to verify them, re-query using intermediate findings, or inspect regions outside the retrieved set. A temporal evidence ledger consolidates observations into an adaptive, compressed view of temporal location, provenance, coverage, conflicts, verification outcomes, and hypothesis support, exposing missing and unresolved evidence to guide subsequent acquisition; finalization prioritizes verified observations. On Video-MME-v2, VESTA improves average accuracy by 2.7 points over VideoARM and gains across all six reported metrics. On LongVideoBench, EgoSchema, and LVBench under shared query-time models, it improves by 6.9 points on the LongVideoBench long subset and 1.5 on LVBench, and matches VideoARM on EgoSchema.

</details>


### [248] [SMG: Semantic Motion Graph for Monocular Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31023)
*Haozheng Yu,Xinyu Yang,Rundong Luo,Jennifer J. Sun,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义运动图（SMG）方法，利用语义一致性先验将动态高斯的运动建模为低秩语义运动，并引入新的多视图ego-exo数据集，在单目动态高斯溅射任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯溅射方法在单目视频场景下常过拟合训练视图，在遮挡或复杂场景运动下因缺少可靠的规则化信号而失效。

Method: 构建语义运动图（SMG），将高斯运动建模为低秩语义运动；高斯运动由SMG节点运动驱动；利用可靠图节点引导附近不可靠节点的运动，以缓解来自不可靠先验和弱约束区域带来的不确定性。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的真实世界基准上，SMG在单目动态高斯溅射任务上取得最先进性能；同时构建并发布了ego-exo多视图新数据集。

Conclusion: 利用语义一致性先验建模结构化运动，可有效约束欠约束区域的高斯运动，提升动态场景建模的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: We study dynamic Gaussian Splatting from monocular videos. While recent advancements in dynamic Gaussian splatting offer a promising foundation for modeling dynamic scenes, they often overfit to the training views and fail under occlusion or complex scene motion due to the lack of reliable regularization signals in under-constrained regions. We propose Semantic Motion Graph (SMG), a novel approach models the Gaussian motion as the low-rank semantic motion. Our key insight is that the real-world scene motion is often structured by semantic coherence: regions that are spatially close and semantically related tend to exhibit consistent dynamics. To leverage this prior, we construct SMG to model structured motion of the scene. The Gaussian motion is driven by the motion of SMG nodes. We further observe that the uncertainty of Gaussian motion arises from both unreliable off-the-shelf priors and weakly constrained regions during optimization. SMG addresses this by using reliable graph nodes to guide the motion of nearby unreliable nodes. To evaluate dynamic Gaussian splatting under challenging real-world scenarios, we introduce a new multiview dataset collected under an ego-exo setup. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SMG achieves state-of-the-art performance on monocular dynamic Gaussian splatting across challenging real-world benchmarks. Project page: https://smg-gaussian.github.io/.

</details>


### [249] [Analytic Dynamics: Learning Physics-Grounded Representation for Fast Intrinsic Dynamics Inference from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31025)
*Jailing Lin,Jikuan Zhang,Jianhua Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Analytic Dynamics，一种前馈式动力学推断框架，通过引入基于物理的中间动力学表示（位置、位移和变形梯度场），弥合视觉观测与内在物理动力学之间的鸿沟，实现从单目视频中高效、准确且可泛化的材料模型分类和参数回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测推断物体动力学是智能体理解物理世界的关键能力，但存在两大难题：现有方法要么依赖逐场景优化导致效率低下，要么直接将视觉输入映射到动力学参数而缺乏中间物理抽象，容易陷入外观和几何捷径，无法真正捕捉动力学相关特征。

Method: 提出Analytic Dynamics框架，在视觉观测与内在动力学之间引入基于物理的中间动力学表示。具体利用仿真中可获得的特权物理状态（位置、位移、变形梯度场），学习结构化动力学表示，并将视觉表示与此空间对齐，从而赋予视觉模型物理层面的归纳偏置，用于材料模型分类和参数回归。同时设计了包含配对物理状态轨迹、渲染视频和真实材料模型参数的动力学数据生成流水线与基准数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，Analytic Dynamics在单目视频上实现了高效、准确且可泛化的动力学推断，验证了引入物理接地中间表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将物理接地动力学表示作为视觉观测与内在参数之间的桥梁，可以在不牺牲效率的前提下提升动力学推断的准确性和泛化能力，为从视觉理解物理世界提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Inferring object dynamics from visual observations is essential for intelligent agents to reason about and interact with the physical world, yet remains challenging due to the fundamental gap between visual evidence and intrinsic dynamics. Existing methods either rely on costly per-scene optimization, limiting efficiency and scalability, or directly map visual evidence to intrinsic dynamics without intermediate physical abstractions, making them prone to appearance and geometry shortcuts. To bridge this gap, we propose Analytic Dynamics, a feed-forward dynamics inference framework that introduces an intermediate physics-grounded dynamics representation between visual observations and intrinsic dynamics. Specifically, we leverage privileged physical states, including position, displacement, and deformation gradient fields, which are available in simulation, to learn a structured dynamics representation that is difficult to discover from visual observations alone. By aligning visual representations with this space, we equip visual models with a physics-grounded inductive bias, guiding them to capture dynamics-relevant patterns for material model classification and parameter regression. To facilitate this research, we develop a dynamics data generation pipeline and benchmark containing paired physical state trajectories, rendered videos, and ground-truth material models and parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Analytic Dynamics achieves efficient, accurate, and generalizable dynamics inference from monocular videos.

</details>


### [250] [Driving on Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31029)
*Christian Löwens,Thorben Funke,Alexandru Paul Condurache*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoryDrivoR，通过将端到端自动驾驶模型的相机输入替换为同一位置的过往驾驶记忆，来探究当前驾驶基准测试（如NAVSIM）中高分是否真正反映模型对当前交通场景的动态反应能力。实验发现，在NAVSIM上，仅靠记忆几乎能达到甚至超越领先的端到端方法的性能，表明其高分并不需要模型实际观察和反应当前交通场景；而在Bench2Drive和RealEngine上，记忆驱动的性能大幅下降，说明该问题具有基准依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶基准（如NAVSIM和Bench2Drive）采用基于仿真的丰富指标来评估模型的安全与合规驾驶能力，但高基准分数到底有多少来源于模型对当前动态场景的反应，这一点尚不明确。本文旨在通过剥离动态视觉输入、仅提供静态场景记忆的方式，揭示基准分数与真实交通反应能力之间的关联。

Method: 作者移除模型的相机输入，将其替换为同一地点历史驾驶中检索到的记忆。这些记忆包含持久场景信息（如道路布局、位置相关的规律性），但不包含当前交通状态。通过对比记忆驱动与正常视觉输入下的性能，量化基准分数中动态场景反应所占的比例，并在三个基准（NAVSIM、Bench2Drive、RealEngine）上进行验证。

Result: 在NAVSIM上，记忆驱动模型几乎完全达到甚至超越了领先端到端方法的性能，说明该基准的高分并不要求规划器反应当前交通场景。相比之下，在Bench2Drive和RealEngine上，记忆驱动的性能显著下降，显示该效应具有基准依赖性。

Conclusion: NAVSIM基准的评估结果应谨慎对待，因为它无法有效区分"真正理解并反应场景"的模型与"仅凭场景记忆"即可得高分的模型。不同基准对此的敏感性差异显著，提示未来驾驶基准设计应引入能有效抑制记忆捷径的评估方式。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models plan future trajectories from raw sensor input. While earlier driving benchmarks often measured deviation from the human trajectory, current benchmarks such as NAVSIM and Bench2Drive evaluate models with richer simulation-based metrics intended to capture safe and compliant driving. A high benchmark score should reflect that a model can understand the scene in front of it and act accordingly. But how much of that score specifically comes from reacting to the dynamic part of that scene?
  To probe this, we remove a model's camera input and replace it with memories from prior drives at the same location. The retrieved memories can provide persistent scene information, including road layout and location-conditioned regularities, but not the current traffic state. Surprisingly, memory is nearly sufficient on NAVSIM, reaching or even exceeding the performance of leading end-to-end methods without actually observing the evaluated scene. Our results suggest that a high NAVSIM score does not require a planner to react to the current traffic scene and should be treated with caution. This effect is benchmark-dependent: driving from memory causes substantially larger performance drops on Bench2Drive and RealEngine. We provide our code at https://github.com/boschresearch/MemoryDrivoR .

</details>


### [251] [FaceSnap: Real-Time Personalized Lightstage Facial Performance Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31033)
*Rukhshanda Hussain,Noé Artru,Emeline Got,Luiz Gustavo Hafemann,Alexandre Messier,Brandon Dearlove,Rafael M. O. Cruz,Abdallah Dib,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceSnap是一个端到端框架，通过两阶段方法将光舞台面部捕捉从多视角、资源密集型流程简化为单相机实时捕捉：先用一次性多视角优化构建个性化模型（编码几何和表情相关外观），再仅靠单目光舞台相机实现83fps的高保真实时面部性能捕捉，同时联合估计几何和动态4K纹理；此外还发布了Multi4D基准用于光舞台环境下4D面部重建的拓扑不变几何比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光舞台面部捕捉能产出制作级数字人，但资源密集且劳动强度大：多相机设置、数小时计算和大规模数据存储造成瓶颈，阻碍迭代工作流程。需要一种更高效的捕捉方案来简化流程同时保持高质量输出。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：(1) 离线一次性多视角优化——从range-of-motion序列构建个性化模型，编码几何和表情依赖外观；(2) 在线实时捕捉——利用该个性化模型，仅用单目光舞台相机即可进行高保真实时面部性能捕捉，无需额外的多视角捕捉。其中4K纹理由一个新颖的个性化残差上采样器生成，能够恢复通用上采样器无法捕捉的受试者特定高频细节。同时发布Multi4D基准，支持拓扑不变几何比较。

Result: FaceSnap在83fps下联合估计几何和动态4K纹理，几何精度与全帧多视角优化相当，同时优于基于生产级3D数据训练的feed-forward方法，且仅需单相机视角即可实现。

Conclusion: FaceSnap证明了通过个性化先验模型，可以将光舞台面部捕捉从重资源的多视角流程转化为轻量级的单相机实时捕捉，同时保持与全量优化相当的几何质量和恢复4K高频外观细节，为迭代式数字人制作提供了高效路径。

Abstract: Lightstage facial capture produces production-quality digital humans, but it is resource and labor-intensive. Multi-camera setups, hours of computation, and massive data storage create bottlenecks that hinder iterative workflows. This paper introduces FaceSnap, an end-to-end framework that streamlines capture via a two-stage approach. First, a one-time multi-view optimization from a range-of-motion sequence builds a personalized model encoding both geometry and expression-dependent appearance. This model then enables high-fidelity real-time facial performance capture from a single monocular lightstage camera, with no further multi-view capture required. FaceSnap jointly estimates geometry and dynamic 4K texture at 83 fps. The 4K texture is produced by a novel personalized residual upscaler that recovers subject-specific high-frequency detail, which generic upscalers fail to capture. FaceSnap achieves geometric accuracy competitive with full per-frame multi-view optimization while outperforming feed-forward methods trained on production-quality 3D data, all from a single camera view. Finally, we introduce Multi4D, a public benchmark for evaluating 4D facial reconstruction methods in lightstage environments, enabling topology-invariant geometric comparison across methods.

</details>


### [252] [Segmentation of Bovid Dentition Under Imperfect Annotations: A Comparative Study of Convolutional and Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31052)
*Keith G. Mills,Evan B. Sanders,Gregory J. Matthews,Juliet K. Brophy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对多种语义分割架构（从卷积骨干网络到视觉Transformer）在B.O.V.I.D.数据集上进行了比较研究，该数据集包含高分辨率牛科动物牙齿照片及手工分割掩码。研究发现，预处理和对齐技术对Dice分数和mIoU等定量指标影响有限，但对预测掩码的定性质量有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割DNN依赖精心设计的掩码目标进行学习，而B.O.V.I.D.数据集中的手工掩码并非为ML训练设计，存在不完美或对齐误差，可能干扰模型的有效学习。作者旨在探究如何通过预处理和对齐技术缓解标签缺陷带来的影响。

Method: 对多种分割架构进行比较研究，涵盖从卷积骨干网络到视觉Transformer的不同模型，并结合一系列预处理和对齐技术来缓解标签不完美的问题，在B.O.V.I.D.数据集上系统评估其定量（Dice、mIoU）和定性效果。

Result: 预处理和对齐技术对定量指标（Dice分数、mIoU）的影响有限，但在预测掩码的定性表现上产生了显著的改善。

Conclusion: 定量指标可能无法充分反映分割模型在标签不完美数据集上的实际表现，定性评估对于理解模型输出质量至关重要；预处理策略的选择应综合考虑其对视觉效果的影响而不应只关注数值指标。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation decomposes an image into distinct mask regions corresponding to different object categories, such as people, cars, signs or buildings. Advances in machine learning (ML) have shifted this task away from traditional rule-based heuristics such as edge detection, towards deep neural networks (DNN) that learn to classify pixels directly. However, semantic segmentation DNNs crucially depend on expertly designed mask targets to learn from, and imperfect or misaligned masks can interfere with a model's ability to learn effectively.
  This paper presents a comparative study of segmentation architectures, ranging from convolutional backbones to vision transformers, applied to the B.O.V.I.D. dataset, a corpus of high-resolution bovid dental photographs paired with hand-made segmentation masks not originally designed for ML-based training. We evaluate a range of preprocessing and alignment techniques to mitigate the resulting label imperfections. We find that while these preprocessing choices have limited effect on quantitative metrics such as Dice score and mIoU, their qualitative impact on predicted masks is substantial.

</details>


### [253] [Identity-Conditioned Latent Consistency Distillation for Face Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31053)
*Tiago Kienen Chaves,Bernardo Biesseck,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过潜在一致性模型（Latent Consistency Model）对Arc2Face扩散模型进行知识蒸馏，将身份条件人脸合成从2.102秒/图加速至0.4819秒/图（4.36倍加速），同时保持与教师模型相当或更优的图像质量（FID分数在CelebA、WebFace42M等基准上接近或超越），适用于大规模合成人脸识别数据集生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽在高保真图像合成上表现出色，但其迭代采样过程计算成本高昂。在合成人脸识别数据集场景中，需要生成大量不同姿态、表情、年龄的受试者样本，计算开销问题尤为突出。因此需要一种在不牺牲图像质量的前提下显著降低身份条件人脸合成计算成本的方法。

Method: 提取基础扩散模型Arc2Face（教师模型）的知识，将其原始的文本到图像管线适配为嵌入到人脸（embedding-to-face）的设置，用ArcFace身份嵌入替代文本提示。通过任务特定的知识蒸馏训练一个潜在一致性模型（学生模型），使其能在少量迭代步骤内完成身份条件人脸图像的生成。

Result: 蒸馏模型平均推理时间为0.4819秒/图，相比Arc2Face的2.102秒/图实现4.36倍加速。FID评估显示：CelebA上几乎持平（13.921 vs. 12.928），WebFace42M上超越教师模型（9.317 vs. 9.802）。Synth-500上存在适度性能差距，AgeDB上结果相当。

Conclusion: 通过任务特定的潜在一致性蒸馏可以有效加速Arc2Face，在保持高质量人脸生成的同时大幅降低计算成本，为大规模合成人脸数据集的生成提供了切实可行的加速方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved strong results in high-fidelity image synthesis, but their iterative sampling process makes large-scale generation computationally expensive. This limitation is especially relevant when generating synthetic face datasets for face recognition, where a large number of subjects with many samples in different poses, expressions, ages, etc., are required. In this work, we show that identity-conditioned face synthesis can be performed at a substantially lower computational cost by a latent Consistency Model with few iterations, without compromising image quality. For training, we distill knowledge from the foundation Diffusion Model Arc2Face (teacher) by adapting its original text-to-image pipeline to an embedding-to-face setting, replacing textual prompts with ArcFace identity embeddings. Our distilled model (student) generates identity-conditioned face images with an average inference time of 0.4819 seconds per image, compared with 2.102 seconds for Arc2Face, resulting in a 4.36$\times$ speed-up. Quantitative results, based on FID scores, show that the distilled model remains competitive with Arc2Face across all evaluation protocols. On 100k generated images, it achieves near-parity on CelebA (13.921 vs. 12.928) and outperforms the teacher on WebFace42M (9.317 vs. 9.802). Further evaluations on Synth-500 and AgeDB show a moderate performance gap for the former but comparable results for the latter. These results indicate that Arc2Face can be accelerated through task-specific latent consistency distillation while preserving high image quality for large-scale synthetic face generation. Our proposal is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASP-PR/FaceRec-IdentityConsistency.

</details>


### [254] [Multimodal Shared Latent Representation of Narration, Microscope and iOCT Images for Phase Recognition in Vitreoretinal Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31065)
*Onur Izmitlioglu,Shervin Dehghani,Tarek Ghannoum,Benedikt Schworm,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的跨模态框架，以显微镜视图为共享锚点，将手术叙述与术中OCT（iOCT）在共享潜空间中对齐，用于玻璃体视网膜手术的阶段识别，无需完全同步的三模态数据集。该框架利用真实显微镜-叙述视频和合成同步数据集，通过对比对齐将结构先验从合成域迁移到真实视频，并采用双头MS-TCN++进行宏观和微观阶段联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃体视网膜手术中，外科医生天然整合多种术中模态信息（显微镜视图、iOCT）以进行决策，但同步的多模态术中数据稀缺，限制了模仿这种多模态整合的方法。相比之下，手术叙述在线上丰富易得，提供了密集的语义监督。先前的对比学习工作主要探索两两配对（如iOCT-显微镜或显微镜-叙述），缺乏三模态联合建模。

Method: 提出一个以显微镜视图作为共享锚点的三模态对齐框架：(1) 利用真实显微镜-叙述视频和合成同步（显微镜+iOCT）数据集，通过对比对齐将合成域的结构先验迁移到缺乏iOCT的真实视频中；(2) 采用双头MS-TCN++架构整合嵌入特征，联合预测宏观阶段（手术步骤）和微观阶段（细粒度器械-组织交互测量）。

Result: 在真实玻璃体视网膜手术上评估，宏观阶段识别的平均F1分数从零样本基线的0.38提升到0.53；微观阶段估计在合成数据上得到定量验证，在真实手术中仅作定性展示。

Conclusion: 这是首个将显微镜视图、iOCT B-scan和手术叙述统一到共享潜空间用于手术阶段识别的工作，展示了在缺乏完全同步三模态数据条件下进行跨模态学习以增强手术理解和反馈的可行路径。

Abstract: Surgical phase recognition is key to context-aware computer-assisted feedback in vitreoretinal procedures, yet the scarcity of synchronized multimodal intraoperative data, particularly microscope views and intraoperative OCT, limits approaches that aim to replicate the multimodal integration surgeons perform naturally. Surgical narration, by contrast, is abundantly available online and offers rich semantic supervision. Prior work has mainly explored pairwise contrastive learning (e.g., intraoperative OCT-microscope or microscope-narration), leaving the joint modeling of all three modalities largely unexplored. We introduce a framework that uses microscope views as a shared anchor to bridge surgical narrations and intraoperative OCT (iOCT) without requiring a fully synchronized tri-modal dataset, leveraging real microscope-narration videos and a synthetic dataset of synchronized microscope video and tool-aligned iOCT pairs. Contrastive alignment transfers structural priors from the synthetic domain to real videos lacking iOCT, and a dual-head MS-TCN++ integrates the resulting embeddings for joint macro- and micro-phase prediction. Evaluated on real vitreoretinal surgeries, our framework improves macro-phase recognition over a zero-shot baseline (mean F1 0.38 to 0.53) and provides an exploratory route to estimating fine-grained instrument-tissue measurements that are not directly observable in real microscope video alone; these micro-phase estimates are validated quantitatively on synthetic data and shown only qualitatively on real surgery. To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify microscope view, iOCT B-scans, and surgical narrations in a shared latent space for surgical phase recognition.

</details>


### [255] [Robust retinal biometrics for patient identity verification and retrieval across age and imaging devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31094)
*Jose D. Vargas-Quiros,Dennis Bontempi,Jeroen Vermeulen,Bart Liefers,Sven Bergmann,Caroline C. W. Klaver*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于彩色眼底图像的视网膜生物特征系统，用于验证患者身份和检索正确身份。系统在包含227,004张图像、21,851个患者-眼别身份的鹿特丹研究数据集上训练（结合ConvNeXtV2主干、ArcFace和三元组损失的512维度量学习编码器），并在鹿特丹研究、英国生物银行和AREDS三个数据集上验证，在身份验证任务中AUROC达到0.9997-0.9998，身份检索Recall@1达到97.2%-99.7%，证明了视网膜解剖结构可作为长期稳定的生物特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者身份错误可能损害纵向医疗记录、研究数据库和下游临床决策，但目前缺乏有效的自动验证手段。作者旨在利用视网膜解剖结构作为稳定的生物特征信号，开发一个能从彩色眼底图像中验证声明的身份并检索正确身份的系统，以保障纵向影像记录的完整性。

Method: 该方法使用ConvNeXtV2作为主干网络，结合ArcFace和三元组损失训练一个512维度量学习编码器。模型在鹿特丹研究的227,004张图像（来自21,851个患者-眼别身份，涵盖多种成像设备及长达32.6年的随访）上训练。评估流程包括：先用模型筛除身份不一致的图像进行人工核查修正，然后在去除近重复图像后执行回顾性验证和基于先前图像的检索任务。

Result: 系统在身份验证中AUROC分别达到0.9998（鹿特丹研究）、0.9997（UK Biobank）和0.9998（AREDS）。身份检索中Recall@1为99.7%、97.2%和97.6%，正确身份进入前五结果的比率至少为98.6%。此外，筛查过程中识别出鹿特丹研究0.588%、UK Biobank 0.259%、AREDS 0.164%的图像存在身份分配错误，性能在多种设备和长随访间隔下保持稳健，主要失败原因为图像质量低和视网膜视野不一致。

Conclusion: 视网膜解剖结构是一种持久、可靠的生物特征信号，该生物特征系统能够有效保障纵向影像记录的身份完整性，对临床数据质量管理和大规模研究数据库的准确性维护具有重要价值。

Abstract: Patient identity errors can compromise longitudinal medical records, research databases, and downstream clinical decisions. We present a retinal biometric system for verifying claimed identities and retrieving the correct identity from color fundus images. We trained a 512-dimensional metric-learning encoder combining a ConvNeXtV2 backbone with ArcFace and triplet losses on 227,004 images from 21,851 patient-eye identities in the Rotterdam Study, spanning multiple imaging devices and up to 32.6 years of follow-up. The system was evaluated on held-out Rotterdam Study data and externally on the UK Biobank and Age-Related Eye Disease Study (AREDS). Before evaluation, we used the model to screen for identity inconsistencies and manually adjudicated flagged images, identifying incorrect assignments in 0.588% of Rotterdam Study images, 0.259% of UK Biobank images, and 0.164% of AREDS images. In retrospective-only verification after removing near-duplicate images, the system achieved AUROCs of 0.9998, 0.9997, and 0.9998 in the Rotterdam Study, UK Biobank, and AREDS, respectively. For identity retrieval using only previously acquired images, Recall@1 was 99.7%, 97.2%, and 97.6%, respectively, from galleries averaging 4436-8510 identities; the correct identity appeared among the top five results in at least 98.6% of cases. Performance remained robust across imaging devices and long follow-up intervals, while lower image quality and inconsistent retinal fields accounted for most failures. These findings establish retinal anatomy as a durable biometric signal, useful for safeguarding the integrity of longitudinal imaging records.

</details>


### [256] [One Adapter, Many Tasks: Task-Conditioned Feature Transformations for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31096)
*Yunxiang Fu,Meng Lou,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FACET是一种用于类增量学习的方法，通过任务条件化特征变换和任务条件化特征一致性损失，在仅使用单一共享适配器的情况下实现高效的参数利用和判别性特征表示，在长任务序列（200个任务）和短任务序列（20个任务）上均取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练模型的类增量学习方法存在局限性：任务特定适配器学习显式的每任务表征但参数和计算效率低；基于LoRA合并的方法将每任务LoRA参数合并为单一模型，其静态聚合权重在推理过程中会造成表示干扰。这两种方法均无法同时兼顾参数效率和特征判别性。

Method: FACET提出两个核心组件：(1) 动态任务条件化特征变换——学习单一共享适配器，通过任务条件将适配器的整体特征分布塑造成多个重叠减少的任务特定组件的混合分布；(2) 免回放的任务条件化特征一致性损失——用于缓解适配器特征空间中学习到的混合分布的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在非常长的任务序列（如200个任务）和标准短任务序列（如20个任务）上，FACET在显著减少可训练参数和GFLOPs的情况下，取得了优越的性能表现。

Conclusion: FACET通过动态任务条件化特征变换结合特征一致性约束，在保持单一适配器的前提下同时解决了参数效率低和静态权重导致的表示干扰问题，展现出良好的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning (CIL) requires a model to incrementally learn tasks that contain new classes without accessing earlier training data while preserving the ability to recognize all seen classes. Recently, pretrained-model-based approaches have become prevalent by adapting a frozen backbone with additional lightweight trainable modules. Existing methods, however, exhibit limitations: task-specific adapters learn explicit per-task representations but are parameter- and computation-inefficient, while LoRA-based merging methods combine per-task LoRA parameters into a single model whose static aggregated weights cause representation interference during inference. To address these problems, we present \textbf{FACET}: task-conditioned \textbf{F}e\textbf{A}ture transformation with \textbf{C}ondition\textbf{E}d feature consis\textbf{T}ency, achieving excellent parameter efficiency while producing highly discriminative features during inference. When continually trained on a task sequence, FACET learns a single shared adapter that employs a dynamic task-conditioned feature transformation, shaping the overall feature distribution of the adapter into a mixture of overlap-reduced task-specific components. On the other hand, we propose an efficient replay-free task-conditioned feature consistency loss, aiming to mitigate catastrophic forgetting of the learned mixture distribution in the adapter's feature space. Even when maintaining only a single adapter, FACET demonstrates robust scalability. On both very long task sequences (e.g., 200 tasks) and standard short task sequences (e.g., 20 tasks), our method achieves superior performance while using significantly fewer trainable parameters and GFLOPs. The code will be made open source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [257] [DreamX-Creator: Democratizing Native Audio-Video Generation at 2K Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31106)
*Jiashu Zhu,Yanhao Zheng,Ruitian Tian,Rujing Dang,Shen Zhang,Bingze Song,Jiachen Lei,Ruimin Lin,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX-Creator 1.0是一个紧凑的原生联合音视频生成系统，基于7B参数的生成器，通过门控跨模态注意力、统一音视频数据系统、渐进式联合训练、音视频强化学习以及自回归1步2K精炼流水线，实现了从静态首帧和文本提示同步生成高分辨率、音画同步的音视频内容，性能与最先进的开源系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器通常省略音频或在独立阶段合成音频，这限制了视觉动态与声学事件之间的交互建模能力，无法实现真正的原生同步音视频生成。

Method: 方法包括四个核心组件：(1) 基于7B生成器的原生联合去噪架构，前半网络独立处理音视频流、后半通过门控跨模态注意力耦合；(2) 统一音视频数据系统，构建过滤时间连贯片段并生成结构化多模态标注；(3) 渐进式联合训练，包含两个音视频预训练阶段和高品质微调；(4) 音视频强化学习后训练，采用模态感知多模态反馈；(5) 自回归1步2K精炼流水线，将双向多步教师蒸馏为单步学生模型。

Result: DreamX-Creator 1.0实现了原生、同步的音视频生成，性能与最先进的开源系统相当，且仅需7B参数和单步去噪评估即可实现2K高分辨率输出。

Conclusion: 通过开源紧凑的7B生成器和2K精炼器，该工作旨在推动原生音视频生成的民主化，为统一音视频生成建模的未来研究提供可访问的基础。

Abstract: Recent video generators often omit audio or synthesize it in a separate stage, limiting reciprocal modeling of visual dynamics and acoustic events. We present DreamX-Creator 1.0, a compact native joint audio-video generation system centered on a 7B generator. Conditioned on a first frame and a text prompt, the generator jointly denoises modality-specialized audio and video streams. The streams are processed independently in the first half of the network and coupled in the latter half through Gated Cross-Modal Attention, whose token- and head-wise output gates modulate each active cross-modal attention-head output. A unified Audio-Video Data System constructs and filters temporally coherent clips, produces structured multimodal annotations, and organizes clips into capability-oriented data pools. Progressive Joint Training comprises two audio-video pre-training stages followed by High-Quality Finetuning. Audio-Video Reinforcement Learning further post-trains the generator with Modality-Aware Multimodal Feedback that routes video-, audio-, and cross-modal feedback to the corresponding streams. For high-resolution output, our Autoregressive 1-Step 2K Refinement pipeline adapts a bidirectional multi-step teacher into an autoregressive multi-step refiner and distills it into a student requiring one denoising evaluation per temporal chunk. Overall, DreamX-Creator 1.0 achieves native, synchronized audio-video generation with performance competitive with state-of-the-art open-source systems. By releasing our compact 7B generator and 2K Refiner, we seek to democratize native audio-video generation and provide an accessible foundation for future research in unified audio-video generative modeling.

</details>


### [258] [VeriCam: A Verification Baseline for the Classification of Unknown Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31107)
*Lucas Wojcik,Gabriel E. Lima,Sergio M. Silva,Eduil Nascimento,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriCam，一个利用图像验证模型学习细粒度特征的流水线，通过构建关系图和图聚类实现未知类别的零样本分类，并在LPLCv2车牌数据集上验证，F1-score达93.45，V-Measure达80.13。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽在零样本分类中表现出色，但无论是图像、文本还是视觉-文本混合模型，都缺乏真实世界任务所需的细粒度、基于微小特征（minutiae）的类别分离能力。现有方法无法解决需要高度专业化特征区分未知类别的挑战。

Method: VeriCam流水线包含三个关键步骤：(1) 利用针对验证任务（判别图像对是否属于同一类别）训练的模型，构建包含细粒度细节的复杂特征空间；(2) 基于模型对图像对同/异类别的判别结果，构建表示数据点间类别关系的关系图；(3) 提出两种图聚类方法：朴素算法和专门适配的Leiden图聚类算法。此外，通过标签无关的方法动态识别采集设备，构建无偏基准。

Result: 在LPLCv2真实道路交通监控数据集上，跨设备场景下验证基线达到93.45的F1-Score，聚类步骤达到80.13的V-Measure分数。同时发现数据集存在固有的采集设备偏差，该偏差对下游车牌识别（如OCR）构成泛化挑战。

Conclusion: VeriCam通过融合验证任务的特征表示与图聚类技术，有效解决了基础模型在细粒度零样本分类中的不足，并在真实世界车牌识别场景中得到验证，代码已开源。

Abstract: The advent of foundation models have enabled a new era in zero-shot classification. Yet, key challenges persist. Despite their impressive generalization power that leverages the immense pre-training knowledge, both foundation models for image and text as well as vision-text hybrids lack the representational power needed for fine-grained, minutiae-based class separation that some real-world tasks require. To address the current gaps in the literature, we propose VeriCam, a pipeline designed to learn highly specialized features that enable classification of unknown classes in unseen data. VeriCam works by leveraging the representation power of image models trained for the verification task, where the model develops an intricate feature space that incorporates fine-grained details. By training a model to discriminate between pairs of images from the same and different classes, a relational graph is constructed, representing the class relationships between data points. We then present two approaches for graph clustering: a naive algorithm and a specific setup for the Leiden graph clustering algorithm. The pipeline is validated on the LPLCv2 dataset, which comprises real-world traffic surveillance images. We show that the dataset carries an inherent capture device bias that is posed as a generalization challenge for downstream License Plate recognition tasks such as OCR. As such, we dynamically identify capture devices with a label-agnostic approach, enabling the construction of a fair and unbiased benchmark. In the cross-device scenario, our pipeline reaches an F1-Score of 93.45 in the verification baseline and a V-Measure score of 80.13 in the clustering step. All code is publicly available at https://github.com/lmlwojcik/VeriCam

</details>


### [259] [BLARM: Animating 3D Objects from Video via Blending Latent Rigid Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31113)
*Pradyumn Goyal,Yizhak Ben-Shabat,Hsueh-Ti Derek Liu,Haomiao Jiang,Snehasish Mukherjee,Kyle Spence,Mark Stauber,Evangelos Kalogerakis,Yunze Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BLARM是一种前馈式视频驱动3D网格动画方法，通过学习紧凑的时变刚性运动分量和时不变的顶点-组件蒙皮权重来表示动画，无需显式骨架、笼子或蒙皮标注，即可从单目视频生成时间连贯的动画网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频驱动的3D网格动画方法要么依赖显式绑定（rigs）或骨架，需要昂贵的标注；要么直接回归高维顶点运动，导致变形空间过大且难以保证时间一致性。作者希望提出一种无需任何显式动画结构标注、同时能学习紧凑可解释运动表示的方法。

Method: BLARM采用前馈架构：1）将动画表示为一组可学习的时变刚性运动分量和时间不变的顶点-组件蒙皮权重，形成低维变形空间；2）通过因子化时空注意力，以视频特征为条件调制几何导出的变形潜码；3）解码刚性变换并通过预测的蒙皮权重进行混合得到最终动画。训练使用轨迹重建损失、熵正则化和运动感知对比学习。

Result: BLARM能够从单目视频中生成准确且时间稳定的动画，同时恢复紧凑、可解释的运动结构，无需任何显式绑定、骨架或蒙皮标注。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一种无需显式动画先验结构的表示方法可以有效捕获视频驱动的3D网格动画中的运动，兼顾了重建精度、时间一致性和运动结构的可解释性。

Abstract: We introduce BLARM, a feed-forward method for video-driven 3D mesh animation. Given a monocular video and a static object mesh, BLARM predicts a temporally coherent animated mesh whose motion follows the video. Rather than relying on explicit rigs or directly regressing high-dimensional vertex motion, we represent animation using a compact set of learned, time-varying rigid motion components and time-invariant vertex-to-component skinning weights. This yields a low-dimensional deformation space without requiring skeletons, cages, skinning weights, or rig annotations. Our architecture conditions geometry-derived deformation latents on video features through factorized spatial-temporal attention, then decodes rigid transformations blended by predicted skinning weights. Trained with trajectory reconstruction, entropy regularization, and motion-aware contrastive learning, BLARM produces accurate and temporally stable animations while recovering compact, interpretable motion structure from monocular video.

</details>


### [260] [BRF-GS: Hyperspectral Bidirectional Reflectance Factor Modeling and Image Generation Based on 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31159)
*Yiling Yao,Wenjuan Zhang,Bowen Wang,Bocheng Li,Wentao Song,Bing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BRF-GS，一种基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的框架，用于地表双向反射因子（BRF）建模和多角度高光谱反射率图像生成。该框架通过混合BRDF驱动核表示复杂方向反射，选取几何可靠的光谱波段进行稳健初始化，并采用两阶段训练策略解耦几何优化与光谱建模。同时构建了AIR-BRF数据集（含三个场景的多角度高光谱方向反射数据），实验验证了其在空间和光谱保真度上的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维辐射传输模型需要复杂的场景构建和计算密集的求解器，难以高效生成多角度高光谱反射率图像；而3DGS虽然提供高效的神经场景表示和新视角合成，但其低阶球谐函数表示不足以刻画复杂的定向反射特性，且高光谱数据的高维性和波段间质量差异带来额外挑战。

Method: BRF-GS框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 引入混合BRDF驱动核来表示复杂的方向反射； (2) 选取几何可靠的光谱波段进行稳健的3D场景初始化； (3) 采用两阶段训练策略，将几何优化与光谱建模解耦。此外构建了AIR-BRF多角度高光谱定向反射数据集。

Result: 实验表明BRF-GS在空间和光谱保真度上均优于现有方法，并能准确再现具有视角依赖特性的BRF响应。

Conclusion: BRF-GS为遥感场景中的BRF建模和多角度高光谱反射率图像生成提供了一种高效的数据驱动方法。

Abstract: The bidirectional reflectance factor (BRF) characterizes the directional radiative properties of terrestrial surfaces. However, existing three-dimensional (3D) radiative transfer models require complex scene construction and computationally intensive radiative transfer solvers, limiting efficient generation of multi-angle hyperspectral reflectance imagery. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers an efficient framework for neural scene representation and novel view synthesis, but its low-order spherical harmonics representation is insufficient for complex directional reflectance, while the high dimensionality and inter-band quality differences of hyperspectral data introduce additional challenges. To address these challenges, we propose BRF-GS, a 3DGS-based framework for BRF modeling and hyperspectral reflectance image generation. BRF-GS introduces a hybrid BRDF-driven kernel to represent complex directional reflectance, selects geometry-reliable spectral bands for robust 3D scene initialization, and adopts a two-stage training strategy that decouples geometry optimization from spectral modeling. We further construct the AIR-BRF dataset, a multi-angle hyperspectral directional reflectance dataset comprising three scenes with diverse natural and artificial targets. Experiments demonstrate that BRF-GS achieves superior spatial and spectral fidelity and accurately reproduces characteristic view-dependent BRF responses. The proposed framework provides an efficient data-driven approach for BRF modeling and multi-angle hyperspectral reflectance image generation in remote sensing scenes.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Is Deformable Image Registration Ready for Brain Metastasis Reirradiation Dose Accumulation? A Longitudinal MRI Benchmark of Registration Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28705)
*Hengjie Liu,Manju Sharma,Xinyi Fu,Di Xu,Ke Sheng*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文针对脑转移瘤再次立体定向放射外科治疗中的剂量累积场景，系统性评测了基于学习和基于优化的多种可变形图像配准（DIR）方法在87对纵向增强T1加权MRI病灶上的性能，发现优化方法仍优于预训练学习方法（即使经实例或靶点优化），目前最先进DIR方法尚不足以支持无人监督的临床剂量累积应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剂量累积在自适应放疗和再照射中日益重要，但其临床有效性取决于DIR性能。脑转移瘤SRS再照射提供了一个受控但临床相关的DIR测试场景：刚性配准后脑内形变通常有限，但复发灶可发生较大局部形状和体积变化，刚性配准无法捕捉，会影响剂量累积准确性。需要系统评估当前DIR方法在该临床场景中的真实表现。

Method: 在87对人工筛选的纵向增强T1加权MRI脑转移瘤病灶对上，对多种基于学习和基于优化的DIR方法进行基准测试。学习方法在健康脑MRI上预训练，分别以零样本、实例特定优化（ISO）和肿瘤邻近靶点特定优化（TSO）三种模式评估。采用病灶重叠（Dice）、表面距离指标（HD95和sASD）、靶区体积恢复、运行时间和内存占用进行评估。

Result: 预训练学习方法零样本表现差异较大；ISO/TSO优化能提升所有测试的学习方法家族的配准性能。但基于优化的方法仍为最佳方案，同时保持合理运行时间。即使最先进的DIR方法也尚未达到可无人监督使用于脑转移瘤再照射剂量累积的足够准确性和一致性水平。

Conclusion: 由于准确配准是形变剂量累积的前提，临床应用中需要病例层面的质量控制，并直接评估配准不确定性对下游剂量指标的影响。

Abstract: Dose accumulation is increasingly important in adaptive radiation therapy and reirradiation, but its clinical validity depends on the performance of deformable image registration (DIR). Reirradiation of brain metastases (BMs) with stereotactic radiosurgery (SRS) provides a controlled but clinically meaningful DIR test case: intra-subject brain deformation is usually limited after rigid alignment, yet recurrent lesions can undergo substantial local shape and volume changes that rigid registration cannot capture and can affect dose accumulation. We benchmarked a wide range of learning-based and optimization-based DIR methods on 87 manually screened longitudinal contrast-enhanced T1-weighted MRI lesion pairs from an institutional BM SRS retreatment cohort. Learning-based methods pretrained on healthy-brain MRI were evaluated zero-shot and after instance-specific optimization (ISO) or tumor-proximity target-specific optimization (TSO). Registration was assessed using lesion overlap (Dice), surface distance metrics (HD95 and sASD), target-volume recovery, and runtime and memory. Pretrained learning-based methods showed variable zero-shot performance, while ISO/TSO improved all tested learning-based families. However, optimization-based methods remained the best-performing approach while maintaining reasonable runtime. These findings suggest that even state-of-the-art DIR methods do not yet provide sufficiently accurate and consistent registration for unmonitored use in brain metastasis reirradiation dose accumulation. Because accurate registration is a prerequisite for deformable dose accumulation, clinical application will require case-level quality control and direct assessment of how registration uncertainty affects downstream dose metrics.

</details>


### [262] [Enhancing MRI Brain Tumor Edge Detection: A Hybrid Preprocessing Approach Utilizing CLAHE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28709)
*Shahid-E-Kaiser Md. Tashrif,Munshi Md Arafat Hussain,Sheikh Nahian,Sumaiya Islam*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合自动边缘检测管道，通过优化CLAHE层配合形态学预处理框架及确定性顺序参数扫描实现全自动阈值选择，在脑肿瘤MRI边界检测中提升了Recall、F1分数和SSIM，同时保持高效执行，可作为深度学习方法的轻量替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤MRI中的精确边界描绘因扫描噪声、复杂解剖结构和光照不均而极具挑战性。传统边缘检测算法依赖全局预处理和手动参数调整，难以捕捉水肿的弥散性、局部化边界，需要更智能的自适应方法。

Method: 提出混合自动边缘检测管道：(1)将优化配置的对比度受限自适应直方图均衡化(CLAHE)层集成到综合形态学预处理框架中；(2)采用确定性顺序参数扫描实现阈值的全自动选择，无需人工干预。

Result: 在Kaggle公开基准数据集上，该方法有效增强关键解剖结构的检测，获得更高的召回率（灵敏度）和F1分数，同时SSIM也得到提升，并且保持了极高的执行效率，接近实时运行。

Conclusion: 该优化管道是一种高度实用、近实时的临床诊断模型，为计算密集型的深度学习方法提供了极具竞争力的轻量级替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate boundary delineation of brain tumors in Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is a critical yet formidable challenge in neuro-oncology due to inherent scanner noise, complex anatomical structures, and uneven illumination. Traditional edge detection algorithms, while computationally lightweight and mathematically interpretable, frequently fail to capture the diffuse, localized boundaries of edema when relying solely on global preprocessing and manual parameter tuning. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid automated edge detection pipeline. Our approach integrates an optimally configured Contrast-Limited Adaptive Histogram Equalization (CLAHE) layer into a comprehensive morphological preprocessing framework, followed by a deterministic sequential parameter sweep to fully automate threshold selection. The proposed hybrid model demonstrated enhancement in detecting critical anatomical structures in a publicly available benchmark database from Kaggle. By intelligently amplifying localized gradients without overwhelming the image with background noise, our method achieved higher Recall (Sensitivity). Consequently, the overall F1-Score elevated, and the Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) improved, all while maintaining a highly efficient execution. This establishes our optimized pipeline as a highly practical and near real-time operational model for clinical diagnostics, offering a compelling alternative to computationally heavy deep learning approaches.

</details>


### [263] [Coronary Mask Guided Registration for Continuous Time 4D Cardiac CT Dataset Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28712)
*Yuang Wang,Shuo Wang,Changyu Chen,Dufan Wu,Pengfei Jin,Yunqiang An,Yang Gao,Bin Lu,Dongrui Dai,Muge Du,Yan Yan,Dong Li,Liang Li,Li Zhang,Zhiqiang Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Coronary Mask Guided Registration (CMGR)的方法，用于从临床多相位心脏CT重建中构建运动保留、伪影减少且时间连续的4D心脏CT数据集，可作为伪地面真值用于心脏CT成像研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床心脏CT多相位重建通常在收缩末期或舒张末期具有可接受的图像质量，但在其他相位特别是右冠状动脉区域会出现运动伪影，这限制了4D心脏CT成像研究中可靠地面真值的获取。现有数据集缺乏连续时间内各相位的高质量重建，难以支撑系统设计仿真和重建算法开发。

Method: CMGR方法包含三个关键设计：(1) 伪影减少——以ED或ES相位作为参考相位，通过将参考体素用形变场变换生成序列；(2) 运动保持——将参考相位配准到多相位重建中的每个非参考相位，并在配准中正则化RCA掩膜，将其融入图像域配准以同时捕捉RCA和其他心脏结构的运动；(3) 时间连续性——通过插值非参考相位的形变场，实现对任意时间的连续形变场生成。

Result: CMGR在捕捉RCA运动和提供合理RCA形态方面优于代表性图像域配准方法，在全心脏运动捕捉方面表现出竞争力；有效减少了临床多相位重建中的运动伪影，且中间帧在离散心相位之间提供了合理的过渡。

Conclusion: CMGR为构建连续时间4D心脏CT数据集提供了一种有效方法，该数据集可用于系统设计仿真和重建算法开发，有助于推动心脏CT成像的进步。

Abstract: Objective: Clinical cardiac CT multiphase reconstructions generally provide acceptable image quality in end-diastole (ED) or end-systole (ES) phases, but in other phases may exhibit motion artifacts, especially in the right coronary artery (RCA). This limits ground-truth availability in 4D cardiac CT imaging research. We aim to construct a 4D cardiac CT dataset that is generally suitable to serve as pseudo ground truth. Methods: We propose Coronary Mask Guided Registration (CMGR) to produce a motion-preserved, artifact-reduced, and continuous-time 4D cardiac CT sequence from the clinical multiphase reconstruction of each patient. For artifact reduction, CMGR uses the ED or ES phase as the reference phase and warps the reference volume with deformation fields to produce the sequence. For motion preservation, CMGR registers the reference phase to each non-reference phase of the multiphase reconstruction. To capture the motion of both the RCA and other cardiac structures in each registration, CMGR regularizes RCA masks and incorporates them into image-domain registration. Time-continuity is achieved by interpolating the deformation fields for non-reference phases to arbitrary times. Results: CMGR outperformed representative image-domain registration methods in capturing RCA motion and providing reasonable RCA shape, and showed competitive performance in capturing whole-heart motion. Additionally, CMGR reduced motion artifacts from clinical multiphase reconstructions, and intermediate CMGR frames generally provided plausible transitions between discrete cardiac phases. Conclusion: CMGR provides an effective approach for constructing continuous-time 4D cardiac CT datasets. Significance: The dataset can be used in system design simulations and in reconstruction algorithm development, thereby facilitating advances in cardiac CT imaging.

</details>


### [264] [Evaluating the Safety of Deep Learning-Based Brain MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28714)
*Dat Tat Mai,Thai Viet Pham,Thu Nguyen Thi Dang,James Jin Kang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 这篇系统综述（纳入263项研究，1995-2026年）发现当前深度学习脑MRI加速重建模型的评估实践存在严重盲区：仅有6.8%的研究同时记录图像保真度指标和读者评估，读者评估普遍较弱且随语料库增长而减少，生成模型（回归率最高的类别）恰恰是读者评估最少的类别之一。结论是现行评估实践无法验证诊断安全性，并据此提出安全导向评估必须满足的五项要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习可将脑MRI加速4-10倍，但模型可能擦除真实病灶或合成虚假组织，而这种失败是PSNR、SSIM等像素平均指标无法捕捉的。作者旨在系统审查现有评估实践能否发现这一盲区，以验证诊断安全性。

Method: 遵循PRISMA 2020指南，检索七个数据库（无时间限制），纳入263项研究（1995-2026），使用QUADAS-2及配套工具进行质量评估，采用叙述性综合法。类别源自标题、摘要和受控词表；重复筛选一致性高（Fleiss kappa=0.877），评估一致性亦较高（0.788；可观察处为0.390），但数据提取未经审计。

Result: 仅18/263项研究（6.8%）在同一数据上同时记录保真度指标和读者评估；读者研究多为评估读者间一致性，结果较弱（fastMRI 2020的Kendall W为0.457和0.386，仅在SSIM发散处有所改善）；在既定误差模型下擦除100mm3腔隙性梗死仅使全局PSNR改变0.03 dB；语料增长5倍后读者评估从32%降至18%（后回升至21%）；生成模型（回归关联最高39%）是读者评估最少的类别之一（11.3%），自监督模型达47%但零读者评估；仅5%的研究同时发布代码并开展读者研究；没有任何研究评估模型观察者；无命名的数据集覆盖急性卒中或出血。

Conclusion: 基于这些底线数据，当前评估实践无法保证诊断安全性。作者由此推导出安全导向评估必须满足的五项要求（虽然摘要未详细列出，但明确指出了这一框架性结论）。

Abstract: Objective: Deep learning accelerates brain MRI four- to tenfold, but models can erase lesions or synthesize false tissue - failures pixel-averaged metrics like PSNR and SSIM miss. We review whether current evaluation practices detect this blind spot. Methods: Following PRISMA 2020, we searched seven databases without date limits, including 263 studies (1995-2026), appraised them using QUADAS-2 and matched instruments, and synthesized narratively. Categories were derived from titles, abstracts, and controlled vocabulary; reported prevalence figures represent floors. Duplicate screening achieved high agreement (Fleiss kappa = 0.877), as did appraisal (0.788; 0.390 where observable). Extraction is unaudited. Results: Only 18 of 263 studies (6.8%) recorded both a fidelity metric and reader assessment on identical data, leaving the central surrogate unmeasured. Reader studies mostly measured inter-reader agreement, which was weak: fastMRI 2020 concordance reached 0.457 and 0.386 (Kendall W), improving only where SSIM diverged. Erasing a 100 mm3 lacunar infarct shifts global PSNR by 0.03 dB under the stated error model. As the corpus grew fivefold, reader assessments dropped from 32% to 18%, recovering to 21%. Generative models - most associated with hallucination (39%) - were among the least reader-evaluated (11.3%), while self-supervised models reached 47% with zero reader evaluation. Only 5% released code and ran reader studies; none evaluated a model observer; no named dataset covered acute stroke or hemorrhage. Conclusions: On these floors, current evaluation practices cannot certify diagnostic safety. We derive five requirements safety-oriented evaluations must meet.

</details>


### [265] [SCoPE-Reg: Efficient Rigid Ultrasound Slice-to-Volume Registration via State-Space Correlation and Closed-Form Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28715)
*Niklas Schwarz,Jens Kleesiek,Moritz Rempe*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: SCoPE-Reg是一个用于超声引导干预的刚性slice-to-volume配准方法，结合状态空间slice-volume交互、密集3D坐标预测和无参数加权Kabsch估计，在CAMUS和μ-RegPro数据集上达到SOTA精度（平均目标配准误差0.73mm和2.27mm），并将峰值误差较SOTA降低56%，同时仅需6.49M参数且分辨率无关，可实时运行于原生采集分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导干预需要在3D解剖参考中定位未追踪的2D帧（六自由度位姿估计），但现有刚性SVR方法面临挑战：稠密交叉注意力的计算成本随切片和体素token数量的乘积增长，而直接位姿回归缺少显式对应约束，导致精度、可靠性或帧率之间难以兼顾。

Method: SCoPE-Reg由三部分组成：(1) 基于状态空间的slice-volume交互机制，避免稠密交叉注意力带来的高计算成本；(2) 密集3D坐标预测，提供显式的对应关系约束；(3) 无参数的加权Kabsch算法进行位姿估计，将预测的坐标对应映射到刚体变换。

Result: 在CAMUS和μ-RegPro上，SCoPE-Reg的平均目标配准误差分别为0.73mm和2.27mm（对比SOTA的1.24mm和2.63mm）；CAMUS峰值误差较SOTA降低56%（12.5→5.5mm）；3mm内配准成功率分别达100%和80%；在128^2分辨率下对位姿扰动保持最低误差；参数量6.49M且与分辨率无关，512^2分辨率下可达51FPS。

Conclusion: SCoPE-Reg通过耦合基于对应的精度、有界的最坏误差和分辨率无关的计算成本，在刚性超声SVR任务中建立了新的SOTA，使其能够在干预过程中以原生采集分辨率实时运行，克服了先前方法在精度、可靠性和帧率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Ultrasound-guided interventions can require localization of an untracked 2D frame within a 3D anatomical reference. Rigid slice-to-volume registration (SVR) estimates this six-degree-of-freedom pose but remains challenging because of limited anatomical context, acoustic artifacts, and view-dependent appearance. Existing methods often use dense cross-attention, whose cost scales with the product of slice and volume token counts, or direct pose regression without explicit correspondence constraints. We introduce SCoPE-Reg, combining state-space slice--volume interaction, dense 3D coordinate prediction, and parameter-free weighted Kabsch estimation. On SVR tasks from CAMUS and $μ$-RegPro, SCoPE-Reg yields mean target registration errors of $0.73$ mm and $2.27$ mm against $1.24$ mm and $2.63$ mm for the state of the art (SOTA), reduces peak error on CAMUS by 56% below SOTA ($12.5\!\to\!5.5$ mm), and registers $100\%$ and $80\%$ of frames within $3$ mm. On CAMUS at $128^2$ it retains the lowest error at increasing pose-perturbation magnitude. It holds $6.49$ M parameters independent of resolution, sustaining $51$ FPS at $512^2$. SCoPE-Reg establishes a SOTA in rigid ultrasound SVR: by coupling correspondence-based accuracy with bounded worst-case error and resolution-independent cost, it becomes viable at native acquisition resolution during intervention, where prior methods trade accuracy, reliability, or frame rate against one another. Supplementary code provided and will be open-sourced upon acceptance.

</details>


### [266] [Evaluating the Effects of Inter-Observer and Model Variability on Radiological Peritoneal Cancer Index Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28716)
*Savvas Saragiotis,Pieter C. Gort,Lotte J. S. Fleurkens-Ewals,Anna F. van Herwijnen,Marion Tops-Welten,L. D. Kampmeijer,Joost Nederend,Fons van der Sommen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该研究通过rPCI区域分割任务，评估深度分割模型在几何指标（Dice、HD95、ASD）上的改进是否能转化为临床决策层面的有意义变化，并采用概率性腹膜转移模拟来分析分割变异对PCI评分和PCI 20阈值分类的影响。结果表明rPCI评分对典型分割变异具有鲁棒性，但在评分接近20的边界情形下，专家复核仍然必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习分割模型通常用Dice、HD95、ASD等几何指标评估，但这些指标改进在多大程度上转化为下游临床决策的有意义变化尚不清楚。作者希望弥合"指标到决策"之间的差距，评估几何性能提升是否真正影响临床诊断结果。

Method: 1. 使用放射学PCI（rPCI）区域分割任务，基于共识定义建立解剖学锚定的3D区域；2. 四名专家对十次腹部CT扫描进行标注以量化观察者间变异性；3. 将已发表的基于nnU-Net的rPCI分割模型与此人类参考基准在全部13个区域上比较（Dice、HD95、ASD）；4. 实施概率性腹膜转移模拟，在多数投票rPCI图上传播区域边界变异性，评估对派生(r)PCI评分及PCI 20阈值分类的影响。

Result: 观察者间一致性高（平均Dice 0.87）；模型在大多数区域达到人类水平，但在区域4、8和小肠区域（9-12）偏差更大。模拟中评分差异通常很小（平均ΔrPCI约0.3-0.6），决策翻转主要发生在参考评分接近20时。

Conclusion: rPCI衍生评分对典型分割变异总体具有鲁棒性，几何指标改进不一定带来明显的临床决策改变；但边界病例（评分接近20）是专家复核不可替代的关键场景。

Abstract: Deep learning segmentation models are often evaluated using geometric metrics such as Dice, HD95, and ASD, yet it remains unclear to what extent improvements in these metrics translate into clinically meaningful changes in downstream decision-making. The metric-to-decision gap is examined using radiological Peritoneal Cancer Index (rPCI) region segmentation on contrast-enhanced CT, where a consensus definition provides anatomically grounded 3D regions and the clinically used PCI 20 threshold enables decision-level evaluation. Inter-observer variability is quantified across four experts on ten abdominal CT scans, and a published nnU-Net based rPCI segmentation model is benchmarked against this human reference using Dice, HD95, and ASD across all 13 regions. To relate geometric differences to clinical impact, a probabilistic peritoneal metastasis simulation is implemented on majority-vote rPCI maps, propagating region-boundary variability into variability of derived (r)PCI scores and classification at the PCI 20 cutoff. Observers showed high agreement (mean Dice $0.87$), while the model matched human performance in most regions but deviated more in regions 4, 8, and the small-bowel regions (9-12). Across simulations, score differences were typically small (mean $Δ$rPCI $\approx 0.3$-$0.6$) for both observers and the model, and decision flips occurred predominantly when the reference score was near 20. These results suggest that rPCI-derived scoring is generally robust to typical segmentation variability, while highlighting borderline cases as the main setting where expert review remains essential.

</details>


### [267] [Efficient JPEG Restoration in the Wavelet Domain via Mean Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28730)
*Stefan-Alexandru Asandei,Mihai-Alexandru Radu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个65M参数的生成式JPEG恢复模型，通过精确可逆的Haar小波变换替代VAE编解码器、使用秩增强线性注意力DiT预测小波域残差，并结合改进的MeanFlow目标实现1-2次网络评估即可推理。在LIVE-1、Urban100和DIV2K-val上达到最低LPIPS，吞吐量比一步SODiff快约4.9倍，参数仅为其二十分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JPEG恢复系统虽能实现高质量恢复，但模型规模大、推理慢、成本高，难以满足设备端高效部署的需求。作者旨在构建一个兼顾质量与吞吐量的小型生成式恢复模型。

Method: 1) 用精确可逆的两级Haar变换替代学习的VAE编码器-解码器；2) 通过秩增强线性注意力DiT预测干净的小波域残差，内部估计压缩严重程度；3) 采用改进的MeanFlow目标进行优化，使模型无需蒸馏即可在一次或两次网络评估中完成推理。

Result: 在QF 10和20条件下，于LIVE-1、Urban100和DIV2K-val三个基准上取得最低LPIPS；在单个RTX 3090上以1024×1024分辨率达到8.05 images/s的吞吐量，约为一步SODiff报告吞吐量的4.9倍，参数数量仅为后者的二十分之一。在极端压缩（QF 5）下，大型预训练先验模型仍然更强。

Conclusion: 该模型在部署受限场景下优先考虑吞吐量，能够以极小的参数量和极高的推理速度实现领先的感知质量，但面对极端压缩时，大型预训练先验模型仍有优势。

Abstract: Latest JPEG restoration systems achieve strong quality with large models, yet often remain too slow and expensive for efficient on-device deployment. We present a 65M-parameter generative restorer that attains the lowest LPIPS at QF 10 and 20 on LIVE-1, Urban100, and DIV2K-val while sustaining 8.05 images/s at $1024\times1024$ on a single RTX 3090, roughly $4.9\times$ the reported throughput of one-step SODiff at one-twentieth of its parameters. Trained from scratch, the model replaces the learned VAE encoder-decoder with an exactly invertible two-level Haar transform, predicts a clean wavelet-domain residual through a rank-enhanced linear-attention DiT that estimates compression severity internally, and is optimized with an improved MeanFlow objective that enables inference in one or two network evaluations without distillation. Large pretrained priors remain stronger under severe compression (QF 5), whereas our model prioritizes throughput for deployment-constrained restoration.

</details>


### [268] [Medical Foundation Model Features as Perceptual Loss for Brain MRI Contrast Dose Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28773)
*Changsheng Fang,Dayang Wang,T. Campbell Arnold,Enhao Gong,Srivathsa Pasumarthi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在脑MRI对比剂剂量模拟中使用医学基础模型特征替代自然图像特征（如VGG16/ResNet50）作为感知损失的可行性。通过两阶段实验：先比较RadImageNet、SegVol、BrainIAC与ImageNet预训练特征在四个医学影像基准上的表现，选出RadImageNet作为最优特征提取器φ*；再将其替换进现有的脑MRI剂量模拟框架中，虽然标准指标仅有微小提升（PSNR从41.63到41.74），但视觉结果显示RadImageNet能更准确减少残留增强、更忠实跟随剂量降低轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学图像合成中的感知损失大多仍使用自然图像预训练的特征骨干（如VGG16、ResNet50），与目标域（如MRI）存在领域不匹配问题，可能削弱对解剖结构、对比增强和采集变异性的监督信号。作者测试医学基础模型特征是否能提供更合适的感知损失。

Method: 两阶段实验设计。阶段一：将RadImageNet、SegVol、BrainIAC与ImageNet预训练的VGG16和ResNet50作为冻结特征提取器，在四个公开医学影像基准（甲状腺超声、乳腺超声、前交叉韧带膝MRI、半月板膝MRI）上比较，RadImageNet获得最低平均排名被选为φ*。阶段二：在现有迭代脑MRI剂量模拟框架中仅替换VGG16为φ*，保持生成器、重建损失、对抗损失、辅助损失、优化调度和损失权重均不变。

Result: 标准指标变化温和：PSNR从41.63提升到41.74，SSIM从0.9739到0.9754，RMSE从0.1384降至0.1369，残留摄取CNR从0.0085降至0.0082。视觉结果显示RadImageNet能减少标记结构中的残留增强、更忠实跟随剂量降低轨迹，并与采集的10%低剂量目标更接近。

Conclusion: 领域对齐的放射学特征可作为MRI剂量模拟中实用的感知特征空间，但还需在临床等效性和更大规模队列上进行验证。

Abstract: Perceptual losses are widely used in medical image synthesis because they encourage agreement in high-level structure beyond voxel-wise intensity similarity. In practice, most perceptual losses are still computed with natural-image backbones such as VGG16 or ResNet50, even when the target domain is magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). This mismatch may weaken supervision for anatomy, contrast enhancement, and acquisition variability. We test whether medical foundation model features provide a more suitable perceptual loss for brain MRI contrast dose simulation. The study has two stages. First, we compare RadImageNet, SegVol, and BrainIAC with ImageNet-pretrained VGG16 and ResNet50 as frozen feature extractors on four public medical imaging benchmarks: thyroid ultrasound, breast ultrasound, anterior cruciate ligament knee MRI, and meniscus knee MRI. RadImageNet achieves the lowest mean rank across the Stage I representation suite and is selected as $φ^\star$. Second, we replace only the VGG16 feature extractor in an existing iterative brain MRI dose simulation framework with $φ^\star$. The generator, reconstruction loss, adversarial loss, auxiliary losses, optimization schedule, and loss weights are kept unchanged. Standard metrics change modestly, with PSNR increasing from 41.63 to 41.74, SSIM from 0.9739 to 0.9754, RMSE decreasing from 0.1384 to 0.1369, and residual-uptake CNR from 0.0085 to 0.0082. The visual results show the main effect: RadImageNet reduces residual enhancement in marked structures, follows a more faithful dose-reduction trajectory, and remains close to the acquired 10% low-dose target. These results support domain-aligned radiology features as a practical perceptual feature space for MRI dose simulation, while leaving clinical equivalence and larger-cohort validation as future work.

</details>


### [269] [Generative Translation Priors: Bayesian Imaging with Cross-Modality Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28872)
*Evan Bell,Jiaming Liu,Yifan Chen,Yu Sun*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式翻译先验（GTP），一种贝叶斯框架，将基于扩散的图像到图像翻译模型转化为跨模态图像先验，用于病态成像逆问题。GTP通过似然引导整合目标域测量，并基于理论分析揭示了似然引导引入的内在偏差，推导出无真值偏差估计公式，进而衍生出基于梯度和近端似然引导的两种离散化算法。在CT（以MRI为侧信息）和PET（以CT为侧信息）重建中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像算法中希望利用来自可共同获得模态的图像来指导目标域重建。现有扩散模型在跨模态翻译上有潜力，但如何将其作为先验融入逆问题求解尚缺乏系统性框架，尤其缺少对后验动态的理论分析与偏差刻画。

Method: 基于贝叶斯框架，将扩散式图像到图像翻译模型转化为跨模态先验；通过似然引导将目标域测量纳入，得到后验分布；对后验动态进行理论分析，揭示并刻画似然引导引入的固有偏差，提出无真值的偏差估计作为后验采样质量评估指标；据此设计基于梯度和近端似然引导的两种离散化GTP算法。

Result: 在CT重建（以MRI为侧信息）和PET重建（以CT为侧信息）实验中，GTP有效整合了互补的跨模态信息，在严重欠采样测量条件下仍实现了高保真重建。

Conclusion: GTP为跨模态扩散先验求解成像逆问题提供了理论与实践框架，兼具理论严谨性与实际有效性，在医学成像的跨模态重建任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: The ability to leverage images from co-available modalities to inform target-domain reconstruction is highly desirable in imaging algorithms.
