In this work, we introduce Generative Translation Priors (GTP)--a Bayesian framework that transforms diffusion-based image-to-image translation models into cross-modality image priors for ill-posed imaging inverse problems. GTP incorporates target-domain measurements through likelihood guidance, steering the translation process toward the desired posterior distribution. The framework is grounded in a theoretical analysis of the resulting posterior dynamics, which reveals an intrinsic bias introduced by likelihood guidance. We further characterize this bias and derive a ground-truth-free formulation for its estimation, enabling it to serve as a practical metric for assessing posterior sampling quality. Building on this analysis, we derive two discretized GTP algorithms based on gradient and proximal likelihood guidance, respectively. We validate GTP on computed tomography reconstruction with magnetic resonance side information, and on positron emission tomography reconstruction with computed tomography side information. Experiments demonstrate that GTP effectively incorporates complementary cross-modality information and achieves high-fidelity reconstruction even under severely undersampled measurements.

</details>


### [270] [Patch-Based Diffusion Reconstruction for Accelerated Cardiac Cine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28927)
*Xuan Lei,Philip Schniter,Juliet Varghese,Rizwan Ahmad*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于扩散模型的图像重建框架CineDiff，用于高度加速的2D实时电影心血管磁共振成像（CMR），在回顾性和前瞻性欠采样数据上均优于传统压缩感知和变分网络方法，并展现出对分布外数据（中场0.55T和猪模型）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度加速的实时电影CMR在临床上具有重要意义，但传统重建方法（如压缩感知）会产生块状伪影并丢失精细解剖结构。需要开发一种能实现高质量、高加速率重建的新方法，以改善实时CMR的图像质量。

Method: 训练了一个无条件的patch-based扩散模型，并将其嵌入到名为CineDiff的重建框架中，利用扩散后验采样（diffusion posterior sampling）实现数据一致性。在四个场景下进行评估：(i) 30例回顾性欠采样屏气电影数据（1.5T和3T，多加速率）；(ii) 15例前瞻性欠采样自由呼吸实时电影数据（1.5T和3T）；(iii) 10例前瞻性欠采样中场（0.55T）自由呼吸扫描（5例健康受试者和5例猪模型）。回顾性欠采样用PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS和DISTS评估；前瞻性欠采样用盲法专家5点Likert量表评分。

Result: 在回顾性欠采样屏气电影数据中，CineDiff在所有加速率下均获得更高的PSNR和SSIM以及更低的LPIPS和DISTS。在前瞻性欠采样自由呼吸实时电影数据中，CineDiff获得更高的专家图像质量评分。与压缩感知和变分网络方法（CineVN）相比，CineDiff减少了块状伪影并保留了更精细的解剖细节。

Conclusion: CineDiff实现了高度加速2D实时电影CMR的高质量重建，并且对分布外数据（包括中场和猪模型采集）表现出良好的鲁棒性，证明了该方法的泛化能力和临床潜力。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a diffusion-based reconstruction framework for highly accelerated 2D real-time (RT) cine cardiovascular magnetic resonance imaging (CMR).
  Methods: We trained an unconditional patch-based diffusion model and incorporated it into a reconstruction framework, termed CineDiff, using diffusion posterior sampling for data consistency. CineDiff was evaluated in four settings: (i) 30 retrospectively undersampled breath-held cine at 1.5T and 3T from healthy participants across multiple acceleration rates, (ii) 15 prospectively undersampled free-breathing RT cine at 1.5T and 3T from patients indicated for clinical CMR, and (iii) 10 prospectively undersampled mid-field (0.55T) free-breathing scans, including five from healthy subjects and five from porcine models. For retrospective undersampling, reconstruction quality was assessed using peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity index measure (SSIM), learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS), and deep image structure and texture similarity (DISTS). For prospective undersampling, image quality was evaluated by blinded expert scoring on a 5-point Likert scale.
  Results: In retrospectively undersampled breath-held cine data, CineDiff achieved higher PSNR and SSIM and lower LPIPS and DISTS than the comparison methods across all evaluated acceleration rates. In prospectively undersampled free-breathing RT cine data, CineDiff received higher expert image-quality scores. Qualitatively, CineDiff reduced block-like artifacts and preserved finer anatomical detail compared with traditional compressed sensing and a variational network method, termed CineVN.
  Conclusion: CineDiff enabled high-quality reconstruction of highly accelerated 2D RT cine CMR. The method also demonstrated robustness to out-of-distribution data, including mid-field and porcine acquisitions.

</details>


### [271] [Multimodal Deep Learning for Uncertainty-Aware Radiation Pneumonitis Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28937)
*Jin Yang,Tian Liu,Jing Wang,Robert Samstein,Kenneth Rosenzweig,Julie Bloom,Ming Chao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态贝叶斯扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）框架，用于同时估计放射性肺炎（RP）风险和量化预测不确定性的来源。该框架整合规划CT图像和三维辐射剂量分布，通过自监督多模态预训练减少对有限且可能有噪声的毒性标签的依赖，并融合潜在扩散Transformer和贝叶斯预测框架，同时纳入可学习的标签噪声模型以显式处理注释不确定性，从而在个体患者层面提供准确的RP风险估计及多种不确定性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于剂量-体积直方图和正常组织并发症概率模型难以捕捉辐射诱导肺损伤中复杂的空间、解剖和患者特异性因素；现有机器学习方法虽能整合多模态信息改进RP风险预测，但大多仅提供点估计，无法量化个体预测的可靠性，限制了其临床效用。

Method: MM-DiT框架：首先通过自监督多模态预训练整合规划CT图像与三维辐射剂量分布；随后利用潜在扩散Transformer优化表征；再将其迁移至贝叶斯预测框架实现概率性RP风险评估；并引入可学习的标签噪声模型显式建模不完美毒性标注引起的不确定性，最终提供包含偶然不确定性、认知不确定性和标签不确定性的个体化风险估计。

Result: 在两个独立队列中，MM-DiT通过预测判别力、校准和不确定性的互补评估，展示了其在提供准确RP风险估计的同时量化临床相关预测不确定性来源方面的潜力。

Conclusion: MM-DiT框架能够在个体患者层面提供准确的RP风险估计和可靠的预测不确定性量化，有望提升机器学习方法在放射性肺炎风险评估中的临床可用性和可信度。

Abstract: Radiation pneumonitis (RP) is a common and clinically significant toxicity of thoracic radiation therapy that can cause pulmonary morbidity and impair quality of life. Although conventional dose-volume histogram-based metrics and normal tissue complication probability models are widely used for RP risk assessment, they inadequately capture the complex spatial, anatomical, and patient-specific factors underlying radiation-induced lung injury. Recent machine learning approaches have improved RP risk prediction by integrating multimodal clinical and imaging information; however, most provide a point risk estimate without quantifying the reliability of individual predictions, limiting their potential clinical utility. We propose a Multimodal Bayesian Diffusion Transformer (MM-DiT) framework that jointly estimates RP risk and characterizes the sources of predictive uncertainty. MM-DiT integrates planning computed tomography (CT) images and three-dimensional radiation dose distributions through self-supervised multimodal pre-training, reducing reliance on limited and potentially noisy toxicity labels. The resulting representations are further refined using a latent diffusion transformer and transferred to a Bayesian prediction framework for probabilistic RP risk estimation. A learnable label-noise model is incorporated to explicitly account for uncertainty arising from imperfect toxicity annotations. Therefore, it provides individualized RP risk estimates with complementary measures of aleatoric, epistemic, and label uncertainty, enabling assessment of prediction reliability at the individual-patient level. We evaluated MM-DiT in two independent cohorts using complementary assessments of predictive discrimination, calibration, and uncertainty. The results demonstrate its potential to provide accurate RP risk estimates while quantifying clinically relevant sources of predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [272] [Manifold-Constrained PET Reconstruction with Learned Flow-Matching Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29158)
*Hengjia Ran,Jie Luo,Yutao Zhu,Rui Hu,Huafeng Liu,Bo Zhao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于流匹配生成模型的无监督PET图像重建框架，将流匹配模型作为学习到的流形先验融入泊松似然优化中，通过ADMM算法交替进行期望最大化数据一致性更新和潜空间梯度投影，在模拟和真实数据集上实现了优于传统方法和最先进深度学习基线的噪声抑制、结构保持和定量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET图像重建是一个病态的泊松逆问题，传统重建方法往往遭受严重的噪声放大和伪影干扰，需要一种既能有效正则化又能保持高质量重建效果的解决方案。

Method: 采用流匹配生成模型在高品质PET图像上训练，学习从高斯潜分布到经验PET图像分布的确定性常微分方程传输，从而得到可微的生成器作为流形先验。将该生成器作为显式流形约束嵌入正则化泊松似然公式中，采用ADMM算法求解，其中期望最大化类型代理更新保证数据一致性，基于梯度的潜空间投影保证流形近邻性。

Result: 在模拟和真实PET数据集上评估了剂量水平鲁棒性、病灶插入泛化性和跨扫描仪迁移能力，与传统重建方法及最先进深度学习基线相比，该方法在噪声抑制、结构保持和定量精度方面表现更优，同时保持了高计算效率。

Conclusion: 基于流匹配生成模型的无监督流形先验方法为PET重建提供了一种高效且鲁棒的替代方案，兼具优越的重建质量和计算效率。

Abstract: Image reconstruction for positron emission tomography (PET) is an ill-posed Poisson inverse problem that often suffers from severe noise amplification and artifacts. In this work, we introduce an unsupervised, optimization-based reconstruction framework that employs a flow-matching generative model as a learned manifold prior. We train the flow-matching model on high-quality PET images to learn a deterministic ordinary differential equation transport from a Gaussian latent distribution to the empirical PET image distribution, yielding a differentiable generator of anatomically plausible images. We incorporate this generator as an explicit manifold constraint into a regularized Poisson likelihood formulation. We solve the resulting optimization problem using an alternating direction method of multipliers algorithm, in which an expectation-maximization-type surrogate update enforces data consistency and a gradient-based latent-space projection enforces manifold proximity. We evaluate the proposed method on both simulated and real PET datasets, assessing dose-level robustness, lesion-insertion generalization, and cross-scanner transfer. Compared with conventional reconstruction methods and state-of-the-art deep learning baselines, the proposed method provides superior noise suppression, structural preservation, and quantitative accuracy, while maintaining high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [273] [The MYOSAIQ Challenge: Myocardial Segmentation with Automated Infarct Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29246)
*Olivier Bernard,William A. Romero R.,Cyprien Bouton,Celia Goujat,Hang Jung Ling,Pierre-Marc Jodoin,Fumin Guo,Calder Sheagren,Graham Wright,Abdul Qayyum,Moona Mazher,Steven A. Niederer,Hairui Wang,Xiaomei Wu,Franz Thaler,Gernot Plank,Martin Urschler,Ricardo M. Rosales,Esther Pueyo,Nicolas Duchateau,Frederic Cervenansky,Patrick Clarysse,Loic Belle,Thomas Bochaton,Nathan Mewton,Magalie Viallon,Pierre Croisille*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MYOSAIQ挑战赛，该挑战赛基于来自两个多中心临床试验的439个CMR体积数据（涵盖急性期和慢性期心肌梗死），来自16个中心和3个不同MRI厂商，旨在建立心肌梗死量化泛化学习的基准。结果显示，精心设计的UNet模型优于完全自动化的基础模型，但对梗死区域的精确分割仍有改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习心肌梗死分割方法多依赖小型数据集，并经过预处理标准化处理，且仅针对再灌注治疗后的特定梗死阶段，导致模型泛化能力差，难以应用于常规临床实践。作者希望通过MYOSAIQ挑战赛建立通用化学习的基准，推动心肌梗死量化研究。

Method: 论文报告了MYOSAIQ挑战赛的设置与结果。数据集整合了两个多中心临床试验的439个CMR体积，涵盖急性和慢性心肌梗死阶段，来自16个中心和3种MRI厂商的扫描仪。六个团队参与竞赛，采用了多种基线模型、数据增强技术和置信度策略。此外，作者将参赛者的结果与微调后的基础模型进行了对比，以评估不同方法的性能。

Result: 研究结果表明，精心设计的UNet-based方法在LGE MR分割上优于完全自动化的基础模型。最佳方法在各种条件下能对左心室和心肌实现高质量、稳定的分割，但对梗死区域的精确分割仍有待改进。

Conclusion: 当前最优方法在心肌和左心室分割上已达到较高质量，但梗死区域的分割精度仍是瓶颈。该挑战赛为心肌梗死量化领域提供了宝贵的泛化性基准数据集和比较框架，有助于推动后续研究向更健壮、更适用于临床的模型发展。

Abstract: Late gadolinium enhancement (LGE) cardiac magnetic resonance (MR) imaging is the modality of choice to assess myocardial infarction (MI) lesions. Nowadays MI volume quantification is not performed routinely in clinical practice. Numerous deep learning (DL) methods have been developed to automate the segmentation of the myocardium and infarct regions. However, most studies rely on relatively small datasets which typically undergo pre-processing steps to standardize images and focus on a specific phase of myocardial infarction following reperfusion therapy. These limitations have impeded the development of models that are generalizable across diverse conditions and thus suitable for routine clinical use. To advance research and establish benchmarks in generalizable learning for myocardial infarct quantification, this paper presents findings from the Myocardial Segmentation with Automated Infarct Quantification (MYOSAIQ) challenge. The dataset set up for the challenge combines 439 CMR volumes from two multicenter clinical trials, with representative data acquired in acute and chronic phases after acute MI. Data were acquired in 16 centers using MRI scanners from three different vendors. Six teams participated until the end of the challenge, employing various baseline models, data augmentation techniques, and confidence strategies. To enhance the significance of this study, we compare the challengers' results with those of fine-tuned foundation models. Our results indicate that well-designed UNet-based techniques outperform fully automatic foundation models for LGE MR segmentation. While the best methods achieve high-quality and stable delineations of the left ventricle and myocardium under various conditions, they remain improvable in accurately segmenting infarct regions.

</details>


### [274] [ReLViC: Loss-Resilient Learned Video Coding with Dispersed Packetization and Controllable Packet Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30202)
*Xuyang Chen,Daquan Feng,Xianfu Chen,Xiang-Gen Xia*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ReLViC是一个面向丢包鲁棒性的学习型视频编码框架，通过将邻近潜在令牌分散到不同数据包、采用双功能Transformer同时进行熵估计和潜在令牌重建，并利用周期性重置的数据包上下文拓扑来控制错误传播范围，从而在严重丢包条件下优于H.265+RS-FEC和GRACE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据包丢失会严重损害学习型视频编码的性能，因为丢失潜在令牌既破坏空间重建又破坏时间预测，而现有方法缺乏系统性的丢包恢复机制。

Method: ReLViC包含三大核心技术：(1) 将空间相邻潜在令牌分散到不同数据包以降低突发丢包影响；(2) 设计双功能Transformer，编码端估计熵模型参数、接收端重建丢失令牌；(3) 通过周期重置的数据包上下文拓扑（由段长参数化）调控包依赖关系，无需重训练即可调节压缩效率与错误传播范围的权衡。训练采用三阶段渐进式流程：单帧编码、时间上下文熵建模、模拟丢包下的掩码令牌恢复。

Result: 在突发丢包轨迹下的实验中，ReLViC在严重丢包条件下实现了比特率比H.265+Reed-Solomon FEC和GRACE（丢包鲁棒学习型视频编码器）更稳定的重建质量，整体性能优于两个基线。

Conclusion: ReLViC证明了通过联合设计潜在编码与丢包恢复机制，学习型视频编码可以在严重丢包场景下超越传统编码器加前向纠错保护的方案，为鲁棒视频传输提供了新思路。

Abstract: Packet loss can severely impair learned video coding because missing latent tokens compromise both spatial reconstruction and temporal prediction. We present ReLViC, a loss-resilient learned video coding framework that jointly addresses latent coding and packet-loss recovery. ReLViC disperses spatially adjacent latent tokens across packets and employs a dual-purpose Transformer to estimate entropy-model parameters during coding and reconstruct missing latent tokens at the receiver. It controls packet dependencies through a periodic-reset packet-context topology parameterized by the segment length, thereby tuning the trade-off between compression efficiency and error-propagation range without retraining. A three-stage progressive training procedure establishes single-frame coding, learns temporal context for entropy modeling, and then optimizes the recovery of masked latent tokens under simulated packet loss. Experiments using burst-loss traces evaluate ReLViC against H.265 protected by Reed--Solomon forward error correction (FEC) and GRACE, a loss-resilient learned video codec. ReLViC delivers more stable reconstruction and outperforms both baselines under severe packet loss.

</details>


### [275] [OmniRAS: Standardizing Foundation Model Training and Evaluation in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31048)
*Leonardo Borgioli,Neil Getty,Wenli Xiu,Jessica Cassiani,Alvaro Ducas,Carlos Agustin Orda,Hira Waris,Fangfang Xia,Rick Stevens,Pier Cristoforo Giulianotti,Milos Zefran*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文介绍OmniRAS，一个面向机器人辅助手术的V-JEPA-2.1编码器家族（1B和2B参数），通过大规模持续预训练（19个数据源、约2650小时手术视频、51%为机器人手术）及两个新发布的数据集（OmniRAS-PR和多标签YT-Chole工具-动词-目标三元组任务），在六个任务上通过254次下游实验验证，实现了最强的下游适配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助手术领域缺乏基础模型，主要原因在于大型机器人手术视频语料难以构建，且现有模型大多仅在腹腔镜基准上评估，缺乏针对机器人手术场景的专门模型和评估体系。

Method: 1) 发布两个密集标注的机器人胆囊切除术数据集：OmniRAS-PR和YT-Chole多标签工具-动词-目标三元组任务，并提供数据集划分、探测协议和标注者间一致性研究；2) 在多达256个计算节点、全局批量大小6144的条件下，对19个数据源（约2650小时手术视频，51%为机器人手术）进行持续预训练；3) 在六个任务（三元组、阶段、步骤识别、动作分割和检测）上，采用冻结编码器和最后四层微调两种范式，与原始V-JEPA-2.1及专门手术模型进行对比评估。

Result: 跨三个随机种子共完成254次下游实验（含109次部分骨干网络微调）。最佳OmniRAS模型在所有任务族上取得最强的适配结果，而冻结编码器设置下的性能差异相对较小。

Conclusion: OmniRAS验证了通过大规模机器人手术视频持续预训练可以有效提升机器人辅助手术领域的下游任务性能，尤其在骨干网络微调场景下优势显著，为手术基础模型的构建和评估提供了新的数据资源与基准。

Abstract: Few foundation models exist for robot-assisted surgery, partly because large robotic-surgery video corpora are difficult to assemble and existing models are evaluated mostly on laparoscopic benchmarks. Further, most existing models are evaluated on a small set of public benchmarks, mostly focused on laparoscopic surgery. We present OmniRAS, a family of 1B- and 2B-parameter V-JEPA-2.1 encoders for robot-assisted surgery, and detail their training. First, we release two densely annotated robotic-cholecystectomy datasets: OmniRAS-PR and a multi-label YT-Chole tool-verb-target task, the first triplet-style annotation for robotic cholecystectomy, together with splits, probe protocols, and an inter-rater study validating the shared phase ontology. Second, we document continued pretraining at up to 256 compute nodes with global batch 6,144 over 19 sources totaling approximately 2,650 hours of surgical video, 51% robotic, and analyze compute and data composition. Third, we evaluate against raw V-JEPA-2.1 and specialized surgical models on six tasks spanning triplet, phase, and step recognition, action segmentation, and detection, under frozen-encoder and final-four-block fine-tuning regimes. Across three seeds, this yields 254 downstream runs, including 109 with partial backbone fine-tuning. The best OmniRAS models achieve the strongest adapted results across all task families, while frozen differences are smaller.

</details>
